In the stock list, allow me to click on any item and open the same screen we see when we scan a product barcode, and we are able to set its quantity and expiry date. Actually we are allowing people to edit the quanitity and expiry date of an item. When the screen opens, by default it will show the quanitty of that item already present, and that will be in the counter as well, if people decrease the number, that means that many items have been dispensed and if people increase the number, that means that many items are added. what do you think of this, if you have better idea, suggest me, before implementing anything.

> DEVELOPER

❯ In the stock list, allow me to click on any item and open the same screen we see when we scan a product
  barcode, and we are able to set its quantity and expiry date. Actually we are allowing people to edit the
  quanitity and expiry date of an item. When the screen opens, by default it will show the quanitty of that
  item already present, and that will be in the counter as well, if people decrease the number, that means that
  many items have been dispensed and if people increase the number, that means that many items are added. what
  do you think of this, if you have better idea, suggest me, before implementing anything.

> AGENT

The flow makes sense for the common case, but the single-counter-delta has one ambiguity worth resolving up front: if someone edits 10 → 8 because of a counting correction (not a dispense), it'll get logged as "2 dispensed" and pollute your dispense analytics. I'd suggest the screen open the same way, but ask the user to pick an intent — **Add**, **Dispense**, or **Correct** — before the counter does anything; default to whichever is most common (probably Dispense) so it's still one tap for the normal case.

The other thing to think about is **expiry**: when stock is *added*, it's usually a new batch with a new expiry, not an edit of the existing one — so on "Add" the expiry field should accept a new date and you'll eventually want batch tracking (FEFO dispensing). On "Dispense" and "Correct", expiry should stay read-only. Want me to go with the intent-selector version, or keep it simpler with just add/dispense and skip "correct" for now?

> DEVELOPER

since our users are not that tech savvy and that much literate, lets keep the seperation of concern, items will only be dispensed from the dispense section, it will only be added using the scan option which internally has option for barcode, manual barcode or no barcode, in no barcode, if a user selects an item which is already linked to a barcode, show them directly the screen which we show when a barcode is matched to a product, the screen to enter quantity and expiry. now coming to the stock list, any changes done in the stock list would be a correction. not dispense, not addition, a correction. analyze this plan, give me any suggestion or advice you have, then after my confirmation, imeplement it.

> AGENT

The separation is clean and matches user literacy well — three sections, three intents, no mode-switching inside a screen. A few things worth deciding before I code:

1. **Label and visual treatment.** Call it "Correct stock" (not "Edit") on the screen so it's clear this isn't normal add/dispense. Consider a subtle warning color or icon so users feel the friction — corrections should be the rare path, not the lazy shortcut for "I don't want to scan."

2. **Audit trail.** Corrections are the most abuse-prone action (someone covers shrinkage by "correcting" 50 → 30). Log every correction with: who, when, old qty, new qty, old expiry, new expiry, and ideally a **required reason** dropdown (Damaged / Expired-discarded / Miscount / Other+note). This is cheap to build now and impossible to backfill later.

3. **"No barcode → item already linked" case.** Good catch in your plan. Confirm the UX: when they pick an already-linked item, do we just silently route to the quantity/expiry screen, or show a tiny "this item has a barcode — next time you can scan" hint? I'd lean toward the silent route (less friction) but it's worth a one-line nudge eventually.

4. **Expiry on correction.** Should correcting let them change expiry? Yes — that's the whole point of "correction" — but if your model is one-expiry-per-item, a correction overwrites batch history. Fine for now; flag for later when batches come in.

5. **Negative/zero corrections.** Allow 0 (item fully consumed/lost). Disallow negative. Confirm whether "correct to 0" should also unlink/remove the item or just leave it at 0.

If you're good with (1) styling as a clear "Correction" action, (2) **required reason dropdown + audit log**, (3) silent route on no-barcode→linked, and (5) min=0, I'll implement. Want the reason dropdown in, or skip it for v1?

23	/** Subtle background tint per stock health */
24	function cardBg(row: StockRow): string {
25	  const s = statusFor(row);
26	  if (s === 'out') return 'rgba(229, 57, 53, 0.07)';     // very faint red
27	  if (s === 'low') return 'rgba(249, 168, 37, 0.07)';     // very faint amber
28	  if (row.on_hand >= row.threshold * 2) return 'rgba(67, 160, 71, 0.06)'; // faint green
29	  return 'rgba(129, 199, 132, 0.06)';                      // faint light green
30	}
31	
32	/** Count text color per stock health */
33	function countColor(row: StockRow): string {
34	  const s = statusFor(row);
35	  if (s === 'out') return '#E53935';
36	  if (s === 'low') return '#F9A825';
37	  if (row.on_hand >= row.threshold * 2) return '#43A047';
38	  return '#66BB6A';
39	}
40	
41	/** Format the pack-size hint that sits under the on-hand count.
42	 *  pack_size null/1 → just the unit ("g"); otherwise "× 100 g". */
43	function packSizeLabel(row: StockRow): string {
44	  const unit = row.unit ?? '';
45	  if (row.pack_size == null || row.pack_size === 1) return unit;
46	  // Trim trailing zero on whole numbers (100 not 100.0) but keep "1.5".
47	  const ps = Number.isInteger(row.pack_size) ? String(row.pack_size) : String(row.pack_size);
48	  return unit ? `× ${ps} ${unit}` : `× ${ps}`;
49	}
50	
51	const STATUS_OPTIONS: FilterOption[] = [
52	  { key: 'All', label: 'All Status' },
53	  { key: 'In Stock', label: 'In Stock' },
54	  { key: 'Low', label: 'Low' },
55	  { key: 'Out', label: 'Out of Stock' },
56	];

58	export function StockScreen({ navigation }: Props) {
59	  const t = useT();
60	  const { palette } = useTheme();
61	  const [rows, setRows] = useState<StockRow[]>([]);
62	  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
63	  const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');
64	  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('All');
65	  const [initialLoading, setInitialLoading] = useState(true);
66	  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
67	
68	  const categoryOptions = useMemo<FilterOption[]>(() => {
69	    const cats = new Set(rows.map((r) => r.category).filter(Boolean) as string[]);
70	    return [{ key: 'All', label: 'All Categories' }, ...Array.from(cats).sort().map((c) => ({ key: c, label: c }))];
71	  }, [rows]);
72	
73	  const fetchRemote = useCallback(async () => {
74	    await flushOnce().catch(() => {});
75	    try {
76	      const remote = await api.fetch.stock();
77	      await replaceStockFromRemote(
78	        remote.map((r) => ({
79	          barcode: r.barcode,
80	          name: r.name,
81	          category: r.category,
82	          unit: r.unit,
83	          pack_size: r.pack_size != null ? Number(r.pack_size) : null,
84	          on_hand: Number(r.on_hand),
85	          threshold: Number(r.threshold),
86	        })),
87	      );
88	      await setLastSyncTime('stock');
89	    } catch {
90	      // Offline fallback
91	    }
92	  }, []);
93	
94	  const load = useCallback(async (forceRefresh = false) => {
95	    const lastSync = await getLastSyncTime('stock');
96	    const stale = isCacheStale(lastSync);
97	
98	    if (forceRefresh || stale) {
99	      await fetchRemote();
100	    }
101	
102	    setRows(await listStock());
103	    setInitialLoading(false);
104	  }, [fetchRemote]);
105	
106	  useEffect(() => {
107	    load(false);
108	    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', () => load(false));
109	    return unsub;
110	  }, [navigation, load]);
111	
112	  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
113	    setRefreshing(true);
114	    try {
115	      await load(true);
116	    } finally {
117	      setRefreshing(false);
118	    }
119	  }, [load]);
120	
121	  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
122	    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
123	    return rows.filter((r) => {
124	      if (category !== 'All' && r.category !== category) return false;
125	      if (statusFilter !== 'All') {
126	        const s = statusFor(r);
127	        if (statusFilter === 'In Stock' && s !== 'ok') return false;
128	        if (statusFilter === 'Low' && s !== 'low') return false;
129	        if (statusFilter === 'Out' && s !== 'out') return false;
130	      }
131	      if (!q) return true;
132	      return r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.barcode.includes(q) || (r.category ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
133	    });
134	  }, [rows, query, category, statusFilter]);
135	
136	  return (
137	    <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
138	      <AppBar title={t('stock')} subtitle={t('stockSub')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />
139	      <KeyboardAvoidingView
140	        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
141	        style={{ flex: 1 }}
142	      >
143	        <View style={styles.search}>
144	        <TextInput
145	          value={query}
146	          onChangeText={setQuery}
147	          placeholder={t('search')}
148	          placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
149	          style={[
150	            styles.searchInput,
151	            {
152	              backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
153	              borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
154	              color: palette.onSurface,
155	            },
156	          ]}
157	        />
158	      </View>
159	
160	      {/* Dropdown filter row */}
161	      <View style={styles.filterRow}>
162	        <FilterDropdown
163	          label={t('filterCategory')}
164	          options={categoryOptions}
165	          selected={category}
166	          onSelect={setCategory}
167	        />
168	        <FilterDropdown
169	          label={t('filterStatus')}
170	          options={STATUS_OPTIONS}
171	          selected={statusFilter}
172	          onSelect={setStatusFilter}
173	        />
174	      </View>
175	
176	      {initialLoading && rows.length === 0 ? (
177	        <View style={styles.list}>
178	          {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
179	            <View key={i} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>
180	              <Card tone="filled" padding="lg">
181	                <View style={styles.row}>
182	                  <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: 6 }}>
183	                    <Skeleton width="60%" height={18} />
184	                    <Skeleton width="40%" height={14} />
185	                  </View>
186	                  <Skeleton width={40} height={24} />
187	                </View>
188	              </Card>
189	            </View>
190	          ))}
191	        </View>
192	      ) : (
193	      <FlatList
194	        data={filtered}
195	        keyExtractor={(r) => r.barcode}
196	        contentContainerStyle={styles.list}
197	        refreshControl={
198	          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} tintColor={palette.primary} />
199	        }
200	        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.sm }} />}
201	        renderItem={({ item }) => (
202	          <Card tone="filled" padding="lg" style={{ backgroundColor: cardBg(item) }}>
203	            <View style={styles.row}>
204	              <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
205	                <Text variant="titleMedium">{item.name}</Text>
206	                <Text
207	                  variant="bodyMedium"
208	                  color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
209	                  style={{ marginTop: 2 }}
210	                >
211	                  {item.category ?? '—'}
212	                </Text>
213	              </View>
214	              <View style={styles.countCol}>
215	                <Text variant="titleLarge" color={countColor(item)} style={{ fontWeight: '700' }}>
216	                  {item.on_hand}
217	                </Text>
218	                {packSizeLabel(item) ? (
219	                  <Text variant="labelMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
220	                    {packSizeLabel(item)}
221	                  </Text>
222	                ) : null}
223	              </View>
224	            </View>
225	          </Card>
226	        )}
227	        ListEmptyComponent={
228	          <View style={styles.empty}>
229	            <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
230	              Nothing matches
231	            </Text>
232	            <Text
233	              variant="bodyLarge"
234	              color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
235	              style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.sm }}
236	            >

238	            </Text>

242	      )}

60	  const { palette } = useTheme();
61	  const kbHeight = useKeyboardHeight();
62	  const { barcode, productId, productName: paramProductName, unit: paramUnit, packSize: paramPackSize } = route.params;
63	  const orderSession = useOrderSession();
64	  const [product, setProduct] = useState<Product | null>(null);
65	  const [item, setItem] = useState<OrderItem | null>(null);
66	  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);
67	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
68	
69	  // Expiry date state — driven by native date picker
70	  const [expiryDateObj, setExpiryDateObj] = useState<Date | null>(null);
71	  const [noExpiry, setNoExpiry] = useState(false);
72	  const [showPicker, setShowPicker] = useState(false);
73	  // Per-pack expiry. Default OFF — most bulk scans share one batch /
74	  // one expiry. When ON, expand a list of N rows so the guard can set
75	  // a different expiry on each pack of the multi-quantity scan.
76	  const [perPackExpiry, setPerPackExpiry] = useState(false);
77	  const [perPackExpiries, setPerPackExpiries] = useState<(Date | null)[]>([]);
78	  const [editingPackIdx, setEditingPackIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);
79	
80	  // Keep the per-pack list in lockstep with qty when the toggle is on.
81	  // Initialise new rows with the bulk expiry so the guard can edit
82	  // only the ones that actually differ.
83	  useEffect(() => {
84	    if (!perPackExpiry) return;
85	    setPerPackExpiries((prev) => {
86	      const n = Math.max(1, Math.floor(qty));
87	      if (prev.length === n) return prev;
88	      const next = [...prev];
89	      while (next.length < n) next.push(expiryDateObj);
90	      while (next.length > n) next.pop();
91	      return next;
92	    });
93	  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, expiryDateObj]);
94	
95	  // Resolved product name: canonical name takes priority over legacy product name
96	  const resolvedName = paramProductName ?? product?.name ?? 'Unknown';
97	  const resolvedUnit = paramUnit ?? product?.unit ?? null;
98	  const resolvedPackSize = paramPackSize ?? null;
99	  const packDisplay =
100	    resolvedPackSize != null && resolvedUnit
101	      ? `${resolvedPackSize} ${resolvedUnit}`
102	      : resolvedUnit ?? null;
103	
104	  useEffect(() => {
105	    (async () => {
106	      setProduct(await findProduct(barcode));
107	      // If we have a productId from catalog, look up order item by product_id
108	      if (productId) {
109	        const byProduct = await findOpenItemByProductId(productId);
110	        setItem(byProduct as OrderItem | null);
111	      } else {
112	        setItem(await findOpenItemByBarcode(barcode));
113	      }
114	    })();
115	  }, [barcode, productId]);
116	
117	  // Auto-fill expiry from last scanned item in the order session
118	  useEffect(() => {
119	    if (orderSession.lastExpiry && !noExpiry && !expiryDateObj) {
120	      setExpiryDateObj(new Date(orderSession.lastExpiry));
121	    }
122	  }, [orderSession.lastExpiry, noExpiry, expiryDateObj]);
123	
124	  const setExact = (n: number) => {
125	    haptic.tap();
126	    setQty(n);
127	  };
128	
129	  const expiryDate = useMemo(() => {
130	    if (noExpiry || !expiryDateObj) return null;
131	    return toISO(expiryDateObj);
132	  }, [expiryDateObj, noExpiry]);
133	
134	  const handleQuickMonth = (months: number) => {
135	    haptic.tap();
136	    const d = new Date();
137	    d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + months);
138	    setExpiryDateObj(d);
139	    setNoExpiry(false);
140	  };
141	
142	  const handlePickerChange = (date: Date) => {
143	    setExpiryDateObj(date);
144	    setNoExpiry(false);
145	  };
146	
147	  /** Standalone receipt path — when no order session is active */
148	  const persistStandalone = async (flagged: boolean) => {
149	    const session = getSession();
150	    // Per-pack expiry: split into N qty=1 receipts so each pack carries
151	    // its own expiry date. Single-expiry path stays as one bulk row.
152	    const receipts: Array<{ id: string; qty: number; expiry: string | null }> = perPackExpiry
153	      ? perPackExpiries.map((d) => ({
154	          id: `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`,
155	          qty: 1,
156	          expiry: d ? toISO(d) : null,
157	        }))
158	      : [{ id: `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`, qty, expiry: expiryDate }];
159	
160	    // The first receipt's id is what we use to link mismatch flags etc.
161	    const id = receipts[0].id;
162	
163	    for (const r of receipts) {
164	      await enqueue('receipt', {
165	        id: r.id,
166	        order_id: item?.order_id ?? null,
167	        order_item_id: item?.id ?? null,
168	        product_id: productId ?? null,
169	        barcode,
170	        product_name: resolvedName,
171	        qty: r.qty,
172	        expiry_date: r.expiry,
173	        flagged,
174	        scanned_at: Date.now(),
175	        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,
176	        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,
177	      });
178	    }
179	
180	    // Barcode learning: if this receipt was resolved via canonical catalog,
181	    // permanently map this barcode to the product_id for auto-resolution.
182	    if (productId && !barcode.startsWith('catalog_')) {
183	      await learnBarcode(barcode, productId, 'scan');
184	      // Also tell the backend to learn it
185	      await enqueue('learn_barcode', {
186	        barcode,
187	        product_id: productId,
188	      }).catch(() => {});
189	    }
190	    if (flagged) {
191	      await enqueue('mismatch_flag', {
192	        id: `flg_${nanoid(12)}`,
193	        receipt_id: id,
194	        order_item_id: item?.id ?? null,
195	        barcode,
196	        expected: item?.expected_qty ?? null,
197	        received_total: (item?.received_qty ?? 0) + qty,
198	        flagged_at: Date.now(),
199	      });
200	    }
201	    if (item) await addReceivedQty(item.id, qty);
202	
203	    const existing = await findStock(barcode);
204	    if (existing) {
205	      await adjustOnHand(barcode, qty);
206	    } else {
207	      await upsertStock({
208	        barcode,
209	        name: resolvedName,
210	        category: product?.category ?? null,
211	        unit: resolvedUnit,
212	        pack_size: resolvedPackSize,
213	        on_hand: qty,
214	        threshold: 0,
215	      });
216	    }
217	
218	    flushOnce().catch(() => {});
219	
220	    // Track expiry for future alerts
221	    if (expiryDate) {
222	      await trackExpiry({
223	        barcode,
224	        productName: resolvedName,
225	        expiryDate,
226	        qty,
227	        receiptId: id,
228	        performedBy: session?.guardId ?? null,
229	        performedByName: session?.guardName ?? null,
230	      });
231	    }
232	  };
233	
234	  /** Add to order session */
235	  const addToSession = () => {
236	    // Per-pack expiry → N qty=1 batches each with its own date.
237	    // Single-expiry → one batch with the full qty.
238	    const batches = perPackExpiry
239	      ? perPackExpiries.map((d) => ({ qty: 1, expiry: d ? toISO(d) : null }))
240	      : [{ qty, expiry: expiryDate }];
241	    const totalQty = batches.reduce((s, b) => s + b.qty, 0);
242	
243	    orderSession.addItem({
244	      barcode,
245	      productId: productId ?? null,
246	      name: resolvedName,
247	      category: product?.category ?? null,
248	      unit: resolvedUnit,
249	      packSize: resolvedPackSize,
250	      qty: totalQty,
251	      batches,
252	    });
253	    haptic.success();
254	    navigation.navigate('OrderSession');
255	  };
256	
257	  /** Standalone confirm (no active order session) */
258	  const confirmStandalone = async () => {
259	    setSaving(true);
260	    try {
261	      await persistStandalone(false);
262	      haptic.success();
263	      navigation.replace('DeliverySummary', {
264	        items: [{ name: resolvedName, category: product?.category ?? null, qty }],
265	        totalItems: 1,
266	        totalQty: qty,
267	        productName: resolvedName,
268	        qty,
269	        expected: item?.expected_qty ?? null,
270	        flagged: false,
271	      });
272	    } finally {
273	      setSaving(false);
274	    }
275	  };
276	
277	  const flagAndContinueStandalone = async () => {
278	    setSaving(true);
279	    try {
280	      await persistStandalone(true);
281	      haptic.warn();
282	      navigation.replace('DeliverySummary', {
283	        items: [{ name: resolvedName, category: product?.category ?? null, qty }],
284	        totalItems: 1,
285	        totalQty: qty,
286	        productName: resolvedName,
287	        qty,
288	        expected: item?.expected_qty ?? null,
289	        flagged: true,
290	      });
291	    } finally {
292	      setSaving(false);
293	    }
294	  };
295	
296	  const expected = item?.expected_qty ?? null;
297	  const alreadyIn = item?.received_qty ?? 0;
298	  const projected = alreadyIn + qty;
299	  const mismatch = expected !== null && projected > expected;
300	
301	  return (
302	    <KeyboardAvoidingView
303	      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'height' : undefined}
304	      style={{ flex: 1 }}
305	    >
306	      <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
307	        <AppBar
308	          title={t('receiveItem')}
309	          onBack={() => navigation.goBack()}
310	          trailing={
311	            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.xs }}>
312	              {expected !== null ? (
313	                <StatusPill
314	                  label={`${projected} / ${expected}`}
315	                  tone={mismatch ? 'danger' : projected === expected ? 'success' : 'neutral'}
316	                />
317	              ) : null}
318	              <IconButton Icon={ScanLine} onPress={() => navigation.replace('Scanner')} />
319	            </View>
320	          }
321	        />
322	
323	        <ScrollView
324	          contentContainerStyle={[styles.content, { paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl + kbHeight }]}
325	          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
326	        >
327	        <Card tone="elevated" padding="xl">
328	          <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
329	            {t('product').toUpperCase()}
330	          </Text>
331	          <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-end', marginTop: spacing.xs, gap: spacing.md }}>
332	            <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ flex: 1 }}>
333	              {resolvedName}
334	            </Text>
335	            {packDisplay ? (
336	              <Text variant="titleMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
337	                {packDisplay}
338	              </Text>
339	            ) : null}
340	          </View>
341	          {/* Barcode chip. For real (scanned or already-learned) barcodes
342	              we surface the code so guards can confirm at a glance. For
343	              an unmapped catalog pick we don't dump the synthetic
344	              `catalog_<product_id>` id — it's meaningless to the user.
345	              We just label the row "No barcode yet" so it's obvious why
346	              there's nothing to read. */}
347	          {barcode.startsWith('catalog_') ? (
348	            <View
349	              style={{
350	                marginTop: spacing.sm,
351	                alignSelf: 'flex-start',
352	                paddingHorizontal: spacing.md,
353	                paddingVertical: spacing.xs,
354	                borderRadius: radius.sm,
355	                borderWidth: 1,
356	                borderStyle: 'dashed',
357	                borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
358	                backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
359	              }}
360	            >
361	              <Text variant="labelMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
362	                {t('noBarcodeYet')}
363	              </Text>
364	            </View>
365	          ) : (
366	            <View
367	              style={{
368	                marginTop: spacing.sm,
369	                alignSelf: 'flex-start',
370	                paddingHorizontal: spacing.md,
371	                paddingVertical: spacing.xs,
372	                borderRadius: radius.sm,
373	                borderWidth: 1,
374	                borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
375	                backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
376	              }}
377	            >
378	              <Text
379	                variant="titleMedium"
380	                color={palette.onSurface}
381	                style={{
382	                  fontFamily: Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'Menlo' : 'monospace',
383	                  letterSpacing: 1.5,
384	                }}
385	              >
386	                {barcode}
387	              </Text>
388	            </View>
389	          )}
390	          {expected !== null ? (
391	            <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>
392	              {t('expectedShort')} {expected} · {alreadyIn} {t('alreadyReceived')}
393	            </Text>
394	          ) : (
395	            <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>
396	              {t('noMatchingOrder')}
397	            </Text>
398	          )}
399	        </Card>
400	
401	        <View style={styles.qtySection}>
402	          <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
403	            {t('quantityReceived').toUpperCase()}
404	          </Text>
405	          <QtyStepper value={qty} onChange={setQty} min={1} />
406	          <View style={styles.quickRow}>
407	            {[5, 10, 20, 50].map((n) => (
408	              <QuickChip key={n} value={n} active={qty === n} onPress={() => setExact(n)} />
409	            ))}
410	          </View>
411	          {mismatch ? (
412	            <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginTop: spacing.md }}>
413	              <StatusPill
414	                label={`Over by ${projected - expected!}`}
415	                tone="danger"
416	                Icon={AlertTriangle}
417	              />
418	            </View>
419	          ) : null}
420	        </View>
421	
422	        {/* Expiry Date Section */}
423	        <View style={styles.expirySection}>
424	          <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
425	            {t('expiryDate').toUpperCase()}
426	          </Text>
427	
428	          {/* Per-pack toggle. Only useful for multi-quantity scans. */}
429	          {qty > 1 && !noExpiry && (
430	            <Pressable
431	              onPress={() => { setPerPackExpiry((v) => !v); haptic.tap(); }}
432	              style={[styles.noExpiryRow, { marginTop: spacing.sm }]}
433	            >
434	              <View
435	                style={[
436	                  styles.checkbox,
437	                  {
438	                    backgroundColor: perPackExpiry ? palette.primary : 'transparent',
439	                    borderColor: perPackExpiry ? palette.primary : palette.outline,
440	                  },
441	                ]}
442	              >
443	                {perPackExpiry && <Check size={14} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={3} />}
444	              </View>
445	              <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
446	                {t('perPackExpiry')} ({Math.floor(qty)} {t('packsLabel')})
447	              </Text>
448	            </Pressable>
449	          )}
450	
451	          {!noExpiry && !perPackExpiry && (
452	            <>
453	              <Pressable
454	                onPress={() => setShowPicker(true)}
455	                style={[
456	                  styles.expiryInput,
457	                  {
458	                    backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
459	                    borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
460	                    flexDirection: 'row',
461	                    alignItems: 'center',
462	                  },
463	                ]}
464	              >
465	                <Calendar size={20} color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} strokeWidth={2} />
466	                <Text
467	                  variant="bodyLarge"
468	                  color={expiryDateObj ? palette.onSurface : palette.onSurfaceVariant}
469	                  style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm, flex: 1, letterSpacing: 1 }}
470	                >
471	                  {expiryDateObj ? formatForDisplay(toISO(expiryDateObj)) : t('tapToSelectDate')}
472	                </Text>
473	              </Pressable>
474	              {showPicker && (
475	                <DatePickerModal
476	                  visible={showPicker}
477	                  value={expiryDateObj}
478	                  minimumDate={new Date()}
479	                  onSelect={handlePickerChange}
480	                  onDismiss={() => setShowPicker(false)}
481	                />
482	              )}
483	              <View style={styles.quickRow}>
484	                {QUICK_MONTHS.map((q) => (
485	                  <Pressable
486	                    key={q.label}
487	                    onPress={() => handleQuickMonth(q.months)}
488	                    android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant }}
489	                    style={[styles.monthChip, { backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLow, borderColor: palette.outlineVariant }]}
490	                  >
491	                    <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurface}>
492	                      {q.label}
493	                    </Text>
494	                  </Pressable>
495	                ))}
496	              </View>
497	            </>
498	          )}
499	
500	          {!noExpiry && perPackExpiry && (
501	            <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm, gap: spacing.xs }}>
502	              {perPackExpiries.map((d, i) => (
503	                <Pressable
504	                  key={i}
505	                  onPress={() => { setEditingPackIdx(i); setShowPicker(true); }}
506	                  style={[
507	                    styles.expiryInput,
508	                    {
509	                      backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
510	                      borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
511	                      flexDirection: 'row',
512	                      alignItems: 'center',
513	                    },
514	                  ]}
515	                >
516	                  <Text variant="labelMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginRight: spacing.sm, minWidth: 56 }}>
517	                    {t('pack')} {i + 1}
518	                  </Text>
519	                  <Calendar size={18} color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} strokeWidth={2} />
520	                  <Text
521	                    variant="bodyLarge"
522	                    color={d ? palette.onSurface : palette.onSurfaceVariant}
523	                    style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm, flex: 1, letterSpacing: 1 }}
524	                  >
525	                    {d ? formatForDisplay(toISO(d)) : t('tapToSelectDate')}
526	                  </Text>
527	                </Pressable>
528	              ))}
529	              {showPicker && editingPackIdx != null && (
530	                <DatePickerModal
531	                  visible={showPicker}
532	                  value={perPackExpiries[editingPackIdx]}
533	                  minimumDate={new Date()}
534	                  onSelect={(date) => {
535	                    setPerPackExpiries((prev) => prev.map((p, i) => (i === editingPackIdx ? date : p)));
536	                  }}
537	                  onDismiss={() => { setShowPicker(false); setEditingPackIdx(null); }}
538	                />
539	              )}
540	            </View>
541	          )}
542	
543	          <Pressable
544	            onPress={() => { setNoExpiry(!noExpiry); haptic.tap(); }}
545	            style={styles.noExpiryRow}
546	          >
547	            <View
548	              style={[
549	                styles.checkbox,
550	                {
551	                  backgroundColor: noExpiry ? palette.primary : 'transparent',
552	                  borderColor: noExpiry ? palette.primary : palette.outline,
553	                },
554	              ]}
555	            >
556	              {noExpiry && <Check size={14} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={3} />}
557	            </View>
558	            <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
559	              {t('noExpiry')}
560	            </Text>
561	          </Pressable>
562	        </View>
563	      </ScrollView>
564	
565	        <View style={[styles.footer, { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant }]}>
566	          {/* Primary action: add to order session */}
567	          <Button
568	            label={t('addToOrder')}
569	            onPress={addToSession}
570	            loading={saving}
571	            size="lg"
572	            fullWidth
573	            leadingIcon={<Check size={22} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={2.4} />}
574	          />
575	          {/* Secondary: standalone receipt (when not in a multi-scan flow) */}
576	          {!orderSession.isActive && mismatch && (
577	            <Button
578	              label={t('flagAndContinue')}
579	              onPress={flagAndContinueStandalone}
580	              variant="danger"
581	              loading={saving}
582	              size="md"
583	              fullWidth
584	              style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}
585	            />
586	          )}
587	          <Button
588	            label={t('cancel')}
589	            variant="text"
590	            onPress={() => navigation.popToTop()}
591	            style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}
592	          />
593	        </View>
594	      </View>
595	    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
596	  );
597	}
598	
599	function QuickChip({ value, active, onPress }: { value: number; active: boolean; onPress: () => void }) {
600	  const { palette } = useTheme();
601	  return (
602	    <Pressable
603	      onPress={onPress}
604	      android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant }}
605	      style={[
606	        styles.quickChip,
607	        {
608	          backgroundColor: active ? palette.secondaryContainer : palette.surfaceContainerLow,
609	          borderColor: active ? palette.secondaryContainer : palette.outlineVariant,
610	        },
611	      ]}
612	    >
613	      <Text variant="titleMedium" color={active ? palette.onSecondaryContainer : palette.onSurface}>
614	        {value}
615	      </Text>
616	    </Pressable>
617	  );
618	}
619	
620	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
621	  safe: { flex: 1 },
622	  content: { padding: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.xl, paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl },
623	  qtySection: { gap: spacing.lg, alignItems: 'stretch' },
624	  quickRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: spacing.sm, justifyContent: 'center' },
625	  quickChip: {
626	    minWidth: 64,
627	    paddingVertical: spacing.sm,
628	    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,
629	    borderRadius: radius.pill,
630	    borderWidth: 1,
631	    alignItems: 'center',
632	    overflow: 'hidden',
633	  },
634	  expirySection: { gap: spacing.sm },
635	  expiryInput: {
636	    borderRadius: radius.md,
637	    borderWidth: 1.5,
638	    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,
639	    minHeight: 52,
640	    fontSize: 18,
641	    letterSpacing: 2,
642	  },
643	  monthChip: {
644	    paddingVertical: spacing.sm,
645	    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,
646	    borderRadius: radius.pill,
647	    borderWidth: 1,
648	    overflow: 'hidden',
649	  },
650	  noExpiryRow: {
651	    flexDirection: 'row',
652	    alignItems: 'center',
653	    gap: spacing.sm,
654	    paddingVertical: spacing.xs,
655	  },
656	  checkbox: {
657	    width: 22,
658	    height: 22,
659	    borderRadius: 4,
660	    borderWidth: 2,
661	    alignItems: 'center',
662	    justifyContent: 'center',
663	  },
664	  footer: {
665	    padding: spacing.xl,
666	    borderTopWidth: 1,
667	  },
668	});

```

> AGENT

Scope flag before you say "go": this touches three subrepos, not just field-app:

1. **field-app** — new `EditStock` screen (or reuse `ReceivingScreen` with a `mode='correction'` param), tap handler on stock list, new outbox action `stock_correction` with reason field, `QtyStepper` needs `min=0` (currently `min=1`).
2. **office-ops-engine** — new `/api/inventory/corrections` endpoint, new `stock_corrections` table (id, barcode, product_name, old_qty, new_qty, old_expiry, new_expiry, reason, note, performed_by, performed_by_name, timestamp), Google Sheet writer to append to a new "Corrections" tab on the linked sheet.
3. **ops-dashboard** — new "Corrections" tab/view to browse the audit log.

A few last decisions:

- **Reuse `ReceivingScreen` vs. new `EditStockScreen`?** I lean **new screen**. ReceivingScreen has a lot of order-session / per-pack-expiry / mismatch-flagging code that doesn't apply to corrections, and overloading it with a mode flag will make both flows harder to reason about. New screen, but it visually mirrors the receiving layout so users feel at home.

1	import { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';
2	import { getDb, now } from './database';
3	
4	export type OutboxKind =
5	  | 'receipt'
6	  | 'product_registration'
7	  | 'issue'
8	  | 'dispense'
9	  | 'reorder_request'
10	  | 'mismatch_flag'
11	  | 'learn_barcode';
12	
13	export type OutboxRow = {
14	  id: string;
15	  kind: OutboxKind;
16	  payload: string;
17	  status: 'queued' | 'sending' | 'sent' | 'failed';
18	  attempts: number;
19	  last_error: string | null;
20	  created_at: number;
21	  updated_at: number;
22	};
23	
24	export async function enqueue(kind: OutboxKind, payload: object): Promise<string> {
25	  const db = await getDb();
26	  const id = `out_${nanoid(12)}`;
27	  const ts = now();
28	  await db.runAsync(
29	    `INSERT INTO outbox (id, kind, payload, status, attempts, created_at, updated_at)
30	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'queued', 0, ?, ?)`,
31	    [id, kind, JSON.stringify(payload), ts, ts]
32	  );
33	  return id;
34	}
35	
36	/**
37	 * Outbox kinds that the user shouldn't see in the Sync Queue UI or
38	 * count in the Home badge. They still flush in the background; they're
39	 * just internal plumbing (e.g. teaching the server a new barcode →
40	 * product alias) that the user never explicitly triggered.
41	 */
42	export const INTERNAL_OUTBOX_KINDS: readonly OutboxKind[] = ['learn_barcode'];
43	
44	export async function pendingCount(): Promise<number> {
45	  const db = await getDb();
46	  const placeholders = INTERNAL_OUTBOX_KINDS.map(() => '?').join(',');
47	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ c: number }>(
48	    `SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM outbox
49	     WHERE status IN ('queued', 'failed') AND kind NOT IN (${placeholders})`,
50	    INTERNAL_OUTBOX_KINDS as string[]
51	  );
52	  return row?.c ?? 0;
53	}
54	
55	export async function nextBatch(limit: number): Promise<OutboxRow[]> {
56	  const db = await getDb();
57	  return db.getAllAsync<OutboxRow>(
58	    `SELECT * FROM outbox WHERE status IN ('queued', 'failed')
59	     ORDER BY created_at ASC LIMIT ?`,
60	    [limit]
61	  );
62	}
63	
64	export async function markSending(id: string): Promise<void> {
65	  const db = await getDb();
66	  await db.runAsync(
67	    `UPDATE outbox SET status = 'sending', last_error = NULL, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`,
68	    [now(), id]
69	  );
70	}
71	
72	/**
73	 * Reset rows orphaned mid-flight (process killed, OS reclaim, crash)
74	 * back to 'queued' so the next flush picks them up. Without this they
75	 * stay 'sending' forever — `nextBatch` only selects queued/failed.
76	 */
77	export async function recoverOrphanedSending(): Promise<number> {
78	  const db = await getDb();
79	  const result = await db.runAsync(
80	    `UPDATE outbox SET status = 'queued', updated_at = ? WHERE status = 'sending'`,
81	    [now()]
82	  );
83	  return result.changes ?? 0;
84	}
85	
86	export async function markSent(id: string): Promise<void> {
87	  const db = await getDb();
88	  await db.runAsync(
89	    `UPDATE outbox SET status = 'sent', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`,
90	    [now(), id]
91	  );
92	}
93	
94	export async function markFailed(id: string, err: string): Promise<void> {
95	  const db = await getDb();
96	  await db.runAsync(
97	    `UPDATE outbox SET status = 'failed', attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`,
98	    [err.slice(0, 500), now(), id]
99	  );
100	}
101

1	import { getDb, now } from './database';
2	
3	export type StockStatus = 'ok' | 'low' | 'out';
4	
5	export type StockRow = {
6	  barcode: string;
7	  name: string;
8	  category: string | null;
9	  unit: string | null;
10	  pack_size: number | null;
11	  on_hand: number;
12	  threshold: number;
13	  updated_at: number;
14	};
15	
16	export function statusFor(row: Pick<StockRow, 'on_hand' | 'threshold'>): StockStatus {
17	  if (row.on_hand <= 0) return 'out';
18	  if (row.on_hand <= row.threshold) return 'low';
19	  return 'ok';
20	}
21	
22	export async function listStock(): Promise<StockRow[]> {
23	  const db = await getDb();
24	  return db.getAllAsync<StockRow>('SELECT * FROM stock_levels ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE ASC');
25	}
26	
27	export async function listLowOrOut(): Promise<StockRow[]> {
28	  const db = await getDb();
29	  return db.getAllAsync<StockRow>(
30	    `SELECT * FROM stock_levels WHERE on_hand <= threshold ORDER BY on_hand ASC, name COLLATE NOCASE ASC`
31	  );
32	}
33	
34	export async function findStock(barcode: string): Promise<StockRow | null> {
35	  const db = await getDb();
36	  return (
37	    (await db.getFirstAsync<StockRow>('SELECT * FROM stock_levels WHERE barcode = ?', [barcode])) ??
38	    null
39	  );
40	}
41	
42	export async function upsertStock(row: {
43	  barcode: string;
44	  name: string;
45	  category: string | null;
46	  unit: string | null;
47	  pack_size?: number | null;
48	  on_hand?: number;
49	  threshold?: number;
50	}): Promise<void> {
51	  const db = await getDb();
52	  // COALESCE on pack_size so a later receipt that doesn't know the pack
53	  // size doesn't blank out a value learned from the catalog/registration.
54	  await db.runAsync(
55	    `INSERT INTO stock_levels (barcode, name, category, unit, pack_size, on_hand, threshold, updated_at)
56	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
57	     ON CONFLICT(barcode) DO UPDATE SET
58	       name=excluded.name,
59	       category=excluded.category,
60	       unit=excluded.unit,
61	       pack_size=COALESCE(excluded.pack_size, stock_levels.pack_size),
62	       updated_at=excluded.updated_at`,
63	    [
64	      row.barcode,
65	      row.name,
66	      row.category,
67	      row.unit,
68	      row.pack_size ?? null,
69	      row.on_hand ?? 0,
70	      row.threshold ?? 0,
71	      now(),
72	    ]
73	  );
74	}
75	
76	/**
77	 * Replace the local cached stock row with the server's values. Used by
78	 * screens after a successful remote fetch so on-hand and threshold
79	 * reflect the source of truth.
80	 */
81	export async function syncStockFromRemote(row: {
82	  barcode: string;
83	  name: string;
84	  category: string | null;
85	  unit: string | null;
86	  pack_size: number | null;
87	  on_hand: number;
88	  threshold: number;
89	}): Promise<void> {
90	  const db = await getDb();
91	  await db.runAsync(
92	    `INSERT INTO stock_levels (barcode, name, category, unit, pack_size, on_hand, threshold, updated_at)
93	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
94	     ON CONFLICT(barcode) DO UPDATE SET
95	       name=excluded.name,
96	       category=excluded.category,
97	       unit=excluded.unit,
98	       pack_size=excluded.pack_size,
99	       on_hand=excluded.on_hand,
100	       threshold=excluded.threshold,
101	       updated_at=excluded.updated_at`,
102	    [row.barcode, row.name, row.category, row.unit, row.pack_size, row.on_hand, row.threshold, now()]
103	  );
104	}
105	
106	/**
107	 * Replace the local stock cache with whatever the remote returned.
108	 * Upsert every remote row, then drop locally-cached rows the remote
109	 * no longer knows about. Prevents stale items lingering after an admin
110	 * wipes inventory data on the server.
111	 */
112	export async function replaceStockFromRemote(
113	  rows: Array<{
114	    barcode: string;
115	    name: string;
116	    category: string | null;
117	    unit: string | null;
118	    pack_size: number | null;
119	    on_hand: number;
120	    threshold: number;
121	  }>,
122	): Promise<void> {
123	  const db = await getDb();
124	  for (const row of rows) {
125	    await syncStockFromRemote(row);
126	  }
127	  if (rows.length === 0) {
128	    // Remote is empty — wipe local too.
129	    await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM stock_levels`);
130	    return;
131	  }
132	  const ids = rows.map((r) => `'${String(r.barcode).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`).join(',');
133	  await db.runAsync(`DELETE FROM stock_levels WHERE barcode NOT IN (${ids})`);
134	}
135	
136	export async function adjustOnHand(barcode: string, delta: number): Promise<void> {
137	  const db = await getDb();
138	  await db.runAsync(
139	    `UPDATE stock_levels SET on_hand = MAX(0, on_hand + ?), updated_at = ? WHERE barcode = ?`,
140	    [delta, now(), barcode]
141	  );
142	}
143

1	import { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';
2	import { getDb, now } from './database';
3	
4	// ---- Types ---------------------------------------------------------------
5	
6	export type ExpiryTrackingRow = {
7	  id: string;
8	  barcode: string;
9	  product_name: string;
10	  expiry_date: string; // ISO date, e.g. "2027-03-15"
11	  qty: number;
12	  receipt_id: string | null;
13	  received_at: number;
14	  performed_by: string | null;
15	  performed_by_name: string | null;
16	};
17	
18	export type ExpiryAlertRow = ExpiryTrackingRow & {
19	  /** Days until expiry (negative = already expired) */
20	  days_remaining: number;
21	};
22	
23	// ---- Writes --------------------------------------------------------------
24	
25	/**
26	 * Record that a received item has a specific expiry date.
27	 * Called from ReceivingScreen / OrderSessionContext after each receipt.
28	 */
29	export async function trackExpiry(params: {
30	  barcode: string;
31	  productName: string;
32	  expiryDate: string;
33	  qty: number;
34	  receiptId: string | null;
35	  performedBy: string | null;
36	  performedByName: string | null;
37	}): Promise<void> {
38	  const db = await getDb();
39	  const id = `exp_${nanoid(12)}`;
40	  await db.runAsync(
41	    `INSERT INTO expiry_tracking (id, barcode, product_name, expiry_date, qty, receipt_id, received_at, performed_by, performed_by_name)
42	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
43	    [
44	      id,
45	      params.barcode,
46	      params.productName,
47	      params.expiryDate,
48	      params.qty,
49	      params.receiptId,
50	      now(),
51	      params.performedBy,
52	      params.performedByName,
53	    ],
54	  );
55	
56	  // Update the stock_levels row's nearest_expiry if this one is sooner
57	  await db.runAsync(
58	    `UPDATE stock_levels
59	     SET nearest_expiry = CASE
60	       WHEN nearest_expiry IS NULL THEN ?
61	       WHEN ? < nearest_expiry THEN ?
62	       ELSE nearest_expiry
63	     END,
64	     updated_at = ?
65	     WHERE barcode = ?`,
66	    [params.expiryDate, params.expiryDate, params.expiryDate, now(), params.barcode],
67	  );
68	}
69	
70	// ---- Reads ---------------------------------------------------------------
71	
72	/**
73	 * List items expiring within the next `withinDays` days, ordered by soonest.
74	 * Also includes items that have *already expired* (negative days_remaining).
75	 */
76	export async function listExpiringSoon(withinDays: number = 30): Promise<ExpiryAlertRow[]> {
77	  const db = await getDb();
78	  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]; // "2026-05-27"
79	  const cutoff = new Date();
80	  cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() + withinDays);
81	  const cutoffStr = cutoff.toISOString().split('T')[0];
82	
83	  const rows = await db.getAllAsync<ExpiryTrackingRow & { days_remaining: number }>(
84	    `SELECT *,
85	       CAST(julianday(expiry_date) - julianday(?) AS INTEGER) as days_remaining
86	     FROM expiry_tracking
87	     WHERE expiry_date <= ?
88	     ORDER BY expiry_date ASC
89	     LIMIT 200`,
90	    [today, cutoffStr],
91	  );
92	  return rows;
93	}
94	
95	/**
96	 * List items that have already expired (expiry_date < today).
97	 */
98	export async function listExpired(): Promise<ExpiryAlertRow[]> {
99	  const db = await getDb();
100	  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
101	  return db.getAllAsync<ExpiryAlertRow>(
102	    `SELECT *,
103	       CAST(julianday(expiry_date) - julianday(?) AS INTEGER) as days_remaining
104	     FROM expiry_tracking
105	     WHERE expiry_date < ?
106	     ORDER BY expiry_date ASC
107	     LIMIT 200`,
108	    [today, today],
109	  );
110	}
111	
112	/**
113	 * Count items expiring within N days (for badge/tile on home screen).
114	 */
115	export async function expiringCount(withinDays: number = 30): Promise<number> {
116	  const db = await getDb();
117	  const cutoff = new Date();
118	  cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() + withinDays);
119	  const cutoffStr = cutoff.toISOString().split('T')[0];
120	
121	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ cnt: number }>(
122	    `SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt FROM expiry_tracking WHERE expiry_date <= ?`,
123	    [cutoffStr],
124	  );
125	  return row?.cnt ?? 0;
126	}
127	
```

1	/**
2	 * Backend client for the office-ops inventory plugin.
3	 *
4	 * Every request:
5	 *   - is prefixed with `${config.apiBaseUrl}/api/inventory/...`
6	 *   - carries `x-guard-token` (from session) and `x-site-id` / `x-device-id`
7	 *   - throws an `ApiError` with status + body on non-2xx
8	 *
9	 * The outbox flusher (syncService) calls `api.send.*`; screens call
10	 * `api.fetch.*`. When no session token is present, requests still go out
11	 * — the server replies 401 and the outbox keeps the row queued (or
12	 * the read screens fall back to local cache).
13	 */
14	
15	import { config } from '../config';
16	import { getSession } from '../auth/session';
17	import { getDeviceId } from '../utils/device';
18	
19	export class ApiError extends Error {
20	  constructor(public status: number, message: string, public body?: string) {
21	    super(message);
22	  }
23	  /** Friendly composite for surfaces that only see `err.message` — e.g.
24	   *  the outbox's `last_error` column. Pulls the server's
25	   *  `{ error: "..." }` body when present so the guard sees a real
26	   *  reason instead of just "HTTP 500". */
27	  static format(status: number, path: string, body: string | undefined): string {
28	    if (body) {
29	      try {
30	        const parsed = JSON.parse(body);
31	        if (parsed && typeof parsed.error === 'string') {
32	          return `HTTP ${status} on ${path} — ${parsed.error}`;
33	        }
34	      } catch {
35	        // not JSON, fall through
36	      }
37	      const trimmed = body.trim();
38	      if (trimmed.length > 0) {
39	        return `HTTP ${status} on ${path} — ${trimmed.slice(0, 180)}`;
40	      }
41	    }
42	    return `HTTP ${status} on ${path}`;
43	  }
44	}
45	
46	async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
47	  const deviceId = await getDeviceId();
48	  const session = getSession();
49	  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
50	    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
51	    'X-Device-Id': deviceId,
52	    'X-Site-Id': config.siteId,
53	    ...((init?.headers as Record<string, string>) ?? {}),
54	  };
55	  if (session?.token) headers['X-Guard-Token'] = session.token;
56	
57	  const res = await fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}${path}`, { ...init, headers });
58	  if (!res.ok) {
59	    const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');
60	    const body = text.slice(0, 500);
61	    throw new ApiError(res.status, ApiError.format(res.status, path, body), body);
62	  }
63	  return res.json() as Promise<T>;
64	}
65	
66	const post = (path: string, payload: unknown) =>
67	  request<{ inserted?: boolean; id?: string; barcode?: string }>(path, {
68	    method: 'POST',
69	    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
70	  });
71	
72	export type RemoteOrderItem = {
73	  id: string;
74	  order_id: string;
75	  barcode: string | null;
76	  product_id: string | null;
77	  product_name: string;
78	  expected_qty: number;
79	  received_qty: number;
80	};
81	
82	export type RemoteOrder = {
83	  id: string;
84	  site_id: string;
85	  vendor: string | null;
86	  expected_at: string | null;
87	  arrived_at: string | null;
88	  status: string;
89	  items: RemoteOrderItem[];
90	};
91	
92	export type RemoteStockRow = {
93	  barcode: string;
94	  site_id: string;
95	  name: string;
96	  category: string | null;
97	  unit: string | null;
98	  on_hand: number;
99	  threshold: number;
100	  pack_size: number | null;
101	};
102	
103	export type RemoteProduct = {
104	  barcode: string;
105	  site_id: string;
106	  name: string;
107	  category: string | null;
108	  unit: string | null;
109	  pack_size: number | null;
110	};
111	
112	export type RemoteCatalogHit = {
113	  product: RemoteProduct | null;
114	  openOrderItem: RemoteOrderItem | null;
115	};
116	
117	export type RemoteCanonicalProduct = {
118	  product_id: string;
119	  site_id: string;
120	  canonical_name: string;
121	  category: string | null;
122	  hsn_code: string | null;
123	  unit: string;
124	  pack_size: number;
125	  created_at: string;
126	  updated_at: string;
127	  /** True iff at least one barcode is mapped to this product server-side.
128	   *  Drives the "hide already-taken products" filter when registering a
129	   *  brand-new barcode on the field-app. */
130	  has_barcode?: boolean;
131	};
132	
133	export const api = {
134	  // Login is handled by visitor's guard auth endpoint, not the inventory
135	  // plugin. Lives here so screens have one client to import.
136	  async login(input: { guardName: string; pin: string }): Promise<{
137	    token: string;
138	    guardId: string;
139	    guardName: string;
140	    language: 'hindi' | 'english';
141	  }> {
142	    const deviceId = await getDeviceId();
143	    const res = await fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}/api/guard/login`, {
144	      method: 'POST',
145	      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
146	      body: JSON.stringify({
147	        guardName: input.guardName,
148	        pin: input.pin,
149	        deviceId,
150	        deviceName: 'field-app',
151	      }),
152	    });
153	    if (!res.ok) {
154	      const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');
155	      // Pull the server's reason out of `{ "error": "..." }` if present so
156	      // the LoginScreen shows something actionable instead of just "Login failed".
157	      let reason = text.slice(0, 200);
158	      try {
159	        const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
160	        if (parsed && typeof parsed.error === 'string') reason = parsed.error;
161	      } catch {}
162	      throw new ApiError(res.status, reason || `Login failed (${res.status})`, text.slice(0, 500));
163	    }
164	    return res.json();
165	  },
166	
167	  // Writes (outbox-dispatched).
168	  send: {
169	    receipt: (payload: object) => post('/api/inventory/receipts', payload),
170	    product: (payload: object) => post('/api/inventory/products', payload),
171	    issue: (payload: object) => post('/api/inventory/issues', payload),
172	    reorderRequest: (payload: object) =>
173	      post('/api/inventory/reorder-requests', payload),
174	    mismatchFlag: (payload: object) =>
175	      post('/api/inventory/mismatch-flags', payload),
176	    learnBarcode: (payload: object) =>
177	      post('/api/inventory/canonical-products/learn-barcode', payload),
178	  },
179	
180	  // Reads (called by screens).
181	  fetch: {
182	    orders: () =>
183	      request<{ orders: RemoteOrder[] }>('/api/inventory/orders').then((r) => r.orders),
184	    stock: () =>
185	      request<{ stock: RemoteStockRow[] }>('/api/inventory/stock').then((r) => r.stock),
186	    alerts: () =>
187	      request<{ alerts: RemoteStockRow[] }>('/api/inventory/alerts').then((r) => r.alerts),
188	    catalog: (barcode: string) =>
189	      request<RemoteCatalogHit>(
190	        `/api/inventory/catalog/${encodeURIComponent(barcode)}`,
191	      ),
192	    normalizeName: (rawName: string) =>
193	      request<{
194	        canonical: string;
195	        language: 'en' | 'hi' | 'hinglish' | 'unknown';
196	        confidence: number;
197	        cached: boolean;
198	      }>('/api/inventory/products/normalize', {
199	        method: 'POST',
200	        body: JSON.stringify({ rawName }),
201	      }),
202	    canonicalProducts: () =>
203	      request<{ products: RemoteCanonicalProduct[] }>(
204	        '/api/inventory/canonical-products',
205	      ).then((r) => r.products),
206	  },
207	};
208

1	import * as Network from 'expo-network';
2	import { config } from '../config';
3	import { OutboxKind } from '../db/outbox';
4	import { markFailed, markSending, markSent, nextBatch, pendingCount, recoverOrphanedSending } from '../db/outbox';
5	import { replaceCanonicalProducts, type CanonicalProduct } from '../db/catalog';
6	import { upsertOrdersFromRemote } from '../db/orders';
7	import { api } from './api';
8	import { getSession } from '../auth/session';
9	
10	type Listener = (count: number, lastSyncAt: number | null) => void;
11	
12	let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
13	let running = false;
14	let retryQueued = false;
15	let _lastSyncAt: number | null = null;
16	const listeners = new Set<Listener>();
17	
18	export function getLastSyncAt(): number | null {
19	  return _lastSyncAt;
20	}
21	
22	export function onPendingChange(l: Listener): () => void {
23	  listeners.add(l);
24	  pendingCount().then((c) => l(c, _lastSyncAt)).catch(() => {});
25	  return () => listeners.delete(l);
26	}
27	
28	async function notify() {
29	  const c = await pendingCount().catch(() => 0);
30	  listeners.forEach((l) => l(c, _lastSyncAt));
31	}
32	
33	// Dispatch table for outbox kinds. Adding a new kind = one line here
34	// + a handler in api.send.*.
35	const DISPATCH: Record<OutboxKind, (payload: object) => Promise<unknown>> = {
36	  receipt: api.send.receipt,
37	  product_registration: api.send.product,
38	  issue: api.send.issue,
39	  dispense: api.send.issue, // same endpoint, renamed in-app
40	  reorder_request: api.send.reorderRequest,
41	  mismatch_flag: api.send.mismatchFlag,
42	  learn_barcode: api.send.learnBarcode,
43	};
44	
45	export async function flushOnce(): Promise<{ sent: number; failed: number }> {
46	  if (running) {
47	    // Queue a retry so manual "Sync Now" taps aren't silently dropped.
48	    retryQueued = true;
49	    return { sent: 0, failed: 0 };
50	  }
51	  running = true;
52	  let sent = 0;
53	  let failed = 0;
54	  try {
55	    const net = await Network.getNetworkStateAsync().catch(() => ({ isConnected: false }));
56	    if (!net.isConnected) return { sent, failed };
57	
58	    // Recover anything orphaned by a prior crash/kill before picking
59	    // the batch — otherwise the stuck row will be skipped again.
60	    await recoverOrphanedSending().catch(() => 0);
61	
62	    const batch = await nextBatch(config.outboxBatchSize);
63	    for (const row of batch) {
64	      const dispatch = DISPATCH[row.kind];
65	      if (!dispatch) {
66	        await markFailed(row.id, `Unknown kind: ${row.kind}`);
67	        failed++;
68	        continue;
69	      }
70	      try {
71	        await markSending(row.id);
72	        await dispatch(JSON.parse(row.payload));
73	        await markSent(row.id);
74	        sent++;
75	      } catch (err) {
76	        await markFailed(row.id, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
77	        failed++;
78	      }
79	    }
80	    if (sent > 0 || failed > 0) {
81	      _lastSyncAt = Date.now();
82	    }
83	
84	    // Skip pulls when not logged in — they'd 401 and only add log noise.
85	    // Login screen / AuthProvider will trigger a flush after a successful login.
86	    if (getSession()?.token) {
87	      // Pull latest canonical products catalog on each sync cycle.
88	      // This is lightweight (just a GET) and ensures guards always
89	      // have the latest admin-curated product names.
90	      await syncCanonicalProducts().catch(() => {});
91	      // Also mirror the open-orders list so ReceivingScreen can resolve
92	      // catalog picks to the right order item without a round-trip.
93	      await syncOrders().catch(() => {});
94	    }
95	  } finally {
96	    running = false;
97	    await notify();
98	  }
99	
100	  // If someone tapped "Sync Now" while we were busy, run again.
101	  if (retryQueued) {
102	    retryQueued = false;
103	    return flushOnce();
104	  }
105	
106	  return { sent, failed };
107	}
108	
109	/**
110	 * Pull the canonical product catalog from the backend and replace
111	 * the local SQLite cache. Guards pick from this list — keeping it
112	 * fresh is essential for the catalog-only gate flow.
113	 *
114	 * Returns the counts so the picker UI can surface "X of Y synced" and
115	 * the user can detect cache mismatches without having to dig into logs.
116	 */
117	let _lastCatalogSync: { remote: number; written: number; failed: number; error: string | null } = {
118	  remote: 0, written: 0, failed: 0, error: null,
119	};
120	export function getLastCatalogSync() { return _lastCatalogSync; }
121	
122	export async function syncCanonicalProducts(): Promise<{ remote: number; written: number; failed: number }> {
123	  try {
124	    const remote = await api.fetch.canonicalProducts();
125	    const local: CanonicalProduct[] = remote.map((r) => ({
126	      product_id: r.product_id,
127	      canonical_name: r.canonical_name,
128	      category: r.category,
129	      hsn_code: r.hsn_code,
130	      unit: r.unit,
131	      pack_size: r.pack_size,
132	      has_barcode: r.has_barcode ? 1 : 0,
133	      updated_at: Date.now(),
134	    }));
135	    const result = await replaceCanonicalProducts(local);
136	    _lastCatalogSync = {
137	      remote: remote.length,
138	      written: result.ok,
139	      failed: result.failed,
140	      error: result.firstError ?? null,
141	    };
142	    if (result.failed > 0) {
143	      // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
144	      console.warn(`[catalog] sync wrote ${result.ok}/${remote.length}; ${result.failed} failed. First error: ${result.firstError}`);
145	    }
146	    return { remote: remote.length, written: result.ok, failed: result.failed };
147	  } catch (err) {
148	    _lastCatalogSync = {
149	      ..._lastCatalogSync,
150	      error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
151	    };
152	    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
153	    console.warn('[catalog] sync failed:', err);
154	    return { remote: 0, written: 0, failed: 0 };
155	  }
156	}
157	
158	/**
159	 * Pull the current open-orders list from the backend and mirror it into
160	 * the local orders/order_items tables. Lets ReceivingScreen resolve a
161	 * catalog pick to the right order item locally.
162	 */
163	export async function syncOrders(): Promise<void> {
164	  try {
165	    const remote = await api.fetch.orders();
166	    await upsertOrdersFromRemote(remote);
167	  } catch {
168	    // Non-fatal: ReceivingScreen falls back to "no matching order".
169	  }
170	}
171	
172	let netSub: { remove: () => void } | null = null;
173	let wasOnline = true;
174	
175	export function startSync(): void {
176	  if (timer) return;
177	  // Recover anything left mid-flight by a previous process before the
178	  // first flush, otherwise nextBatch skips them and they stay stuck on
179	  // "Sending…" forever.
180	  recoverOrphanedSending()
181	    .then((n) => {
182	      if (n > 0) {
183	        // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
184	        console.log(`[sync] recovered ${n} orphaned 'sending' row${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
185	      }
186	    })
187	    .catch(() => {})
188	    .finally(() => {
189	      flushOnce().catch(() => {});
190	    });
191	  timer = setInterval(() => {
192	    flushOnce().catch(() => {});
193	  }, config.syncIntervalMs);
194	
195	  // Trigger a flush the moment connectivity returns, instead of waiting
196	  // up to syncIntervalMs for the next timer tick. This makes "I just
197	  // walked out from a dead zone" reliably catch up in ~1s.
198	  try {
199	    netSub = Network.addNetworkStateListener((state) => {
200	      const online = !!state.isConnected;
201	      if (online && !wasOnline) {
202	        // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
203	        console.log('[sync] connectivity returned — triggering flush');
204	        flushOnce().catch(() => {});
205	      }
206	      wasOnline = online;
207	    });
208	  } catch {
209	    // Listener API not available on this platform / SDK version; the
210	    // periodic timer still picks things up.
211	  }
212	}
213	
214	export function stopSync(): void {
215	  if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
216	  timer = null;
217	  if (netSub) {
218	    try { netSub.remove(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
219	    netSub = null;
220	  }
221	}
222

235	export type StringKey = keyof typeof STR;
236	
237	export function translate(key: StringKey, lang: Lang): string {
238	  const entry = STR[key];
239	  if (!entry) return key;
240	  return lang === 'hi' ? entry[1] : entry[0];
241	}

1	import { DefaultTheme, NavigationContainer } from '@react-navigation/native';
2	import { createNativeStackNavigator } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
3	import React from 'react';
4	import { useAuth } from '../auth';
5	import { getAllScreens } from '../modules';
6	import { OrderSessionProvider } from '../modules/inventory/components/OrderSessionContext';
7	import { LoginScreen } from '../modules/inventory/screens/LoginScreen';
8	import { useTheme } from '../theme';
9	
10	const Stack = createNativeStackNavigator();
11	
12	export function RootNavigator() {
13	  const { palette } = useTheme();
14	  const { session, ready } = useAuth();
15	
16	  const navTheme = {
17	    ...DefaultTheme,
18	    colors: {
19	      ...DefaultTheme.colors,
20	      primary: palette.primary,
21	      background: palette.background,
22	      card: palette.surface,
23	      text: palette.onSurface,
24	      border: palette.outlineVariant,
25	      notification: palette.error,
26	    },
27	  };
28	
29	  const screens = getAllScreens();
30	
31	  return (
32	    <OrderSessionProvider>
33	    <NavigationContainer theme={navTheme}>
34	      <Stack.Navigator
35	        screenOptions={{
36	          headerShown: false,
37	          contentStyle: { backgroundColor: palette.background },
38	          animation: 'slide_from_right',
39	        }}
40	      >
41	        {!ready || !session ? (
42	          <Stack.Screen name="Login" component={LoginScreen} options={{ animation: 'fade' }} />
43	        ) : (
44	          screens.map((s) => (
45	            <Stack.Screen
46	              key={s.name}
47	              name={s.name}
48	              component={s.component}
49	              options={s.options as any}
50	            />
51	          ))
52	        )}
53	      </Stack.Navigator>
54	    </NavigationContainer>
55	    </OrderSessionProvider>
56	  );
57	}
58

1	import * as SQLite from 'expo-sqlite';
2	
3	let dbInstance: SQLite.SQLiteDatabase | null = null;
4	
5	/**
6	 * Current schema version. Bump this and add a migration block in
7	 * `runMigrations()` whenever you need additive schema changes.
8	 */
9	const SCHEMA_VERSION = 6;
10	
11	export async function getDb(): Promise<SQLite.SQLiteDatabase> {
12	  if (dbInstance) return dbInstance;
13	  const db = await SQLite.openDatabaseAsync('fieldapp.db');
14	  await db.execAsync(`
15	    PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;
16	    PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;
17	
18	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS products (
19	      barcode TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
20	      name TEXT NOT NULL,
21	      category TEXT,
22	      unit TEXT,
23	      pack_size REAL,
24	      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
25	    );
26	
27	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS orders (
28	      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
29	      vendor TEXT,
30	      expected_at INTEGER,
31	      status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',
32	      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
33	    );
34	
35	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS order_items (
36	      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
37	      order_id TEXT NOT NULL,
38	      barcode TEXT,
39	      product_name TEXT NOT NULL,
40	      expected_qty REAL NOT NULL,
41	      received_qty REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
42	      FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
43	    );
44	
45	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS receipts (
46	      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
47	      order_id TEXT,
48	      barcode TEXT NOT NULL,
49	      product_name TEXT NOT NULL,
50	      qty REAL NOT NULL,
51	      scanned_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
52	      note TEXT
53	    );
54	
55	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS outbox (
56	      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
57	      kind TEXT NOT NULL,
58	      payload TEXT NOT NULL,
59	      status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued',
60	      attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
61	      last_error TEXT,
62	      created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
63	      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
64	    );
65	
66	    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_outbox_status ON outbox(status);
67	    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_items_order ON order_items(order_id);
68	
69	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS stock_levels (
70	      barcode TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
71	      name TEXT NOT NULL,
72	      category TEXT,
73	      unit TEXT,
74	      pack_size REAL,
75	      on_hand REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
76	      threshold REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
77	      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
78	    );
79	
80	    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_stock_category ON stock_levels(category);
81	  `);
82	
83	  await runMigrations(db);
84	  dbInstance = db;
85	  return db;
86	}
87	
88	/**
89	 * Forward-only migrations keyed by PRAGMA user_version. Each migration
90	 * runs once; version is bumped after success. ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN is
91	 * idempotent in SQLite (errors if column exists), so we guard with the
92	 * version check rather than catching errors.
93	 */
94	async function runMigrations(db: SQLite.SQLiteDatabase): Promise<void> {
95	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ user_version: number }>(
96	    'PRAGMA user_version',
97	  );
98	  let version = row?.user_version ?? 0;
99	
100	  if (version < 1) {
101	    await db.execAsync(`
102	      -- Per-scan expiry and audit columns on receipts
103	      ALTER TABLE receipts ADD COLUMN expiry_date TEXT;
104	      ALTER TABLE receipts ADD COLUMN performed_by TEXT;
105	      ALTER TABLE receipts ADD COLUMN performed_by_name TEXT;
106	
107	      -- Audit trail table
108	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS activity_log (
109	        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
110	        action TEXT NOT NULL,
111	        barcode TEXT,
112	        qty REAL,
113	        expiry_date TEXT,
114	        performed_by TEXT,
115	        performed_by_name TEXT,
116	        performed_at INTEGER NOT NULL
117	      );
118	
119	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_activity_action ON activity_log(action);
120	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_activity_barcode ON activity_log(barcode);
121	
122	      PRAGMA user_version = 1;
123	    `);
124	    version = 1;
125	  }
126	
127	  // Future migrations go here:
128	  // if (version < 3) { ... PRAGMA user_version = 3; }
129	
130	  if (version < 2) {
131	    await db.execAsync(`
132	      -- Track nearest expiry per stock item for quick alert queries
133	      ALTER TABLE stock_levels ADD COLUMN nearest_expiry TEXT;
134	
135	      -- Per-receipt expiry tracking. Each received item with an expiry date
136	      -- gets a row here. This lets us query "what's expiring in the next N days"
137	      -- and trace it back to the order/receipt it came from.
138	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS expiry_tracking (
139	        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
140	        barcode TEXT NOT NULL,
141	        product_name TEXT NOT NULL,
142	        expiry_date TEXT NOT NULL,
143	        qty REAL NOT NULL,
144	        receipt_id TEXT,
145	        received_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
146	        performed_by TEXT,
147	        performed_by_name TEXT
148	      );
149	
150	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_expiry_barcode ON expiry_tracking(barcode);
151	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_expiry_date ON expiry_tracking(expiry_date);
152	
153	      PRAGMA user_version = 2;
154	    `);
155	    version = 2;
156	  }
157	
158	  if (version < 3) {
159	    await db.execAsync(`
160	      -- Canonical product catalog (synced from backend).
161	      -- Guards pick from this list — no free-text product names.
162	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS canonical_products (
163	        product_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
164	        canonical_name TEXT NOT NULL,
165	        category TEXT,
166	        hsn_code TEXT,
167	        unit TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pcs',
168	        pack_size REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
169	        has_barcode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
170	        updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
171	      );
172	
173	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_canonical_name ON canonical_products(canonical_name);
174	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_canonical_hsn ON canonical_products(hsn_code);
175	
176	      -- Learned barcode → canonical product mappings.
177	      -- Populated when guard scans a barcode + picks from catalog.
178	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product_barcodes (
179	        barcode TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
180	        product_id TEXT NOT NULL,
181	        learned_from TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'scan',
182	        learned_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
183	        FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES canonical_products(product_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
184	      );
185	
186	      -- Link order items to canonical products for name-based matching
187	      ALTER TABLE order_items ADD COLUMN product_id TEXT;
188	
189	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_items_product ON order_items(product_id);
190	
191	      PRAGMA user_version = 3;
192	    `);
193	    version = 3;
194	  }
195	
196	  if (version < 4) {
197	    // Fresh installs already have pack_size from the initial CREATE TABLE;
198	    // only older DBs (created before the column was added to the base schema)
199	    // need the ALTER. Probe table_info to decide.
200	    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(
201	      'PRAGMA table_info(products)',
202	    );
203	    const hasPackSize = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'pack_size');
204	    if (!hasPackSize) {
205	      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE products ADD COLUMN pack_size REAL;`);
206	    }
207	    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 4;`);
208	    version = 4;
209	  }
210	
211	  if (version < 5) {
212	    // Mirror the canonical product's pack_size onto stock so the Stock
213	    // screen can render "{on_hand} × {pack_size} {unit}" without an extra
214	    // join at read time.
215	    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(
216	      'PRAGMA table_info(stock_levels)',
217	    );
218	    const hasPackSize = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'pack_size');
219	    if (!hasPackSize) {
220	      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE stock_levels ADD COLUMN pack_size REAL;`);
221	    }
222	    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 5;`);
223	    version = 5;
224	  }
225	
226	  if (version < 6) {
227	    // Track whether each canonical product already has a barcode mapped
228	    // server-side. The register-new-barcode suggestion list filters these
229	    // out so two barcodes can't map to the same product (current rule).
230	    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(
231	      'PRAGMA table_info(canonical_products)',
232	    );
233	    const hasBarcodeCol = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'has_barcode');
234	    if (!hasBarcodeCol) {
235	      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE canonical_products ADD COLUMN has_barcode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;`);
236	    }
237	    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 6;`);
238	    version = 6;
239	  }
240	}
241	
242	export function now(): number {
243	  return Date.now();
244	}
245

1	import type { StringKey } from '../i18n';
2	import type { Permission } from '../rbac';
3	
4	/**
5	 * A Module is a self-contained feature area (inventory, attendance,
6	 * maintenance, …). Each module declares its home-screen tile, the route it
7	 * lands on, the navigator screens it owns, and the permissions a user must
8	 * hold to see and enter it.
9	 *
10	 * Modules live under `src/modules/<id>/` with a single `index.ts` exporting
11	 * the Module object. Add a new module by importing it into the registry.
12	 */
13	export type ModuleId = 'inventory' | 'attendance' | 'dailyTasks' | 'maintenance';
14	
15	export type ModuleScreen = {
16	  /** Route name as it appears in the navigator. */
17	  name: string;
18	  /** React component for the screen. Typed loose to avoid leaking
19	   * RootStackParamList details into the registry. */
20	  component: React.ComponentType<any>;
21	  /** Override default screen options for this route. */
22	  options?: Record<string, unknown>;
23	};
24	
25	export type Module = {
26	  id: ModuleId;
27	  /** i18n key for the module's display name. */
28	  titleKey: StringKey;
29	  /** i18n key for the home-tile subtitle. */
30	  subtitleKey?: StringKey;
31	  /** Short emoji/glyph used by the launcher tile until proper icons land. */
32	  glyph: string;
33	  /** Route the home tile navigates to. */
34	  entryRoute: string;
35	  /** Permissions a user must hold to see this module on the home screen. */
36	  requiredPermissions: Permission[];
37	  /** Screens this module contributes to the root navigator. */
38	  screens: ModuleScreen[];
39	};
40
