```
1	import { Minus, Plus, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react-native';
2	import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
3	import { Pressable, StyleSheet, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';
4	import { useTheme } from '../theme';
5	import { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';
6	import { Text } from './Text';
7	import { spacing, type as typo } from './tokens';
8	
9	type Props = {
10	  value: number;
11	  onChange: (n: number) => void;
12	  min?: number;
13	  max?: number;
14	  /** Allow fractional values (e.g. 0.5 kg). +/- still nudge by 1. */
15	  decimal?: boolean;
16	};
17	
18	/**
19	 * Big stepper for low-literacy users. Plus/minus circles flank a tappable
20	 * display: tapping the number swaps it for an inline TextInput so power
21	 * users can type the qty directly. Commits on blur or submit.
22	 */
23	export function QtyStepper({ value, onChange, min = 1, max, decimal = true }: Props) {
24	  const { palette } = useTheme();
25	  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
26	  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(String(value));
27	  const inputRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
28	
29	  useEffect(() => {
30	    if (!editing) setDraft(String(value));
31	  }, [value, editing]);
32	
33	  const clamp = (n: number) => {
34	    let out = n;
35	    if (Number.isNaN(out)) out = min;
36	    if (out < min) out = min;
37	    if (max !== undefined && out > max) out = max;
38	    return out;
39	  };
40	
41	  const bump = (delta: number) => {
42	    haptic.tap();
43	    onChange(clamp(value + delta));
44	  };
45	
46	  const startEdit = () => {
47	    haptic.tap();
48	    setDraft(String(value));
49	    setEditing(true);
50	    requestAnimationFrame(() => inputRef.current?.focus());
51	  };
52	
53	  /**
54	   * Propagate every keystroke to the parent so a submit handler that
55	   * fires before our `onBlur` (e.g. tapping a Confirm button) still sees
56	   * the latest qty. The local `draft` keeps the literal text so partial
57	   * states like "" don't snap the display while typing.
58	   */
59	  const parseValue = (text: string) => decimal ? parseFloat(text) : parseInt(text, 10);
60	  const validText = (text: string) =>
61	    decimal ? /^-?\d*\.?\d*$/.test(text) : /^-?\d*$/.test(text);
62	
63	  const onTextChange = (text: string) => {
64	    if (!validText(text)) return; // reject stray characters
65	    setDraft(text);
66	    if (text.trim() === '' || text === '.' || text === '-') {
67	      // Don't push min on every backspace; wait for commit.
68	      return;
69	    }
70	    const parsed = parseValue(text);
71	    if (!Number.isNaN(parsed)) onChange(clamp(parsed));
72	  };
73	
74	  const commit = () => {
75	    const next = clamp(parseValue(draft));
76	    onChange(next);
77	    setDraft(String(next));
78	    setEditing(false);
79	  };
80	
81	  return (
82	    <View style={styles.row}>
83	      <StepperButton Icon={Minus} onPress={() => bump(-1)} bg={palette.primary} fg={palette.onPrimary} ripple={palette.outlineVariant} />
84	      {editing ? (
85	        <TextInput
86	          ref={inputRef}
87	          value={draft}
88	          onChangeText={onTextChange}
89	          onBlur={commit}
90	          onSubmitEditing={commit}
91	          keyboardType={decimal ? "decimal-pad" : "number-pad"}
92	          selectTextOnFocus
93	          returnKeyType="done"
94	          style={[
95	            typo.displayLarge,
96	            styles.qtyText,
97	            { color: palette.onSurface, borderBottomColor: palette.primary },
98	          ]}
99	        />
100	      ) : (
101	        <Pressable
102	          onPress={startEdit}
103	          android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant, borderless: true }}
104	          hitSlop={{ top: 12, bottom: 12, left: 12, right: 12 }}
105	        >
106	          <Text variant="displayLarge" style={styles.qtyText}>
107	            {value}
108	          </Text>
109	        </Pressable>
110	      )}
111	      <StepperButton Icon={Plus} onPress={() => bump(1)} bg={palette.primary} fg={palette.onPrimary} ripple={palette.outlineVariant} />
112	    </View>
113	  );
114	}
115	
116	function StepperButton({
117	  Icon,
118	  onPress,
119	  bg,
120	  fg,
121	  ripple,
122	}: {
123	  Icon: LucideIcon;
124	  onPress: () => void;
125	  bg: string;
126	  fg: string;
127	  ripple: string;
128	}) {
129	  return (
130	    <Pressable
131	      onPress={onPress}
132	      android_ripple={{ color: ripple, borderless: true, radius: 44 }}
133	      style={[styles.btn, { backgroundColor: bg }]}
134	    >
135	      <Icon size={40} color={fg} strokeWidth={2.4} />
136	    </Pressable>
137	  );
138	}
139	
140	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
141	  row: {
142	    flexDirection: 'row',
143	    alignItems: 'center',
144	    justifyContent: 'center',
145	    gap: spacing.xl,
146	  },
147	  btn: {
148	    width: 88,
149	    height: 88,
150	    borderRadius: 44,
151	    alignItems: 'center',
152	    justifyContent: 'center',
153	    overflow: 'hidden',
154	  },
155	  qtyText: {
156	    minWidth: 120,
157	    textAlign: 'center',
158	    paddingBottom: 2,
159	    borderBottomWidth: 2,
160	    borderBottomColor: 'transparent',
161	  },
162	});
163
```

```
1	import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
2	import { Check } from 'lucide-react-native';
3	import { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';
4	import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
5	import {
6	  FlatList,
7	  KeyboardAvoidingView,
8	  Platform,
9	  ScrollView,
10	  StyleSheet,
11	  TextInput,
12	  View,
13	} from 'react-native';
14	import {
15	  AppBar,
16	  Button,
17	  Card,
18	  Chip,
19	  QtyStepper,
20	  radius,
21	  Skeleton,
22	  spacing,
23	  Text,
24	} from '../../../design';
25	import { enqueue } from '../../../db/outbox';
26	import { adjustOnHand, findStock, listStock, StockRow, statusFor } from '../../../db/stock';
27	import { getSession } from '../../../auth/session';
28	import { useT } from '../../../i18n';
29	import { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';
30	import { flushOnce } from '../../../sync/syncService';
31	import { haptic } from '../../../utils/haptics';
32	import { useKeyboardHeight } from '../../../hooks/useKeyboardHeight';
33	import { FilterDropdown, FilterOption } from '../components/FilterDropdown';
34	import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
35	
36	type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Dispense'>;
37	
38	const REASONS = ['Office use', 'Pantry', 'Cleaning', 'Maintenance', 'Other'];
39	
40	const STATUS_OPTIONS: FilterOption[] = [
41	  { key: 'All', label: 'All Status' },
42	  { key: 'In Stock', label: 'In Stock' },
43	  { key: 'Low', label: 'Low' },
44	  { key: 'Out', label: 'Out of Stock' },
45	];
46	
47	/** Subtle background tint per stock health */
48	function cardBg(row: StockRow): string {
49	  const s = statusFor(row);
50	  if (s === 'out') return 'rgba(229, 57, 53, 0.07)';
51	  if (s === 'low') return 'rgba(249, 168, 37, 0.07)';
52	  if (row.on_hand >= row.threshold * 2) return 'rgba(67, 160, 71, 0.06)';
53	  return 'rgba(129, 199, 132, 0.06)';
54	}
55	
56	/** Count text color per stock health */
57	function countColor(row: StockRow): string {
58	  const s = statusFor(row);
59	  if (s === 'out') return '#E53935';
60	  if (s === 'low') return '#F9A825';
61	  if (row.on_hand >= row.threshold * 2) return '#43A047';
62	  return '#66BB6A';
63	}
64	
65	export function DispenseScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
66	  const t = useT();
67	  const { palette } = useTheme();
68	  const kbHeight = useKeyboardHeight();
69	  const initialBarcode = route.params?.barcode;
70	  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<StockRow | null>(null);
71	  const [all, setAll] = useState<StockRow[]>([]);
72	  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
73	  const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');
74	  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('All');
75	  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);
76	  const [reason, setReason] = useState(REASONS[0]);
77	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
78	  const [initialLoading, setInitialLoading] = useState(true);
79	
80	  const categoryOptions = useMemo<FilterOption[]>(() => {
81	    const cats = new Set(all.map((r) => r.category).filter(Boolean) as string[]);
82	    return [{ key: 'All', label: 'All Categories' }, ...Array.from(cats).sort().map((c) => ({ key: c, label: c }))];
83	  }, [all]);
84	
85	  useEffect(() => {
86	    listStock().then((rows) => {
87	      setAll(rows);
88	      setInitialLoading(false);
89	    });
90	  }, []);
91	
92	  useEffect(() => {
93	    if (initialBarcode) {
94	      findStock(initialBarcode).then((r) => r && setSelected(r));
95	    }
96	  }, [initialBarcode]);
97	
98	  const results = useMemo(() => {
99	    if (selected) return [];
100	    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
101	    return all
102	      .filter((r) => {
103	        if (category !== 'All' && r.category !== category) return false;
104	        if (statusFilter !== 'All') {
105	          const s = statusFor(r);
106	          if (statusFilter === 'In Stock' && s !== 'ok') return false;
107	          if (statusFilter === 'Low' && s !== 'low') return false;
108	          if (statusFilter === 'Out' && s !== 'out') return false;
109	        }
110	        if (!q) return true;
111	        return r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.barcode.includes(q) || (r.category ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
112	      })
113	      .slice(0, 30);
114	  }, [all, query, selected, category, statusFilter]);
115	
116	  const submit = async () => {
117	    if (!selected) return;
118	    const session = getSession();
119	    setSaving(true);
120	    try {
121	      await enqueue('dispense', {
122	        id: `dsp_${nanoid(12)}`,
123	        barcode: selected.barcode,
124	        product_name: selected.name,
125	        qty,
126	        reason,
127	        // taken_by is now always the logged-in guard — no separate
128	        // free-text "who" field. Removes one mandatory input from the
129	        // dispense flow.
130	        taken_by: session?.guardName ?? null,
131	        issued_at: Date.now(),
132	        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,
133	        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,
134	      });
135	      await adjustOnHand(selected.barcode, -qty);
136	      await flushOnce().catch(() => {});
137	      haptic.success();
138	      navigation.goBack();
139	    } finally {
140	      setSaving(false);
141	    }
142	  };
143	
144	  if (!selected) {
145	    return (
146	      <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
147	        <AppBar title={t('dispense')} subtitle={t('dispenseSub')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />
148	        <View style={styles.search}>
149	          <TextInput
150	            value={query}
151	            onChangeText={setQuery}
152	            placeholder={t('search')}
153	            placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
154	            style={[
155	            styles.searchInput,
156	            {
157	              backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
158	              borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
159	              color: palette.onSurface,
160	            },
161	          ]}
162	            autoFocus
163	          />
164	        </View>
165	        <View style={styles.filterRow}>
166	          <FilterDropdown
167	            label={t('filterCategory')}
168	            options={categoryOptions}
169	            selected={category}
170	            onSelect={setCategory}
171	          />
172	          <FilterDropdown
173	            label={t('filterStatus')}
174	            options={STATUS_OPTIONS}
175	            selected={statusFilter}
176	            onSelect={setStatusFilter}
177	          />
178	        </View>
179	        {initialLoading && all.length === 0 ? (
180	          <View style={styles.list}>
181	            {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
182	              <View key={i} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>
183	                <Card tone="filled" padding="lg">
184	                  <Skeleton width="55%" height={18} />
185	                  <View style={{ height: 6 }} />
186	                  <Skeleton width="75%" height={14} />
187	                </Card>
188	              </View>
189	            ))}
190	          </View>
191	        ) : (
192	          <FlatList
193	            data={results}
194	            keyExtractor={(r) => r.barcode}
195	            contentContainerStyle={styles.list}
196	            ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.sm }} />}
197	            renderItem={({ item }) => (
198	              <Card tone="filled" padding="lg" onPress={() => setSelected(item)} style={{ backgroundColor: cardBg(item) }}>
199	                <View style={styles.pickerRow}>
200	                  <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
201	                    <Text variant="titleMedium">{item.name}</Text>
202	                    <Text
203	                      variant="bodyMedium"
204	                      color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
205	                      style={{ marginTop: 2 }}
206	                    >
207	                      {item.category ?? '—'}
208	                    </Text>
209	                  </View>
210	                  <Text variant="titleLarge" color={countColor(item)} style={{ fontWeight: '700' }}>
211	                    {item.on_hand}
212	                  </Text>
213	                </View>
214	              </Card>
215	            )}
216	            ListEmptyComponent={
217	              <View style={styles.empty}>
218	                <Text variant="bodyLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
219	                  No items match.
220	                </Text>
221	              </View>
222	            }
223	          />
224	        )}
225	      </View>
226	    );
227	  }
228	
229	  return (
230	    <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
231	      <AppBar
232	        title={t('dispense')}
233	        subtitle={selected.name}
234	        onBack={() => setSelected(null)}
235	      />
236	      <KeyboardAvoidingView
237	        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
238	        style={{ flex: 1 }}
239	      >
240	        <ScrollView
241	          contentContainerStyle={[styles.body, { paddingBottom: spacing.xl + kbHeight }]}
242	          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
243	        >
244	          <Card tone="elevated" padding="xl">
245	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
246	              PRODUCT
247	            </Text>
248	            <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
249	              {selected.name}
250	            </Text>
251	            <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
252	              {selected.on_hand}
253	              {selected.pack_size != null && selected.pack_size !== 1 ? ` × ${selected.pack_size}` : ''}
254	              {selected.unit ? ` ${selected.unit}` : ''} {t('onHand')}
255	            </Text>
256	          </Card>
257	
258	          <View style={styles.qtySection}>
259	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
260	              {t('howMany').toUpperCase()}
261	            </Text>
262	            <QtyStepper value={qty} onChange={setQty} min={1} max={selected.on_hand || undefined} />
263	          </View>
264	
265	          <View>
266	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>
267	              {t('reason').toUpperCase()}
268	            </Text>
269	            <View style={styles.chipRow}>
270	              {REASONS.map((r) => (
271	                <Chip key={r} label={r} selected={r === reason} onPress={() => setReason(r)} />
272	              ))}
273	            </View>
274	          </View>
275	
276	          {/* Who took it: always the logged-in guard. No need for a
277	              text input; just surface their name so they know the
278	              record will be filed under them. */}
279	          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>
280	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
281	              {t('whoTook').toUpperCase()}
282	            </Text>
283	            <Text variant="titleMedium" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
284	              {getSession()?.guardName ?? '—'}
285	            </Text>
286	          </View>
287	        </ScrollView>
288	
289	        <View style={[styles.footer, { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant }]}>
290	          <Button
291	            label={t('confirm')}
292	            onPress={submit}
293	            loading={saving}
294	            size="lg"
295	            fullWidth
296	            leadingIcon={<Check size={22} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={2.4} />}
297	          />
298	        </View>
299	      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
300	    </View>
301	  );
302	}
303	
304	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
305	  safe: { flex: 1 },
306	  search: { paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl, paddingTop: spacing.sm },
307	  filterRow: {
```

```json
  "new_string": "export async function adjustOnHand(barcode: string, delta: number): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.runAsync(\n    `UPDATE stock_levels SET on_hand = MAX(0, on_hand + ?), updated_at = ? WHERE barcode = ?`,\n    [delta, now(), barcode]\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Set on_hand and nearest_expiry directly. Used by the \"Edit stock\"\n * correction flow — guard is overwriting the count/expiry, not adding or\n * dispensing. Caller is also expected to enqueue a `stock_correction` row\n * so the server keeps an audit trail.\n */\nexport async function correctStockLocal(\n  barcode: string,\n  onHand: number,\n  expiry: string | null,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.runAsync(\n    `UPDATE stock_levels SET on_hand = ?, nearest_expiry = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE barcode = ?`,\n    [Math.max(0, onHand), expiry, now(), barcode],\n  );\n}\n\n/** Read the current row's nearest_expiry (TEXT column added in migration 2). */\nexport async function getNearestExpiry(barcode: string): Promise<string | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ nearest_expiry: string | null }>(\n    `SELECT nearest_expiry FROM stock_levels WHERE barcode = ?`,\n    [barcode],\n  );\n  return row?.nearest_expiry ?? null;\n}"
}
```

```json
  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport { Calendar, Check } from 'lucide-react-native';\nimport { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';\nimport React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport {\n  KeyboardAvoidingView,\n  Platform,\n  Pressable,\n  ScrollView,\n  StyleSheet,\n  View,\n} from 'react-native';\nimport {\n  AppBar,\n  Button,\n  Card,\n  QtyStepper,\n  radius,\n  spacing,\n  Text,\n} from '../../../design';\nimport { getSession } from '../../../auth/session';\nimport { correctStockLocal, findStock, getNearestExpiry, StockRow } from '../../../db/stock';\nimport { enqueue } from '../../../db/outbox';\nimport { useT } from '../../../i18n';\nimport { useTheme } from '../../../theme';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';\nimport { flushOnce } from '../../../sync/syncService';\nimport { haptic } from '../../../utils/haptics';\nimport { DatePickerModal } from '../components/DatePickerModal';\nimport { useKeyboardHeight } from '../../../hooks/useKeyboardHeight';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'EditStock'>;\n\nfunction formatForDisplay(iso: string): string {\n  try {\n    const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');\n    return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;\n  } catch {\n    return iso;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction toISO(d: Date): string {\n  return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];\n}\n\nexport function EditStockScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const kbHeight = useKeyboardHeight();\n  const { barcode } = route.params;\n\n  const [row, setRow] = useState<StockRow | null>(null);\n  const [originalQty, setOriginalQty] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [originalExpiry, setOriginalExpiry] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [qty, setQty] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [expiryDateObj, setExpiryDateObj] = useState<Date | null>(null);\n  const [noExpiry, setNoExpiry] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [showPicker, setShowPicker] = useState(false);\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    (async () => {\n      const r = await findStock(barcode);\n      const exp = await getNearestExpiry(barcode);\n      if (!r) return;\n      setRow(r);\n      setOriginalQty(r.on_hand);\n      setOriginalExpiry(exp);\n      setQty(r.on_hand);\n      if (exp) {\n        setExpiryDateObj(new Date(exp));\n        setNoExpiry(false);\n      } else {\n        setNoExpiry(true);\n      }\n    })();\n  }, [barcode]);\n\n  const newExpiry = useMemo(() => {\n    if (noExpiry || !expiryDateObj) return null;\n    return toISO(expiryDateObj);\n  }, [expiryDateObj, noExpiry]);\n\n  const changed = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!row) return false;\n    return qty !== originalQty || newExpiry !== originalExpiry;\n  }, [row, qty, originalQty, newExpiry, originalExpiry]);\n\n  const packDisplay = row && row.pack_size != null && row.pack_size !== 1 && row.unit\n    ? `${row.pack_size} ${row.unit}`\n    : row?.unit ?? null;\n\n  const submit = async () => {\n    if (!row || !changed) return;\n    const session = getSession();\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await correctStockLocal(barcode, qty, newExpiry);\n      await enqueue('stock_correction', {\n        id: `crn_${nanoid(12)}`,\n        barcode,\n        product_name: row.name,\n        old_qty: originalQty,\n        new_qty: qty,\n        old_expiry: originalExpiry,\n        new_expiry: newExpiry,\n        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,\n        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,\n        corrected_at: Date.now(),\n      });\n      flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n      haptic.success();\n      navigation.goBack();\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (!row) {\n    return (\n      <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>\n        <AppBar title={t('editStock')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />\n      </View>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <KeyboardAvoidingView\n      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'height' : undefined}\n      style={{ flex: 1 }}\n    >\n      <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>\n        <AppBar title={t('editStock')} subtitle={t('editStockSub')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />\n\n        <ScrollView\n          contentContainerStyle={[styles.content, { paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl + kbHeight }]}\n          keyboardShouldPersistTaps=\"handled\"\n        >\n          <Card tone=\"elevated\" padding=\"xl\">\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n              {t('product').toUpperCase()}\n            </Text>\n            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-end', marginTop: spacing.xs, gap: spacing.md }}>\n              <Text variant=\"headlineSmall\" style={{ flex: 1 }}>{row.name}</Text>\n              {packDisplay ? (\n                <Text variant=\"titleMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>{packDisplay}</Text>\n              ) : null}\n            </View>\n            <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>\n              {t('currentCount')}: {originalQty}{row.unit ? ` ${row.unit}` : ''}\n            </Text>\n          </Card>\n\n          <View style={styles.qtySection}>\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>\n              {t('newCount').toUpperCase()}\n            </Text>\n            <QtyStepper value={qty} onChange={setQty} min={0} />\n          </View>\n\n          <View style={styles.expirySection}>\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n              {t('expiryDate').toUpperCase()}\n            </Text>\n\n            {!noExpiry && (\n              <>\n                <Pressable\n                  onPress={() => setShowPicker(true)}\n                  style={[\n                    styles.expiryInput,\n                    {\n                      backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,\n                      borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n                      flexDirection: 'row',\n                      alignItems: 'center',\n                    },\n                  ]}\n                >\n                  <Calendar size={20} color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} strokeWidth={2} />\n                  <Text\n                    variant=\"bodyLarge\"\n                    color={expiryDateObj ? palette.onSurface : palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n                    style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm, flex: 1, letterSpacing: 1 }}\n                  >\n                    {expiryDateObj ? formatForDisplay(toISO(expiryDateObj)) : t('tapToSelectDate')}\n                  </Text>\n                </Pressable>\n                {showPicker && (\n                  <DatePickerModal\n                    visible={showPicker}\n                    value={expiryDateObj}\n                    onSelect={(d) => { setExpiryDateObj(d); setNoExpiry(false); }}\n                    onDismiss={() => setShowPicker(false)}\n                  />\n                )}\n              </>\n            )}\n\n            <Pressable\n              onPress={() => { setNoExpiry(!noExpiry); haptic.tap(); }}\n              style={styles.noExpiryRow}\n            >\n              <View\n                style={[\n                  styles.checkbox,\n                  {\n                    backgroundColor: noExpiry ? palette.primary : 'transparent',\n                    borderColor: noExpiry ? palette.primary : palette.outline,\n                  },\n                ]}\n              >\n                {noExpiry && <Check size={14} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={3} />}\n              </View>\n              <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n                {t('noExpiry')}\n              </Text>\n            </Pressable>\n          </View>\n        </ScrollView>\n\n        <View style={[styles.footer, { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant }]}>\n          <Button\n            label={changed ? t('saveChanges') : t('noChanges')}\n            onPress={submit}\n            loading={saving}\n            disabled={!changed}\n            size=\"lg\"\n            fullWidth\n            leadingIcon={<Check size={22} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={2.4} />}\n          />\n          <Button\n            label={t('cancel')}\n            variant=\"text\"\n            onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}\n            style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}\n          />\n        </View>\n      </View>\n    </KeyboardAvoidingView>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1 },\n  content: { padding: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.xl },\n  qtySection: { gap: spacing.lg, alignItems: 'stretch' },\n  expirySection: { gap: spacing.sm },\n  expiryInput: {\n    borderRadius: radius.md,\n    borderWidth: 1.5,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,\n    minHeight: 52,\n    fontSize: 18,\n    letterSpacing: 2,\n  },\n  noExpiryRow: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    gap: spacing.sm,\n    paddingVertical: spacing.xs,\n  },\n  checkbox: {\n    width: 22,\n    height: 22,\n    borderRadius: 4,\n    borderWidth: 2,\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n  },\n  footer: {\n    padding: spacing.xl,\n    borderTopWidth: 1,\n  },\n});\n"
}
```

```
1	/**
2	 * Inventory Plugin — Database Layer
3	 *
4	 * SQL surface backing the field-app gate-entry flow. Distinct from the
5	 * legacy top-level `inventory` table, which stores LLM extractions from
6	 * chat messages — these tables persist the structured capture stream
7	 * (products, orders, receipts, stock, issues, reorder requests,
8	 * mismatch flags).
9	 *
10	 * Schema migration runs from `lib/postgres-schema.ts` via
11	 * `ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema()`, the same boot path the visitor
12	 * module uses. This file owns the CRUD only.
13	 */
14	
15	import { getDb } from "../../lib/database";
16	import { ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema } from "../../lib/postgres";
17	
18	export type InventoryProduct = {
19	  barcode: string;
20	  site_id: string;
21	  name: string;
22	  category: string | null;
23	  unit: string | null;
24	  pack_size: number | null;
25	  registered_by: string | null;
26	  created_at: string;
27	  updated_at: string;
28	};
29	
30	export type InventoryOrder = {
31	  id: string;
32	  site_id: string;
33	  vendor: string | null;
34	  expected_at: string | null;
35	  arrived_at: string | null;
36	  status: string;
37	  created_at: string;
38	  updated_at: string;
39	};
40	
41	export type InventoryOrderItem = {
42	  id: string;
43	  order_id: string;
44	  barcode: string | null;
45	  product_id: string | null;
46	  product_name: string;
47	  expected_qty: number;
48	  received_qty: number;
49	};
50	
51	export type InventoryStockRow = {
52	  barcode: string;
53	  site_id: string;
54	  name: string;
55	  category: string | null;
56	  unit: string | null;
57	  on_hand: number;
58	  threshold: number;
59	  updated_at: string;
60	  pack_size: number | null;
61	};
62	
63	export type InventoryReceipt = {
64	  id: string;
65	  site_id: string;
66	  order_id: string | null;
67	  order_item_id: string | null;
68	  barcode: string;
69	  product_name: string;
70	  qty: number;
71	  flagged: boolean;
72	  device_id: string | null;
73	  guard_id: string | null;
74	  scanned_at: string;
75	};
76	
77	export async function initInventorySchema(): Promise<void> {
78	  await ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema();
79	}
80	
81	// ─── Products ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
82	
83	export async function upsertProduct(input: {
84	  siteId: string;
85	  barcode: string;
86	  name: string;
87	  category?: string | null;
88	  unit?: string | null;
89	  packSize?: number | null;
90	  registeredBy?: string | null;
91	}): Promise<void> {
92	  const db = await getDb();
93	  await db.execute({
94	    sql: `
95	      INSERT INTO inventory_products (barcode, site_id, name, category, unit, pack_size, registered_by)
96	      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
97	      ON CONFLICT (site_id, barcode) DO UPDATE SET
98	        name = EXCLUDED.name,
99	        category = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.category, inventory_products.category),
100	        unit = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.unit, inventory_products.unit),
101	        pack_size = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.pack_size, inventory_products.pack_size),
102	        updated_at = now()
103	    `,
104	    args: [
105	      input.barcode,
106	      input.siteId,
107	      input.name,
108	      input.category ?? null,
109	      input.unit ?? null,
110	      input.packSize ?? null,
111	      input.registeredBy ?? null,
112	    ],
113	  });
114	}

182	// ─── Receipts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
183	
184	/**
185	 * Idempotent on `id`. Field-app outbox retries land here without
186	 * double-counting because we ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING and report whether
187	 * the row was actually inserted.
188	 */
189	export async function insertReceipt(input: {
190	  id: string;
191	  siteId: string;
192	  orderId?: string | null;
193	  orderItemId?: string | null;
194	  productId?: string | null;
195	  barcode: string;
196	  productName: string;
197	  qty: number;
198	  expiryDate?: string | null;
199	  flagged?: boolean;
200	  deviceId?: string | null;
201	  guardId?: string | null;
202	  scannedAt: string;
203	}): Promise<{ inserted: boolean }> {
204	  const db = await getDb();
205	  const result = await db.execute({
206	    sql: `
207	      INSERT INTO inventory_receipts (
208	        id, site_id, order_id, order_item_id, product_id, barcode, product_name,
209	        qty, expiry_date, flagged, device_id, guard_id, scanned_at
210	      )
211	      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
212	      ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING
213	    `,
214	    args: [
215	      input.id,
216	      input.siteId,
217	      input.orderId ?? null,
218	      input.orderItemId ?? null,
219	      input.productId ?? null,
220	      input.barcode,
221	      input.productName,
222	      input.qty,
223	      input.expiryDate ?? null,
224	      input.flagged ?? false,
225	      input.deviceId ?? null,
226	      input.guardId ?? null,
227	      input.scannedAt,
228	    ],
229	  });
230	  return { inserted: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };
231	}
232	
233	export type ComplianceLogRow = {
234	  scanned_at: string;
235	  date: string;
236	  vendor: string | null;
237	  bill_number: string | null;
238	  product_id: string | null;
239	  product_name: string;
240	  bill_item_name: string | null;
241	  category: string | null;
242	  hsn_code: string | null;
243	  unit: string | null;
244	  pack_size: number | null;
245	  qty_received: number;
246	  expected_qty: number | null;
247	  unit_rate: number | null;
248	  line_value: number | null;
249	  expiry_date: string | null;
250	  received_by: string | null;
251	  status: 'OK' | 'Flagged';
252	};
253	
254	export async function getComplianceLogForReceipt(
255	  siteId: string,
256	  receiptId: string,
257	): Promise<ComplianceLogRow | null> {
258	  const db = await getDb();
259	  const result = await db.execute({
260	    sql: `
261	      SELECT
262	        r.scanned_at,
263	        to_char(r.scanned_at, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS date,
264	        io.vendor AS vendor,
265	        io.bill_number AS bill_number,
266	        COALESCE(r.product_id, oi.product_id) AS product_id,
267	        COALESCE(cp.canonical_name, r.product_name) AS product_name,
268	        oi.bill_item_name AS bill_item_name,
269	        cp.category AS category,
270	        COALESCE(cp.hsn_code, oi.hsn_code) AS hsn_code,
271	        cp.unit AS unit,
272	        cp.pack_size AS pack_size,
273	        r.qty AS qty_received,
274	        oi.expected_qty AS expected_qty,
275	        oi.unit_rate AS unit_rate,
276	        (r.qty * COALESCE(oi.unit_rate, 0)) AS line_value,
277	        r.expiry_date AS expiry_date,
278	        COALESCE(g.name, r.guard_id) AS received_by,
279	        -- Flagged = the guard explicitly raised a mismatch flag at the
280	        -- gate (qty mismatch, bad expiry). Pure qty vs expected was
281	        -- misleading for partial deliveries.
282	        -- (Historical note: this comment used to contain an apostrophe
283	        -- which broke the driver placeholder normalizer; see comments
284	        -- in lib/database.ts replaceQuestionPlaceholders for details.)
285	        CASE WHEN r.flagged THEN 'Flagged' ELSE 'OK' END AS status
286	      FROM inventory_receipts r
287	      LEFT JOIN inventory_order_items oi ON oi.id = r.order_item_id
288	      LEFT JOIN inventory_orders io ON io.id = r.order_id
289	      LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = COALESCE(r.product_id, oi.product_id)
290	      LEFT JOIN guards g ON g.id = r.guard_id
291	      WHERE r.id = ? AND r.site_id = ?
292	      LIMIT 1
293	    `,
294	    args: [receiptId, siteId],
295	  });
296	  const row = (result.rows[0] as unknown as ComplianceLogRow | undefined) ?? null;
297	  return row;
298	}
299	
300	export async function listComplianceLog(
301	  siteId: string,
302	  fromDate?: string | null,
303	  toDate?: string | null,
304	): Promise<ComplianceLogRow[]> {
305	  const db = await getDb();
306	  const conds: string[] = ['r.site_id = ?'];
307	  const args: (string | number | null)[] = [siteId];
308	  if (fromDate) { conds.push('r.scanned_at >= ?'); args.push(fromDate); }
309	  if (toDate) { conds.push('r.scanned_at <= ?'); args.push(toDate); }
310	  const result = await db.execute({
311	    sql: `
312	      SELECT
313	        r.scanned_at,
314	        to_char(r.scanned_at, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS date,
315	        io.vendor AS vendor,
316	        io.bill_number AS bill_number,
317	        COALESCE(r.product_id, oi.product_id) AS product_id,
318	        COALESCE(cp.canonical_name, r.product_name) AS product_name,
319	        oi.bill_item_name AS bill_item_name,
320	        cp.category AS category,
321	        COALESCE(cp.hsn_code, oi.hsn_code) AS hsn_code,
322	        cp.unit AS unit,
323	        cp.pack_size AS pack_size,
324	        r.qty AS qty_received,
325	        oi.expected_qty AS expected_qty,
326	        oi.unit_rate AS unit_rate,
327	        (r.qty * COALESCE(oi.unit_rate, 0)) AS line_value,
328	        r.expiry_date AS expiry_date,
329	        COALESCE(g.name, r.guard_id) AS received_by,
330	        -- Flagged = guard explicitly raised a mismatch flag at the
331	        -- gate (qty mismatch, bad expiry, etc.). Pure qty vs expected
332	        -- was misleading for partial deliveries.
333	        --   IMPORTANT: no apostrophes in SQL comments here (see notes in
334	        --   getComplianceLogForReceipt above for the placeholder bug).
335	        CASE WHEN r.flagged THEN 'Flagged' ELSE 'OK' END AS status
336	      FROM inventory_receipts r
337	      LEFT JOIN inventory_order_items oi ON oi.id = r.order_item_id
338	      LEFT JOIN inventory_orders io ON io.id = r.order_id
339	      LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = COALESCE(r.product_id, oi.product_id)
340	      LEFT JOIN guards g ON g.id = r.guard_id
341	      WHERE ${conds.join(' AND ')}
342	      ORDER BY r.scanned_at DESC
343	    `,
344	    args,
345	  });
346	  return result.rows as unknown as ComplianceLogRow[];
347	}
348	
349	// ─── Stock ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
350	
351	// Pack size lives on canonical_products (or the older inventory_products
352	// fallback) — not on inventory_stock. Join so the field-app can render
353	// "{on_hand} × {pack_size} {unit}" instead of just "{on_hand} {unit}".
354	const STOCK_SELECT = `
355	  s.barcode, s.site_id, s.name, s.category, s.unit, s.on_hand, s.threshold, s.updated_at,
356	  COALESCE(cp.pack_size, ip.pack_size) AS pack_size
357	`;
358	const STOCK_JOINS = `
359	  FROM inventory_stock s
360	  LEFT JOIN product_barcodes pb ON pb.site_id = s.site_id AND pb.barcode = s.barcode
361	  LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = pb.product_id
362	  LEFT JOIN inventory_products ip ON ip.site_id = s.site_id AND ip.barcode = s.barcode
363	`;
364	
365	export async function listStock(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockRow[]> {
366	  const db = await getDb();
367	  const result = await db.execute({
368	    sql: `SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT} ${STOCK_JOINS} WHERE s.site_id = ? ORDER BY s.name`,
369	    args: [siteId],
370	  });
371	  return result.rows as InventoryStockRow[];
372	}
373	
374	export async function listLowOrOut(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockRow[]> {
375	  const db = await getDb();
376	  const result = await db.execute({
377	    sql: `
378	      SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT}
379	      ${STOCK_JOINS}
380	      WHERE s.site_id = ? AND s.on_hand <= s.threshold
381	      ORDER BY s.on_hand ASC, s.name ASC
382	    `,
383	    args: [siteId],
384	  });
385	  return result.rows as InventoryStockRow[];
386	}

391	): Promise<InventoryStockRow | null> {

399	
400	export async function upsertStock(input: {
401	  siteId: string;
402	  barcode: string;
403	  name: string;
404	  category?: string | null;
405	  unit?: string | null;
406	  onHand?: number;
407	  threshold?: number;
408	}): Promise<void> {
409	  const db = await getDb();
410	  await db.execute({
411	    sql: `
412	      INSERT INTO inventory_stock (
413	        barcode, site_id, name, category, unit, on_hand, threshold
414	      )
415	      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
416	      ON CONFLICT (site_id, barcode) DO UPDATE SET
417	        name = EXCLUDED.name,
418	        category = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.category, inventory_stock.category),
419	        unit = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.unit, inventory_stock.unit),
420	        updated_at = now()
421	    `,
422	    args: [
423	      input.barcode,
424	      input.siteId,
425	      input.name,
426	      input.category ?? null,
427	      input.unit ?? null,
428	      input.onHand ?? 0,
429	      input.threshold ?? 0,
430	    ],
431	  });
432	}
433	
434	/**
435	 * Apply a quantity delta to the on-hand level. Creates the row from the
436	 * receipt's product info if it doesn't yet exist, so the field-app's
437	 * Stock screen surfaces new SKUs as soon as they're scanned.
438	 */
439	export async function adjustOnHand(input: {
440	  siteId: string;
441	  barcode: string;
442	  delta: number;
443	  fallbackName?: string;
444	  fallbackCategory?: string | null;
445	  fallbackUnit?: string | null;
446	}): Promise<void> {
447	  const db = await getDb();
448	  // The delta is passed twice: once clamped for the INSERT path (a brand
449	  // new row created by a negative-delta issue starts at 0, never below)
450	  // and once raw for the UPDATE path (`EXCLUDED.on_hand` would be the
451	  // clamped value, which would silently swallow negative deltas).
452	  await db.execute({
453	    sql: `
454	      INSERT INTO inventory_stock (barcode, site_id, name, category, unit, on_hand, threshold)
455	      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, GREATEST(?, 0), 0)
456	      ON CONFLICT (site_id, barcode) DO UPDATE SET
457	        on_hand = GREATEST(inventory_stock.on_hand + ?, 0),
458	        updated_at = now()
459	    `,
460	    args: [
461	      input.barcode,
462	      input.siteId,
463	      input.fallbackName ?? "Unknown",
464	      input.fallbackCategory ?? null,
465	      input.fallbackUnit ?? null,
466	      input.delta,
467	      input.delta,
468	    ],
469	  });
470	}

633	// ─── Canonical Products ──────────────────────────────────────────
634	
635	export type CanonicalProduct = {
636	  product_id: string;
637	  site_id: string;
638	  canonical_name: string;
639	  category: string | null;
640	  hsn_code: string | null;
641	  unit: string;
642	  pack_size: number;
643	  created_by: string | null;
644	  created_at: string;
645	  updated_at: string;
646	  /** True if any barcode is already mapped to this product. Populated by
647	   *  list queries; the field-app's register-new-barcode flow uses this to
648	   *  hide products that are already taken (one barcode per product, for
649	   *  now). Single-row lookups leave it undefined. */
650	  has_barcode?: boolean;
651	};
652	
653	export async function upsertCanonicalProduct(input: {
654	  productId: string;
655	  siteId: string;
656	  canonicalName: string;
657	  category?: string | null;
658	  hsnCode?: string | null;
659	  unit: string;
660	  packSize?: number;
661	  createdBy?: string | null;
662	}): Promise<void> {
663	  const db = await getDb();
664	  await db.execute({
665	    sql: `
666	      INSERT INTO canonical_products (product_id, site_id, canonical_name, category, hsn_code, unit, pack_size, created_by)
667	      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
668	      ON CONFLICT (product_id) DO UPDATE SET
669	        canonical_name = EXCLUDED.canonical_name,
670	        category = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.category, canonical_products.category),
671	        hsn_code = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.hsn_code, canonical_products.hsn_code),
672	        unit = EXCLUDED.unit,
673	        pack_size = EXCLUDED.pack_size,
674	        updated_at = now()
675	    `,
676	    args: [
677	      input.productId,
678	      input.siteId,
679	      input.canonicalName,
680	      input.category ?? null,
681	      input.hsnCode ?? null,
682	      input.unit,
683	      input.packSize ?? 1,
684	      input.createdBy ?? null,
685	    ],
686	  });
687	}
688	
689	/** Direct UPDATE on canonical_products. Unlike upsertCanonicalProduct
690	 *  (which COALESCEs nullable fields into the existing row), passing
691	 *  `null` here actually clears category / hsn_code. Undefined fields
692	 *  are left untouched. Use this from the admin Catalog editor where
693	 *  blanking a field must persist. */
694	export async function updateCanonicalProduct(input: {
695	  productId: string;
696	  canonicalName?: string;
697	  category?: string | null;
698	  hsnCode?: string | null;
699	  unit?: string;
700	  packSize?: number;
701	}): Promise<{ updated: boolean }> {
702	  const db = await getDb();
703	  const sets: string[] = [];
704	  const args: (string | number | null)[] = [];
705	  if (input.canonicalName !== undefined) {
706	    sets.push(`canonical_name = ?`);
707	    args.push(input.canonicalName);
708	  }
709	  if (input.category !== undefined) {
710	    sets.push(`category = ?`);
711	    args.push(input.category);
712	  }
713	  if (input.hsnCode !== undefined) {
714	    sets.push(`hsn_code = ?`);
715	    args.push(input.hsnCode);
716	  }
717	  if (input.unit !== undefined) {
718	    sets.push(`unit = ?`);
719	    args.push(input.unit);
720	  }
721	  if (input.packSize !== undefined) {
722	    sets.push(`pack_size = ?`);
723	    args.push(input.packSize);
724	  }
725	  if (sets.length === 0) return { updated: false };
726	  sets.push(`updated_at = now()`);
727	  args.push(input.productId);
728	  const result = await db.execute({
729	    sql: `UPDATE canonical_products SET ${sets.join(", ")} WHERE product_id = ?`,
730	    args,
731	  });
732	  return { updated: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };
733	}
734

741	    args: [productId],
742	  });

758	// `has_barcode` is computed from product_barcodes so the field-app's
759	// register-new-barcode flow can hide products that are already taken
760	// (one-barcode-per-product rule, for now).
761	const CANONICAL_PRODUCT_COLUMNS = `
762	  cp.*,
763	  EXISTS (
764	    SELECT 1 FROM product_barcodes pb
765	    WHERE pb.product_id = cp.product_id AND pb.site_id = cp.site_id
766	  ) AS has_barcode
767	`;

775	    args: [siteId],
776	  });

792	// ─── Product Aliases ─────────────────────────────────────────────
793	
794	export type ProductAlias = {
795	  id: string;
796	  site_id: string;
797	  raw_name: string;
798	  product_id: string;
799	  hsn_code: string | null;
800	  vendor_name: string | null;
801	  confidence: number;
802	  source: string;
803	  created_at: string;
804	};
805	
806	export async function findProductAlias(
807	  siteId: string,
808	  rawName: string,
809	  hsnCode?: string | null,
810	): Promise<ProductAlias | null> {
811	  const db = await getDb();
812	  // Try HSN-specific alias first, then generic (no HSN)
813	  if (hsnCode) {
814	    const specific = await db.execute({
815	      sql: `SELECT * FROM product_aliases WHERE site_id = ? AND lower(raw_name) = lower(?) AND hsn_code = ? LIMIT 1`,
816	      args: [siteId, rawName.trim(), hsnCode],
817	    });
818	    if (specific.rows[0]) return specific.rows[0] as ProductAlias;
819	  }
820	  const generic = await db.execute({
821	    sql: `SELECT * FROM product_aliases WHERE site_id = ? AND lower(raw_name) = lower(?) AND (hsn_code IS NULL OR hsn_code = '') LIMIT 1`,
822	    args: [siteId, rawName.trim()],
823	  });
824	  return (generic.rows[0] as ProductAlias | undefined) ?? null;
825	}
826	
827	export async function saveProductAlias(input: {
828	  id: string;
829	  siteId: string;
830	  rawName: string;
831	  productId: string;
832	  hsnCode?: string | null;
833	  vendorName?: string | null;
834	  confidence?: number;
835	  source?: string;
836	}): Promise<void> {
837	  const db = await getDb();
838	  await db.execute({
839	    sql: `
840	      INSERT INTO product_aliases (id, site_id, raw_name, product_id, hsn_code, vendor_name, confidence, source)
841	      VALUES (?, ?, lower(?), ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
842	      ON CONFLICT (site_id, raw_name, COALESCE(hsn_code, '')) DO UPDATE SET
843	        product_id = EXCLUDED.product_id,
844	        vendor_name = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.vendor_name, product_aliases.vendor_name),
845	        confidence = EXCLUDED.confidence,
846	        source = EXCLUDED.source
847	    `,
848	    args: [
849	      input.id,
850	      input.siteId,
851	      input.rawName.trim(),
852	      input.productId,
853	      input.hsnCode ?? null,
854	      input.vendorName ?? null,
855	      input.confidence ?? 1.0,
856	      input.source ?? "admin_manual",
857	    ],
858	  });
859	}
860	
861	export async function listProductAliases(
862	  productId: string,
863	): Promise<ProductAlias[]> {
864	  const db = await getDb();
865	  const result = await db.execute({
866	    sql: `SELECT * FROM product_aliases WHERE product_id = ? ORDER BY raw_name`,
867	    args: [productId],
868	  });
869	  return result.rows as ProductAlias[];
870	}
871	
872	/** Update raw_name / hsn_code on an existing alias. Undefined fields are
873	 *  left untouched; `hsn_code: null` explicitly clears the HSN. The
874	 *  (site_id, raw_name, COALESCE(hsn_code, '')) unique index can collide
875	 *  if the new combination already exists for the site — let Postgres
876	 *  raise 23505 and the route surface a clean error. */
877	export async function updateProductAlias(input: {
878	  id: string;
879	  rawName?: string;
880	  hsnCode?: string | null;
881	}): Promise<{ updated: boolean }> {
882	  const db = await getDb();
883	  const sets: string[] = [];
884	  const args: (string | null)[] = [];
885	  if (input.rawName !== undefined) {
886	    sets.push(`raw_name = lower(?)`);
887	    args.push(input.rawName.trim());
888	  }
889	  if (input.hsnCode !== undefined) {
890	    sets.push(`hsn_code = ?`);
891	    args.push(input.hsnCode);
892	  }
893	  if (sets.length === 0) return { updated: false };
894	  args.push(input.id);
895	  const result = await db.execute({
896	    sql: `UPDATE product_aliases SET ${sets.join(", ")} WHERE id = ?`,
897	    args,
898	  });
899	  return { updated: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };
900	}
901	
902	/** Remove a single alias by its primary key. Used by the dashboard's
903	 *  per-product alias expander when an admin wants to drop a poisoned
904	 *  or wrong mapping without nuking every alias on the site. */
905	export async function deleteProductAlias(aliasId: string): Promise<{ deleted: boolean }> {
906	  const db = await getDb();
907	  const result = await db.execute({
908	    sql: `DELETE FROM product_aliases WHERE id = ?`,
909	    args: [aliasId],
910	  });
911	  return { deleted: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };
912	}

1089	export async function createOrderFromBill(input: {
1090	  id: string;
1091	  siteId: string;
1092	  vendor?: string | null;
1093	  billNumber?: string | null;
1094	  billDate?: string | null;
1095	  billPhotoUrl?: string | null;
1096	  grandTotal?: number | null;
1097	  expectedAt?: string | null;
1098	}): Promise<void> {
1099	  const db = await getDb();
1100	  await db.execute({
1101	    sql: `
1102	      INSERT INTO inventory_orders (id, site_id, vendor, bill_number, bill_date, bill_photo_url, grand_total, expected_at, status)
1103	      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'open')
1104	      ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET
1105	        vendor = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.vendor, inventory_orders.vendor),
1106	        bill_number = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.bill_number, inventory_orders.bill_number),
1107	        bill_date = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.bill_date, inventory_orders.bill_date),
1108	        bill_photo_url = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.bill_photo_url, inventory_orders.bill_photo_url),
1109	        grand_total = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.grand_total, inventory_orders.grand_total),
1110	        updated_at = now()
1111	    `,
1112	    args: [
1113	      input.id,
1114	      input.siteId,
1115	      input.vendor ?? null,
1116	      input.billNumber ?? null,
1117	      input.billDate ?? null,
1118	      input.billPhotoUrl ?? null,
1119	      input.grandTotal ?? null,
1120	      input.expectedAt ?? null,
1121	    ],
1122	  });
1123	}
1124

1144	      )
1145	      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
1146	      ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING

1181	
1182	export type CatalogSheetRow = {
1183	  canonical_name: string;
1184	  category: string | null;
1185	  unit: string;
1186	  pack_size: number;
1187	  last_price: number | null;
1188	  barcodes: string | null; // comma-separated
1189	  hsn_code: string | null;
1190	};
1191	
1192	export type StockSheetRow = {
1193	  canonical_name: string;
1194	  category: string | null;
1195	  unit: string;
1196	  pack_size: number;
1197	  last_price: number | null;
1198	  barcodes: string | null; // comma-separated
1199	  on_hand: number;
1200	  expiry_dates: string | null; // comma-separated YYYY-MM-DD, ascending
1201	};
1202	
1203	/**
1204	 * Snapshot of the catalog for the Google Sheet "Catalog" tab. Joins
1205	 * canonical_products → product_barcodes (concatenated) and pulls the
1206	 * most recent unit_rate from inventory_order_items as a price proxy
1207	 * (canonical products don't carry a price column today).
1208	 */
1209	export async function listCatalogForSheet(siteId: string): Promise<CatalogSheetRow[]> {
1210	  const db = await getDb();
1211	  const result = await db.execute({
1212	    sql: `
1213	      SELECT
1214	        cp.canonical_name,
1215	        cp.category,
1216	        cp.unit,
1217	        cp.pack_size,
1218	        cp.hsn_code,
1219	        (SELECT string_agg(pb.barcode, ', ')
1220	           FROM product_barcodes pb
1221	          WHERE pb.product_id = cp.product_id) AS barcodes,
1222	        (SELECT oi.unit_rate
1223	           FROM inventory_order_items oi
1224	           JOIN inventory_orders o ON o.id = oi.order_id
1225	          WHERE oi.product_id = cp.product_id AND o.site_id = ?
1226	          ORDER BY o.created_at DESC
1227	          LIMIT 1) AS last_price
1228	      FROM canonical_products cp
1229	      WHERE cp.site_id = ?
1230	      ORDER BY cp.canonical_name
1231	    `,
1232	    args: [siteId, siteId],
1233	  });
1234	  return result.rows as unknown as CatalogSheetRow[];
1235	}
1236	
1237	/**
1238	 * Snapshot of current stock for the Google Sheet "Stock" tab and the
1239	 * dashboard Stock view. One row per canonical product that has an
1240	 * inventory_stock entry; pulls the most recent unit_rate as price
1241	 * (same source as Catalog) and aggregates distinct *future* expiry
1242	 * dates from receipts (already-passed expiries are dropped — the
1243	 * item is logically consumed/gone).
1244	 */
1245	export async function listStockForSheet(siteId: string): Promise<StockSheetRow[]> {
1246	  const db = await getDb();
1247	  const result = await db.execute({
1248	    sql: `
1249	      SELECT
1250	        COALESCE(cp.canonical_name, s.name) AS canonical_name,
1251	        COALESCE(cp.category, s.category)   AS category,
1252	        COALESCE(cp.unit, s.unit, 'pcs')    AS unit,
1253	        COALESCE(cp.pack_size, 1)           AS pack_size,
1254	        (SELECT string_agg(pb2.barcode, ', ')
1255	           FROM product_barcodes pb2
1256	          WHERE pb2.site_id = s.site_id
1257	            AND pb2.product_id = cp.product_id) AS barcodes,
1258	        (SELECT oi.unit_rate
1259	           FROM inventory_order_items oi
1260	           JOIN inventory_orders o ON o.id = oi.order_id
1261	          WHERE oi.product_id = cp.product_id AND o.site_id = ?
1262	          ORDER BY o.created_at DESC
1263	          LIMIT 1) AS last_price,
1264	        s.on_hand AS on_hand,
1265	        (SELECT string_agg(DISTINCT to_char(r.expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'), ', ' ORDER BY to_char(r.expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'))
1266	           FROM inventory_receipts r
1267	          WHERE r.site_id = s.site_id
1268	            AND r.expiry_date IS NOT NULL
1269	            AND r.expiry_date >= CURRENT_DATE
1270	            AND (r.product_id = cp.product_id OR r.barcode = s.barcode)) AS expiry_dates
1271	      FROM inventory_stock s
1272	      LEFT JOIN product_barcodes pb ON pb.site_id = s.site_id AND pb.barcode = s.barcode
1273	      LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = pb.product_id
1274	      WHERE s.site_id = ?
1275	      ORDER BY canonical_name
1276	    `,
1277	    args: [siteId, siteId],
1278	  });
1279	  return result.rows as unknown as StockSheetRow[];
1280	}
1281	
1282	/**
1283	 * Drop every learned alias for a site. Useful when the alias cache has
1284	 * been poisoned by a prior run that auto-saved wrong mappings.
1285	 */
1286	export async function clearProductAliases(siteId: string): Promise<{ deleted: number }> {
1287	  const db = await getDb();
1288	  const result = await db.execute({
1289	    sql: `DELETE FROM product_aliases WHERE site_id = ?`,
1290	    args: [siteId],
1291	  });
1292	  return { deleted: Number(result.rowsAffected ?? 0) };
1293	}
1294	
1295	// ─── Orders for dashboard (full listing with items) ─────────────
1296	
1297	export type OrderWithItems = {
1298	  id: string;
1299	  site_id: string;
1300	  vendor: string | null;
1301	  bill_number: string | null;
1302	  bill_date: string | null;
1303	  grand_total: number | null;
1304	  status: string;
1305	  expected_at: string | null;
1306	  arrived_at: string | null;
1307	  created_at: string;
1308	  items: Array<{
1309	    id: string;
1310	    order_id: string;
1311	    product_id: string | null;
1312	    product_name: string;
1313	    bill_item_name: string | null;
1314	    hsn_code: string | null;
1315	    expected_qty: number;
1316	    received_qty: number;
1317	    unit_rate: number | null;
1318	    line_value: number | null;
1319	    unit: string | null;
1320	    pack_size: number | null;
1321	  }>;
1322	};
1323	
1324	export async function listOrdersWithItems(siteId: string): Promise<OrderWithItems[]> {
1325	  const db = await getDb();
1326	  const ordersRes = await db.execute({
1327	    // bill_date is a DATE column — pg returns it as a JS Date which JSON-
1328	    // serialises as a full ISO timestamp ("2026-06-03T00:00:00.000Z").
1329	    // <input type="date"> silently rejects that, so the dashboard would
1330	    // show an empty input on refresh even though the DB had the value.
1331	    // Coerce to plain "YYYY-MM-DD" here.
1332	    sql: `
1333	      SELECT id, site_id, vendor, bill_number,
1334	             to_char(bill_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS bill_date,
1335	             grand_total, status,
1336	             expected_at, arrived_at, created_at
1337	      FROM inventory_orders
1338	      WHERE site_id = ?
1339	      ORDER BY created_at DESC
1340	    `,
1341	    args: [siteId],
1342	  });
1343	  const orders = ordersRes.rows as Array<Omit<OrderWithItems, "items">>;
1344	  if (orders.length === 0) return [];
1345	
1346	  const itemsRes = await db.execute({
1347	    sql: `
1348	      SELECT oi.id, oi.order_id, oi.product_id, oi.product_name, oi.bill_item_name,
1349	             oi.hsn_code, oi.expected_qty, oi.received_qty, oi.unit_rate, oi.line_value,
1350	             cp.unit AS unit, cp.pack_size AS pack_size
1351	      FROM inventory_order_items oi
1352	      LEFT JOIN canonical_products cp ON cp.product_id = oi.product_id
1353	      WHERE oi.order_id IN (${orders.map(() => "?").join(",")})
1354	    `,
1355	    args: orders.map((o) => o.id),
1356	  });
1357	  const itemsByOrder = new Map<string, OrderWithItems["items"]>();
1358	  for (const row of itemsRes.rows as OrderWithItems["items"]) {
1359	    const list = itemsByOrder.get(row.order_id) ?? [];
1360	    list.push(row);
1361	    itemsByOrder.set(row.order_id, list);
1362	  }
1363	  return orders.map((o) => ({ ...o, items: itemsByOrder.get(o.id) ?? [] }));
1364	}
1365	
1366	export async function deleteOrder(siteId: string, orderId: string): Promise<{ deleted: boolean }> {
1367	  const db = await getDb();
1368	  // Detach any receipts pointing at this order so the audit trail
1369	  // survives but the FK doesn't block the delete. This works whether
1370	  // or not the migration to ON DELETE SET NULL has run.
1371	  await db.execute({
1372	    sql: `UPDATE inventory_receipts SET order_item_id = NULL
1373	          WHERE order_item_id IN (
1374	            SELECT id FROM inventory_order_items WHERE order_id = ?
1375	          )`,
1376	    args: [orderId],
1377	  });
1378	  await db.execute({
1379	    sql: `UPDATE inventory_receipts SET order_id = NULL WHERE order_id = ?`,
1380	    args: [orderId],
1381	  });
1382	  await db.execute({
1383	    sql: `UPDATE inventory_mismatch_flags SET order_item_id = NULL
1384	          WHERE order_item_id IN (
1385	            SELECT id FROM inventory_order_items WHERE order_id = ?
1386	          )`,
1387	    args: [orderId],
1388	  });
1389	  // ON DELETE CASCADE on inventory_order_items removes the items.
1390	  const result = await db.execute({
1391	    sql: `DELETE FROM inventory_orders WHERE id = ? AND site_id = ?`,
1392	    args: [orderId, siteId],
1393	  });
1394	  return { deleted: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };
1395	}
1396	
1397	export async function updateOrderMeta(input: {
1398	  orderId: string;
1399	  siteId: string;
1400	  vendor?: string | null;
1401	  billNumber?: string | null;
1402	  billDate?: string | null;
1403	}): Promise<void> {
1404	  const db = await getDb();
1405	  await db.execute({
1406	    sql: `
1407	      UPDATE inventory_orders
1408	      SET vendor = COALESCE(?, vendor),
1409	          bill_number = COALESCE(?, bill_number),
1410	          bill_date = COALESCE(?, bill_date),
1411	          updated_at = now()
1412	      WHERE id = ? AND site_id = ?
1413	    `,
1414	    args: [
1415	      input.vendor ?? null,
1416	      input.billNumber ?? null,
1417	      input.billDate ?? null,
1418	      input.orderId,
1419	      input.siteId,
1420	    ],
1421	  });
1422	}
1423	
1424	export async function updateOrderItem(input: {
1425	  itemId: string;
1426	  productId?: string | null;
1427	  productName?: string | null;
1428	  hsnCode?: string | null;
1429	  expectedQty?: number | null;
1430	  unitRate?: number | null;
1431	}): Promise<void> {
1432	  const db = await getDb();
1433	  await db.execute({
1434	    sql: `
1435	      UPDATE inventory_order_items
1436	      SET product_id = COALESCE(?, product_id),
1437	          product_name = COALESCE(?, product_name),
1438	          hsn_code = COALESCE(?, hsn_code),
1439	          expected_qty = COALESCE(?, expected_qty),
1440	          unit_rate = COALESCE(?, unit_rate)
1441	      WHERE id = ?
1442	    `,
1443	    args: [
1444	      input.productId ?? null,
1445	      input.productName ?? null,
1446	      input.hsnCode ?? null,
1447	      input.expectedQty ?? null,
1448	      input.unitRate ?? null,
1449	      input.itemId,
1450	    ],
1451	  });
1452	}
1453	
1454	export async function deleteOrderItem(itemId: string): Promise<{ deleted: boolean }> {
1455	  const db = await getDb();
1456	  const result = await db.execute({
1457	    sql: `DELETE FROM inventory_order_items WHERE id = ?`,
1458	    args: [itemId],
1459	  });
1460	  return { deleted: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };
1461	}
1462	
1463	// ─── Site Settings (key-value) ───────────────────────────────────
1464	
1465	// "key" is a SQL reserved-ish word — Postgres errored with "syntax error
1466	// at or near AND" on `WHERE site_id = ? AND key = ?` because the parser
1467	// interpreted the bareword `key` as a reserved token. Always quote it.
1468	export async function getSetting(siteId: string, key: string): Promise<string | null> {
1469	  const db = await getDb();
1470	  const result = await db.execute({
1471	    sql: `SELECT value FROM inventory_settings WHERE site_id = ? AND "key" = ?`,
1472	    args: [siteId, key],
1473	  });
1474	  const row = result.rows[0] as { value: string | null } | undefined;
1475	  return row?.value ?? null;
1476	}
1477	
1478	export async function setSetting(siteId: string, key: string, value: string): Promise<void> {
1479	  const db = await getDb();
1480	  await db.execute({
1481	    sql: `
1482	      INSERT INTO inventory_settings (site_id, "key", value, updated_at)
1483	      VALUES (?, ?, ?, now())
1484	      ON CONFLICT (site_id, "key") DO UPDATE SET
1485	        value = excluded.value,
1486	        updated_at = now()
1487	    `,
1488	    args: [siteId, key, value],
1489	  });
1490	}
1491	
1492	export async function deleteSetting(siteId: string, key: string): Promise<void> {
1493	  const db = await getDb();
1494	  await db.execute({
1495	    sql: `DELETE FROM inventory_settings WHERE site_id = ? AND "key" = ?`,
1496	    args: [siteId, key],
1497	  });
1498	}
```

```
1	/**
2	 * Inventory Plugin — HTTP Routes
3	 *
4	 * Field-app facing surface under /api/inventory/*.
5	 *
6	 * Writes (idempotent on `id`):
7	 *   POST /api/inventory/receipts          — gate capture
8	 *   POST /api/inventory/products          — catalog upsert (register barcode)
9	 *   POST /api/inventory/issues            — consumption
10	 *   POST /api/inventory/reorder-requests  — low-stock reorder ask

13	 * Reads (guard-authenticated):
```

```
323	/**
324	 * Append a single row to a specific spreadsheet + tab using a caller-
325	 * supplied authenticated `sheets` client. The client must already be
326	 * authorized for `spreadsheetId` (e.g. built from an OAuth refresh
327	 * token via buildOAuthClientWithRefreshToken).
328	 */
329	export async function appendInventoryRow(
330	  sheets: sheets_v4.Sheets,
331	  spreadsheetId: string,
332	  tabName: string,
333	  headers: string[],
334	  rowData: string[],
335	): Promise<void> {
336	  await ensureSheet(sheets, spreadsheetId, tabName);
337	
338	  try {
339	    const headerCheck = await sheets.spreadsheets.values.get({
340	      spreadsheetId,
341	      range: formatSheetRange(tabName, 'A1:A1'),
342	    });
343	    const hasHeaders = (headerCheck.data.values?.[0]?.length ?? 0) > 0;
344	    if (!hasHeaders) {
345	      await sheets.spreadsheets.values.update({
346	        spreadsheetId,
347	        range: formatSheetRange(tabName, 'A1'),
348	        valueInputOption: 'USER_ENTERED',
349	        requestBody: { values: [headers] },
350	      });
351	    }
352	  } catch {
353	    // non-critical
354	  }
355	
356	  await sheets.spreadsheets.values.append({
357	    spreadsheetId,
358	    range: formatSheetRange(tabName, 'A:A'),
359	    valueInputOption: 'USER_ENTERED',
360	    insertDataOption: 'INSERT_ROWS',
361	    requestBody: { values: [rowData] },
362	  });
363	
364	  // Apply date/time column formats AFTER the append so that the newly
365	  // inserted row (created by INSERT_ROWS) gets the correct format.
366	  await ensureInventoryDateColumnFormats(sheets, spreadsheetId, tabName, headers);
367	}
368	
369	// Sheets' USER_ENTERED parses "2026-06-03" → date serial 46176, then
370	// renders it using whatever number format the column already had. On
371	// older tabs that defaulted to "Number", that produces "46176" instead
372	// of a date — the bug we hit on Ordered/Received.
373	//
374	// This runs AFTER every append (not before) because INSERT_ROWS creates
375	// a brand-new row whose cells start with no format. RepeatCell then
376	// stamps the correct date/time format onto all data rows including the
377	// freshly inserted one. No per-process cache — we always reapply so
378	// that both old and new rows are guaranteed to render correctly.
379	async function ensureInventoryDateColumnFormats(
380	  sheets: sheets_v4.Sheets,
381	  spreadsheetId: string,
382	  tabName: string,
383	  headers: string[],
384	): Promise<void> {
385	
386	  try {
387	    const meta = await sheets.spreadsheets.get({ spreadsheetId });
388	    const sheet = meta.data.sheets?.find((s) => s.properties?.title === tabName);
389	    const sheetId = sheet?.properties?.sheetId;
390	    if (sheetId == null) return;
391	
392	    const dateColumns: string[] = ['Bill Date', 'Date', 'Expiry'];
393	    const timeColumns: string[] = ['Time (IST)'];
394	
395	    const requests: sheets_v4.Schema$Request[] = [];
396	    const addFormat = (col: number, pattern: string, type: 'DATE' | 'TIME') => {
397	      requests.push({
398	        repeatCell: {
399	          range: {
400	            sheetId,
401	            startRowIndex: 1,
402	            startColumnIndex: col,
403	            endColumnIndex: col + 1,
404	          },
405	          cell: { userEnteredFormat: { numberFormat: { type, pattern } } },
406	          fields: 'userEnteredFormat.numberFormat',
407	        },
408	      });
409	    };
410	
411	    for (const name of dateColumns) {
412	      const idx = headers.indexOf(name);
413	      if (idx >= 0) addFormat(idx, 'dd-mmm-yyyy', 'DATE');
414	    }
415	    for (const name of timeColumns) {
416	      const idx = headers.indexOf(name);
417	      if (idx >= 0) addFormat(idx, 'hh:mm:ss', 'TIME');
418	    }
419	
420	    if (requests.length > 0) {
421	      await sheets.spreadsheets.batchUpdate({ spreadsheetId, requestBody: { requests } });
422	    }
423	  } catch {
424	    // Non-critical: a missing format just means the column may still
425	    // render as a serial. We don't want to block the write.
426	  }
427	}
428	
429	/**
430	 * Replace every row in a tab (clear → headers → values). For snapshot
431	 * sheets like Catalog that should reflect the current DB state rather
432	 * than accumulate history. Auto-creates the tab if missing.
433	 */
434	export async function replaceSheetTab(
435	  sheets: sheets_v4.Sheets,
436	  spreadsheetId: string,
437	  tabName: string,
438	  headers: string[],
439	  rows: string[][],
440	): Promise<void> {
441	  await ensureSheet(sheets, spreadsheetId, tabName);
442	  await sheets.spreadsheets.values.clear({
443	    spreadsheetId,
444	    range: formatSheetRange(tabName, 'A:Z'),
445	  });
446	  await sheets.spreadsheets.values.update({
447	    spreadsheetId,
448	    range: formatSheetRange(tabName, 'A1'),
449	    valueInputOption: 'USER_ENTERED',
450	    requestBody: { values: [headers, ...rows] },
451	  });
452	}
453	
454	/**
455	 * Verify the supplied sheets client can read a spreadsheet — used by
456	 * the dashboard "Attach" button to confirm the URL is valid before
457	 * saving the id.
458	 */
459	export async function verifySpreadsheetAccess(
460	  sheets: sheets_v4.Sheets,
461	  spreadsheetId: string,
462	): Promise<{ ok: boolean; title?: string; error?: string }> {
463	  try {
464	    const meta = await sheets.spreadsheets.get({ spreadsheetId });
465	    return { ok: true, title: meta.data.properties?.title ?? undefined };
466	  } catch (err) {
467	    return {
468	      ok: false,
469	      error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
470	    };
471	  }
472	}

475	  Ordered: INVENTORY_ORDERED_HEADERS,
476	  Received: INVENTORY_RECEIVED_HEADERS,
477	  Catalog: INVENTORY_CATALOG_HEADERS,

483	/**
484	 * Build a Telegram deep-link URL from chat ID and message ID.
485	 */
486	function buildTelegramLink(chatId: unknown, msgId: unknown): string {
487	  if (!chatId || !msgId) return '';
488	  const absId = Math.abs(Number(chatId));
489	  const idStr = String(absId);
490	  const cleanId = idStr.startsWith('100') && idStr.length > 10 ? idStr.slice(3) : idStr;
491	  return `https://t.me/c/${cleanId}/${msgId}`;
492	}
493	
494	function formatSheetRange(sheetTitle: string, range: string): string {
495	  const escapedTitle = sheetTitle.replace(/'/g, "''");
496	  return `'${escapedTitle}'!${range}`;
497	}
498	
499	function formatHostResponse(value: unknown): string {
500	  return {
501	    coming: 'Coming',
502	    '5min': '5 min',
503	    not_expecting: 'Not expecting',
504	  }[String(value ?? '')] ?? '';
505	}
506	
507	function colIndexToLetter(index: number): string {
508	  let result = '';
509	  let n = index;
510	  do {
511	    result = String.fromCharCode(65 + (n % 26)) + result;
512	    n = Math.floor(n / 26) - 1;
513	  } while (n >= 0);
514	  return result;
515	}
516	
517	function normalizeHeader(value: unknown): string {
518	  return String(value ?? '')
519	    .trim()
520	    .toLowerCase()
521	    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '_')
522	    .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, '');
523	}
524	
525	function getRowValue(
526	  row: string[],
527	  headerIndex: Map<string, number>,
528	  aliases: string[],
529	): string {
530	  for (const alias of aliases) {
531	    const index = headerIndex.get(alias);
532	    if (index !== undefined) {
533	      return String(row[index] ?? '').trim();
534	    }
535	  }
536	  return '';
537	}
```

```
1963	/**
1964	 * Push the per-product on-hand snapshot to the linked Google Sheet's
1965	 * "Stock" tab, NOW. Most callers should use `syncStockTabToSheet` so
1966	 * bursts of receipts/issues coalesce into one write.
1967	 */
1968	async function syncStockTabToSheetNow(siteId: string): Promise<void> {
1969	  const [spreadsheetId, ownerEmail] = await Promise.all([
1970	    getSetting(siteId, SHEET_ID_KEY),
1971	    getSetting(siteId, SHEET_OWNER_KEY),
1972	  ]);
1973	  if (!spreadsheetId || !ownerEmail) return;
1974	  const sheets = await getAdminSheetsClient(ownerEmail);
1975	  if (!sheets) return;
1976	  const rows = await listStockForSheet(siteId);
1977	  const sheetRows = rows.map((r) => [
1978	    r.canonical_name,
1979	    r.category ?? "",
1980	    r.unit,
1981	    String(r.pack_size),
1982	    r.last_price != null ? String(r.last_price) : "",
1983	    r.barcodes ?? "",
1984	    String(r.on_hand),
1985	    r.expiry_dates ?? "",
1986	  ]);
1987	  await replaceSheetTab(
1988	    sheets,
1989	    spreadsheetId,
1990	    "Stock",
1991	    INVENTORY_SHEET_HEADERS.Stock,
1992	    sheetRows,
1993	  );
1994	}
1995	
1996	// Same debounce pattern as Catalog. Receipts and issues both fire on
1997	// every scan; we coalesce so one Sheets write happens per burst.
1998	const STOCK_SYNC_DEBOUNCE_MS = 15_000;
1999	const pendingStockSync = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();
2000	
2001	function syncStockTabToSheet(siteId: string): Promise<void> {
2002	  const existing = pendingStockSync.get(siteId);
2003	  if (existing) clearTimeout(existing);
2004	  const handle = setTimeout(() => {
2005	    pendingStockSync.delete(siteId);
2006	    void syncStockTabToSheetNow(siteId).catch((err) => {
2007	      logError("[Inventory] debounced stock sync failed", err, {
2008	        scope: "inventory.sheets",
2009	      });
2010	    });
2011	  }, STOCK_SYNC_DEBOUNCE_MS);
2012	  pendingStockSync.set(siteId, handle);
2013	  return Promise.resolve();
2014	}
2015	
2016	async function appendComplianceRow(siteId: string, receiptId: string): Promise<void> {
2017	  // Granular step-by-step logging to pinpoint which call throws. The
2018	  // outer try/catch in the receipts route only logs "Sheet append
2019	  // failed" — it can't tell us whether it was getSetting, the SELECT,
2020	  // drizzle, or the actual Sheets API.
2021	  /* eslint-disable no-console */
2022	  let step: string = "init";
2023	  try {
2024	    step = "getSetting(SHEET_ID_KEY)";
2025	    const spreadsheetId = await getSetting(siteId, SHEET_ID_KEY);
2026	    step = "getSetting(SHEET_OWNER_KEY)";
2027	    const ownerEmail = await getSetting(siteId, SHEET_OWNER_KEY);
2028	    if (!spreadsheetId || !ownerEmail) {
2029	      console.warn(`[Inventory] Received append skipped (no sheet attached): site=${siteId}, receipt=${receiptId}`);
2030	      return;
2031	    }
2032	
2033	    step = "getAdminSheetsClient";
2034	    const sheets = await getAdminSheetsClient(ownerEmail);
2035	    if (!sheets) {
2036	      console.warn(`[Inventory] Received append skipped (owner Google token missing): owner=${ownerEmail}, receipt=${receiptId}`);
2037	      return;
2038	    }
2039	
2040	    step = "getComplianceLogForReceipt";
2041	    const row = await getComplianceLogForReceipt(siteId, receiptId);
2042	    if (!row) {
2043	      console.warn(`[Inventory] Received append skipped (compliance row not found): site=${siteId}, receipt=${receiptId}`);
2044	      return;
2045	    }
2046	
2047	    step = "appendInventoryRow(Received)";
2048	    console.log(`[Inventory] Received append → "${row.product_name}" qty=${row.qty_received} (receipt=${receiptId})`);
2049	    await appendInventoryRow(
2050	      sheets,
2051	      spreadsheetId,
2052	      "Received",
2053	      INVENTORY_SHEET_HEADERS.Received,
2054	      complianceRowToSheetValues(row),
2055	    );
2056	  } catch (err) {
2057	    // Re-throw so the outer catch still logs through logError(); but
2058	    // first emit a step label so we see WHICH call blew up.
2059	    console.error(`[Inventory] Received append failed at step "${step}":`, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
2060	    throw err;
2061	  }
2062	  /* eslint-enable no-console */
2063	}
2064
```
