  "new_string": "// ─── Stock Corrections (Edit Stock audit) ────────────────────────\n\nexport type StockCorrectionRow = {\n  id: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  product_name: string;\n  old_qty: number | null;\n  new_qty: number;\n  old_expiry: string | null;\n  new_expiry: string | null;\n  device_id: string | null;\n  guard_id: string | null;\n  guard_name: string | null;\n  corrected_at: string;\n  created_at: string;\n};\n\nexport async function insertStockCorrection(input: {\n  id: string;\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  productName: string;\n  oldQty: number | null;\n  newQty: number;\n  oldExpiry?: string | null;\n  newExpiry?: string | null;\n  deviceId?: string | null;\n  guardId?: string | null;\n  guardName?: string | null;\n  correctedAt: string;\n}): Promise<{ inserted: boolean }> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_stock_corrections (\n        id, site_id, barcode, product_name, old_qty, new_qty,\n        old_expiry, new_expiry, device_id, guard_id, guard_name, corrected_at\n      )\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n      ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING\n    `,\n    args: [\n      input.id,\n      input.siteId,\n      input.barcode,\n      input.productName,\n      input.oldQty,\n      input.newQty,\n      input.oldExpiry ?? null,\n      input.newExpiry ?? null,\n      input.deviceId ?? null,\n      input.guardId ?? null,\n      input.guardName ?? null,\n      input.correctedAt,\n    ],\n  });\n  return { inserted: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };\n}\n\nexport async function getStockCorrection(\n  siteId: string,\n  id: string,\n): Promise<StockCorrectionRow | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT id, site_id, barcode, product_name, old_qty, new_qty,\n             to_char(old_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS old_expiry,\n             to_char(new_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS new_expiry,\n             device_id, guard_id, guard_name, corrected_at, created_at\n      FROM inventory_stock_corrections\n      WHERE site_id = ? AND id = ?\n      LIMIT 1\n    `,\n    args: [siteId, id],\n  });\n  return (result.rows[0] as StockCorrectionRow | undefined) ?? null;\n}\n\nexport async function listStockCorrections(\n  siteId: string,\n): Promise<StockCorrectionRow[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT id, site_id, barcode, product_name, old_qty, new_qty,\n             to_char(old_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS old_expiry,\n             to_char(new_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS new_expiry,\n             device_id, guard_id, guard_name, corrected_at, created_at\n      FROM inventory_stock_corrections\n      WHERE site_id = ?\n      ORDER BY corrected_at DESC\n    `,\n    args: [siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows as StockCorrectionRow[];\n}\n\n/**\n * Apply a correction directly to inventory_stock. Sets on_hand to the\n * absolute new value (not a delta) so the result on the server matches\n * what the guard saw and confirmed in the field-app.\n */\nexport async function setStockOnHand(input: {\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  onHand: number;\n  fallbackName?: string;\n  fallbackCategory?: string | null;\n  fallbackUnit?: string | null;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_stock (barcode, site_id, name, category, unit, on_hand, threshold)\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, GREATEST(?, 0), 0)\n      ON CONFLICT (site_id, barcode) DO UPDATE SET\n        on_hand = GREATEST(?, 0),\n        updated_at = now()\n    `,\n    args: [\n      input.barcode,\n      input.siteId,\n      input.fallbackName ?? \"Unknown\",\n      input.fallbackCategory ?? null,\n      input.fallbackUnit ?? null,\n      input.onHand,\n      input.onHand,\n    ],\n  });\n}\n\n// ─── Canonical Products ──────────────────────────────────────────"
}

  "new_string": "/**\n * Append a single stock-correction row to the \"Corrections\" tab on the\n * linked sheet. Append-only audit — never replaces. Number(row.old_qty) : null;\n  const newQty = Number(row.new_qty);\n  const delta = oldQty != null ? newQty - oldQty : null;\n  await appendInventoryRow(\n    sheets,\n    spreadsheetId,\n    \"Corrections\",\n    INVENTORY_SHEET_HEADERS.Corrections,\n    [\n      formatShortDateInIST(correctedAt),\n      formatTimeInIST(correctedAt),\n      row.product_name,\n      row.barcode,\n      oldQty != null ? String(oldQty) : \"\",\n      String(newQty),\n      delta != null ? (delta > 0 ? `+${delta}` : String(delta)) : \"\",\n      row.old_expiry ?? \"\",\n      row.new_expiry ?? \"\",\n      row.guard_name ?? row.guard_id ?? \"\",\n    ],\n  );\n}\n\nasync function appendComplianceRow(siteId: string, receiptId: string): Promise<void> {"

1455	// ───────────────────────── Stock ─────────────────────────
1456	
1457	function StockTab() {
1458	  const stockQuery = useQuery({
1459	    queryKey: ["inv-stock-snapshot"],
1460	    queryFn: listStockSnapshot,
1461	    staleTime: 30_000,
1462	  });
1463	  const rows: StockSnapshotRow[] = stockQuery.data?.data?.rows ?? [];
1464	  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("");
1465	
1466	  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
1467	    const q = filter.trim().toLowerCase();
1468	    if (!q) return rows;
1469	    return rows.filter(
1470	      (r) =>
1471	        r.canonical_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
1472	        (r.category ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
1473	        (r.barcodes ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(q),
1474	    );
1475	  }, [rows, filter]);
1476	
1477	  const syncMutation = useMutation({
1478	    mutationFn: syncStockSheet,
1479	    onSuccess: (res) => {
1480	      if (!res.ok) {
1481	        toastError(errorMessage((res.data as { error?: string }).error, "Sync failed"));
1482	        return;
1483	      }
1484	      toastSuccess("Stock pushed to Google Sheet");
1485	    },
1486	    onError: (err) => toastError(err, "Sync failed"),
1487	  });
1488	
1489	  return (
1490	    <div>
1491	      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", marginBottom: 10, gap: 10 }}>
1492	        <input
1493	          type="text"
1494	          placeholder="Filter by name, category, barcode…"
1495	          value={filter}
1496	          onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}
1497	          style={{ ...input, flex: 1, maxWidth: 360 }}
1498	        />
1499	        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
1500	          <button type="button" onClick={() => void stockQuery.refetch()} style={ghostBtn}>
1501	            <RefreshCw size={14} /> Refresh
1502	          </button>
1503	          <button
1504	            type="button"
1505	            style={ghostBtn}
1506	            disabled={syncMutation.isPending}
1507	            onClick={() => syncMutation.mutate()}
1508	          >
1509	            <Upload size={14} /> {syncMutation.isPending ? "Syncing…" : "Sync to Sheet"}
1510	          </button>
1511	        </div>
1512	      </div>
1513	
1514	      <div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
1515	        <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse", fontSize: 13 }}>
1516	          <thead>
1517	            <tr style={{ textAlign: "left", color: "var(--muted)" }}>

493	export async function fileToBase64(file: File): Promise<string> {
494	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
495	    const reader = new FileReader();
496	    reader.onload = () => {
497	      const result = reader.result;
498	      if (typeof result !== "string") {
499	        reject(new Error("Failed to read file"));
500	        return;
501	      }
502	      // strip "data:image/...;base64," prefix
503	      const comma = result.indexOf(",");
504	      resolve(comma >= 0 ? result.slice(comma + 1) : result);
505	    };
506	    reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error);
507	    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
508	  });

144	function header(req: IncomingMessage, name: string): string | undefined {
145	  const v = req.headers[name];
146	  return Array.isArray(v) ? v[0] : v;
147	}
148	
149	type AuthedRequest = {
150	  guardId: string;
151	  guardName: string;
152	  siteId: string;
153	  deviceId: string | null;
154	  /** Set when admin Bearer auth was used (dashboard). Null on guard auth. */
155	  adminEmail: string | null;
156	};
157	
158	async function authenticate(
159	  req: IncomingMessage,
160	  res: ServerResponse,
161	): Promise<AuthedRequest | null> {
162	  const token =
163	    header(req, "x-guard-token") ??
164	    (parseUrl(req.url ?? "", true).query["token"] as string | undefined);
165	
166	  // Guard auth path (field-app)
167	  if (token) {
168	    const session = await validateGuardSession(token);
169	    if (!session) {
170	      json(res, 401, { error: "Invalid or expired session" });
171	      return null;
172	    }
173	    const siteId = header(req, "x-site-id");
174	    if (!siteId) {
175	      json(res, 400, { error: "Missing x-site-id" });
176	      return null;
177	    }
178	    return {
179	      guardId: session.guardId,
180	      guardName: session.guardName,
181	      siteId,
182	      deviceId: header(req, "x-device-id") ?? null,
183	      adminEmail: null,
184	    };
185	  }
186	
187	  // Admin auth path (dashboard)
188	  const admin = await resolveAdminSession(req);
189	  if (!admin) {
190	    json(res, 401, { error: "Missing x-guard-token or admin Bearer token" });
191	    return null;
192	  }
193	  const siteId = header(req, "x-site-id");
194	  if (!siteId) {
195	    json(res, 400, { error: "Missing x-site-id" });
196	    return null;
197	  }
198	  return {
199	    guardId: `admin:${admin.email}`,
200	    guardName: admin.name,
201	    siteId,
202	    deviceId: header(req, "x-device-id") ?? null,
203	    adminEmail: admin.email ?? null,
204	  };
205	}
206	
207	function asString(v: unknown): string | null {
208	  if (typeof v !== "string") return null;
209	  const t = v.trim();
210	  return t.length === 0 ? null : t;
211	}
212	
213	function asNumber(v: unknown): number | null {
214	  if (typeof v === "number" && Number.isFinite(v)) return v;

  "new_string": "// ───────────────────────── History (stock corrections) ─────────────────────────\n\nfunction HistoryTab() {\n  const correctionsQuery = useQuery({\n    queryKey: [\"inv-corrections\"],\n    queryFn: listStockCorrections,\n    staleTime: 30_000,\n  });\n  const rows: StockCorrectionRow[] = correctionsQuery.data?.data?.corrections ?? [];\n  const [filter, setFilter] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const filtered = useMemo(() => {\n    const q = filter.trim().toLowerCase();\n    if (!q) return rows;\n    return rows.filter(\n      (r) =>\n        r.product_name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\n        r.barcode.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\n        (r.guard_name ?? \"\").toLowerCase().includes(q),\n    );\n  }, [rows, filter]);\n\n  const fmtQty = (v: number | null) => (v == null ? \"—\" : String(v));\n  const fmtDelta = (oldQ: number | null, newQ: number) => {\n    if (oldQ == null) return \"—\";\n    const d = newQ - oldQ;\n    if (d === 0) return \"0\";\n    return d > 0 ? `+${d}` : String(d);\n  };\n  const fmtDate = (v: string | null) => (v ? v : \"—\");\n  const fmtWhen = (iso: string) => {\n    try {\n      const d = new Date(iso);\n      return d.toLocaleString();\n    } catch {\n      return iso;\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div>\n      <div style={{ display: \"flex\", justifyContent: \"space-between\", alignItems: \"center\", marginBottom: 10, gap: 10 }}>\n        <input\n          type=\"text\"\n          placeholder=\"Filter by product, barcode, or guard…\"\n          value={filter}\n          onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}\n          style={{ ...input, flex: 1, maxWidth: 360 }}\n        />\n        <button type=\"button\" onClick={() => void correctionsQuery.refetch()} style={ghostBtn}>\n          <RefreshCw size={14} /> Refresh\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      <div style={{ overflowX: \"auto\" }}>\n        <table style={{ width: \"100%\", borderCollapse: \"collapse\", fontSize: 13 }}>\n          <thead>\n            <tr style={{ textAlign: \"left\", color: \"var(--muted)\" }}>\n              <th style={th}>When</th>\n              <th style={th}>Product</th>\n              <th style={th}>Barcode</th>\n              <th style={th}>Old qty</th>\n              <th style={th}>New qty</th>\n              <th style={th}>Δ</th>\n              <th style={th}>Old expiry</th>\n              <th style={th}>New expiry</th>\n              <th style={th}>By</th>\n            </tr>\n          </thead>\n          <tbody>\n            {correctionsQuery.isLoading && (\n              <tr>\n                <td style={td} colSpan={9}>Loading…</td>\n              </tr>\n            )}\n            {!correctionsQuery.isLoading && filtered.length === 0 && (\n              <tr>\n                <td style={{ ...td, color: \"var(--muted)\" }} colSpan={9}>\n                  No stock corrections yet. Edits made from the field-app's Stock screen appear here.\n                </td>\n              </tr>\n            )}\n            {filtered.map((r) => (\n              <tr key={r.id} style={{ borderTop: \"1px solid var(--line)\" }}>\n                <td style={td}>{fmtWhen(r.corrected_at)}</td>\n                <td style={td}>{r.product_name}</td>\n                <td style={{ ...td, fontFamily: \"monospace\", fontSize: 12 }}>{r.barcode}</td>\n                <td style={td}>{fmtQty(r.old_qty)}</td>\n                <td style={td}>{fmtQty(r.new_qty)}</td>\n                <td style={td}>{fmtDelta(r.old_qty, r.new_qty)}</td>\n                <td style={td}>{fmtDate(r.old_expiry)}</td>\n                <td style={td}>{fmtDate(r.new_expiry)}</td>\n                <td style={td}>{r.guard_name ?? r.guard_id ?? \"—\"}</td>\n              </tr>\n            ))}\n          </tbody>\n        </table>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n// ───────────────────────── Catalog ─────────────────────────"
}

> AGENT

All four pieces done and type-clean. Summary of what shipped:

**field-app** (`field-app/`)
- New `EditStockScreen` (`src/modules/inventory/screens/EditStockScreen.tsx`) — opens with current qty + nearest expiry pre-filled, `QtyStepper` `min=0`, "No expiry" checkbox, Save disabled when nothing changed.
- StockScreen card now `onPress` → `navigation.navigate('EditStock', { barcode })` (`StockScreen.tsx:202`).
- New outbox kind `stock_correction` + dispatcher entry → `api.send.stockCorrection` → `POST /api/inventory/corrections`.
- New DB helpers `correctStockLocal`, `getNearestExpiry` (`src/db/stock.ts`).
- Registered in `inventoryModule.screens` + `RootStackParamList`. i18n keys: `editStock`, `editStockSub`, `currentCount`, `newCount`, `saveChanges`, `noChanges`.

**office-ops-engine** (`office-ops-engine/`)
- New table `inventory_stock_corrections` in `lib/postgres-schema.ts` (idempotent on `id`, FK to guards, indexed by site+date and site+barcode).
- New DB fns `insertStockCorrection`, `getStockCorrection`, `listStockCorrections`, `setStockOnHand` in `modules/inventory/db.ts`.
- New routes in `modules/inventory/routes.ts`:
  - `POST /api/inventory/corrections` — inserts audit row, overwrites `inventory_stock.on_hand` to the absolute new value, fires sheet append + debounced Stock-tab refresh.
  - `GET  /api/inventory/corrections` — list for dashboard.
- New `appendCorrectionRowToSheet` helper + `Corrections` headers added to `lib/google-sheets.ts` (date / time / product / barcode / old qty / new qty / Δ / old expiry / new expiry / corrected by).

**ops-dashboard** (`ops-dashboard/`)
- New `listStockCorrections` + `StockCorrectionRow` in `src/api/inventory.ts`.
- New **History** tab between Stock and Export in `InventorySection.tsx` — filterable table (product / barcode / guard) showing when, product, barcode, old→new qty + Δ, old→new expiry, performed by.

**Notes for testing:**
- I type-checked all three subrepos (only pre-existing visitor errors remain, unrelated to this work). I did **not** run the field-app on a device or the dashboard in a browser — please verify the UI flow yourself, especially: tap card → screen opens with correct qty + expiry, decrementing to 0 works, saving updates the stock count, and the History tab shows the row after a sync.
- Nothing deployed, nothing committed. Schema migration runs automatically on next engine boot (the `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` is idempotent and ships in the same `officeOpsSchemaSql()` block).

  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/modules/inventory/screens/EditStockScreen.tsx",
  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport { Calendar, Check } from 'lucide-react-native';\nimport { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';\nimport React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport {\n  KeyboardAvoidingView,\n  Platform,\n  Pressable,\n  ScrollView,\n  StyleSheet,\n  View,\n} from 'react-native';\nimport {\n  AppBar,\n  Button,\n  Card,\n  QtyStepper,\n  radius,\n  spacing,\n  Text,\n} from '../../../design';\nimport { getSession } from '../../../auth/session';\nimport { correctStockLocal, findStock, getNearestExpiry, StockRow } from '../../../db/stock';\nimport { enqueue } from '../../../db/outbox';\nimport { useT } from '../../../i18n';\nimport { useTheme } from '../../../theme';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';\nimport { flushOnce } from '../../../sync/syncService';\nimport { haptic } from '../../../utils/haptics';\nimport { DatePickerModal } from '../components/DatePickerModal';\nimport { useKeyboardHeight } from '../../../hooks/useKeyboardHeight';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'EditStock'>;\n\nfunction formatForDisplay(iso: string): string {\n  try {\n    const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');\n    return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;\n  } catch {\n    return iso;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction toISO(d: Date): string {\n  return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];\n}\n\n/** Earliest date out of a list; nulls ignored. Returns null if all null. */\nfunction earliestISO(dates: (Date | null)[]): string | null {\n  const isos = dates.filter((d): d is Date => !!d).map(toISO).sort();\n  return isos[0] ?? null;\n}\n\nexport function EditStockScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const kbHeight = useKeyboardHeight();\n  const { barcode } = route.params;\n\n  const [row, setRow] = useState<StockRow | null>(null);\n  const [originalQty, setOriginalQty] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [originalExpiry, setOriginalExpiry] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [qty, setQty] = useState<number>(0);\n  const [expiryDateObj, setExpiryDateObj] = useState<Date | null>(null);\n  const [noExpiry, setNoExpiry] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // Per-pack expiry. Mirrors ReceivingScreen: off by default; appears only\n  // when qty > 1 and not noExpiry. When on, expand into N rows so the\n  // guard can set a different expiry on each pack.\n  const [perPackExpiry, setPerPackExpiry] = useState(false);\n  const [perPackExpiries, setPerPackExpiries] = useState<(Date | null)[]>([]);\n  const [editingPackIdx, setEditingPackIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);\n\n  const [showPicker, setShowPicker] = useState(false);\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    (async () => {\n      const r = await findStock(barcode);\n      const exp = await getNearestExpiry(barcode);\n      if (!r) return;\n      setRow(r);\n      setOriginalQty(r.on_hand);\n      setOriginalExpiry(exp);\n      setQty(r.on_hand);\n      if (exp) {\n        setExpiryDateObj(new Date(exp));\n        setNoExpiry(false);\n      } else {\n        setNoExpiry(true);\n      }\n    })();\n  }, [barcode]);\n\n  // Keep the per-pack list in sync with qty when toggle is on. Seed new\n  // rows with the bulk expiry so the guard only edits the differing ones.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!perPackExpiry) return;\n    setPerPackExpiries((prev) => {\n      const n = Math.max(1, Math.floor(qty));\n      if (prev.length === n) return prev;\n      const next = [...prev];\n      while (next.length < n) next.push(expiryDateObj);\n      while (next.length > n) next.pop();\n      return next;\n    });\n  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, expiryDateObj]);\n\n  // Auto-disable per-pack when the toggle's preconditions go away\n  // (qty drops to 1, or guard checks \"no expiry\").\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (perPackExpiry && (qty <= 1 || noExpiry)) {\n      setPerPackExpiry(false);\n    }\n  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, noExpiry]);\n\n  /** Single new_expiry sent to server when per-pack is off. */\n  const newSingleExpiry = useMemo(() => {\n    if (noExpiry || perPackExpiry || !expiryDateObj) return null;\n    return toISO(expiryDateObj);\n  }, [expiryDateObj, noExpiry, perPackExpiry]);\n\n  /** Array of expiries sent when per-pack is on. */\n  const newExpiriesArray = useMemo<string[] | null>(() => {\n    if (noExpiry || !perPackExpiry) return null;\n    return perPackExpiries.map((d) => (d ? toISO(d) : '')).filter((s) => s.length > 0);\n  }, [noExpiry, perPackExpiry, perPackExpiries]);\n\n  /** Whatever ends up as the locally-tracked \"nearest\" expiry. */\n  const localNearestExpiry = useMemo(() => {\n    if (noExpiry) return null;\n    if (perPackExpiry) return earliestISO(perPackExpiries);\n    return expiryDateObj ? toISO(expiryDateObj) : null;\n  }, [noExpiry, perPackExpiry, perPackExpiries, expiryDateObj]);\n\n  const changed = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!row) return false;\n    if (qty !== originalQty) return true;\n    if (localNearestExpiry !== originalExpiry) return true;\n    // Per-pack with same nearest as original still counts as a change if\n    // any pack carries a date different from the bulk default.\n    if (perPackExpiry) return true;\n    return false;\n  }, [row, qty, originalQty, localNearestExpiry, originalExpiry, perPackExpiry]);\n\n  const packDisplay = row && row.pack_size != null && row.pack_size !== 1 && row.unit\n    ? `${row.pack_size} ${row.unit}`\n    : row?.unit ?? null;\n\n  const submit = async () => {\n    if (!row || !changed) return;\n    const session = getSession();\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await correctStockLocal(barcode, qty, localNearestExpiry);\n      await enqueue('stock_correction', {\n        id: `crn_${nanoid(12)}`,\n        barcode,\n        product_name: row.name,\n        old_qty: originalQty,\n        new_qty: qty,\n        old_expiry: originalExpiry,\n        new_expiry: newSingleExpiry,\n        new_expiries: newExpiriesArray,\n        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,\n        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,\n        corrected_at: Date.now(),\n      });\n      flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n      haptic.success();\n      navigation.goBack();\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (!row) {\n    return (\n      <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>\n        <AppBar title={t('editStock')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />\n      </View>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <KeyboardAvoidingView\n      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'height' : undefined}\n      style={{ flex: 1 }}\n    >\n      <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>\n        <AppBar title={t('editStock')} subtitle={t('editStockSub')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />\n\n        <ScrollView\n          contentContainerStyle={[styles.content, { paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl + kbHeight }]}\n          keyboardShouldPersistTaps=\"handled\"\n        >\n          <Card tone=\"elevated\" padding=\"xl\">\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n              {t('product').toUpperCase()}\n            </Text>\n            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-end', marginTop: spacing.xs, gap: spacing.md }}>\n              <Text variant=\"headlineSmall\" style={{ flex: 1 }}>{row.name}</Text>\n              {packDisplay ? (\n                <Text variant=\"titleMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>{packDisplay}</Text>\n              ) : null}\n            </View>\n            <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>\n              {t('currentCount')}: {originalQty}{row.unit ? ` ${row.unit}` : ''}\n            </Text>\n          </Card>\n\n          <View style={styles.qtySection}>\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>\n              {t('newCount').toUpperCase()}\n            </Text>\n            <QtyStepper value={qty} onChange={setQty} min={0} />\n          </View>\n\n          <View style={styles.expirySection}>\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n              {t('expiryDate').toUpperCase()}\n            </Text>\n\n            {/* Per-pack toggle. Mirrors ReceivingScreen: only useful for\n                multi-qty edits. */}\n            {qty > 1 && !noExpiry && (\n              <Pressable\n                onPress={() => { setPerPackExpiry((v) => !v); haptic.tap(); }}\n                style={[styles.noExpiryRow, { marginTop: spacing.sm }]}\n              >\n                <View\n                  style={[\n                    styles.checkbox,\n                    {\n                      backgroundColor: perPackExpiry ? palette.primary : 'transparent',\n                      borderColor: perPackExpiry ? palette.primary : palette.outline,\n                    },\n                  ]}\n                >\n                  {perPackExpiry && <Check size={14} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={3} />}\n                </View>\n                <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n                  {t('perPackExpiry')} ({Math.floor(qty)} {t('packsLabel')})\n                </Text>\n              </Pressable>\n            )}\n\n            {!noExpiry && !perPackExpiry && (\n              <>\n                <Pressable\n                  onPress={() => setShowPicker(true)}\n                  style={[\n                    styles.expiryInput,\n                    {\n                      backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,\n                      borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n                      flexDirection: 'row',\n                      alignItems: 'center',\n                    },\n                  ]}\n                >\n                  <Calendar size={20} color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} strokeWidth={2} />\n                  <Text\n                    variant=\"bodyLarge\"\n                    color={expiryDateObj ? palette.onSurface : palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n                    style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm, flex: 1, letterSpacing: 1 }}\n                  >\n                    {expiryDateObj ? formatForDisplay(toISO(expiryDateObj)) : t('tapToSelectDate')}\n                  </Text>\n                </Pressable>\n                {showPicker && editingPackIdx == null && (\n                  <DatePickerModal\n                    visible={showPicker}\n                    value={expiryDateObj}\n                    onSelect={(d) => { setExpiryDateObj(d); setNoExpiry(false); }}\n                    onDismiss={() => setShowPicker(false)}\n                  />\n                )}\n              </>\n            )}\n\n            {!noExpiry && perPackExpiry && (\n              <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm, gap: spacing.xs }}>\n                {perPackExpiries.map((d, i) => (\n                  <Pressable\n                    key={i}\n                    onPress={() => { setEditingPackIdx(i); setShowPicker(true); }}\n                    style={[\n                      styles.expiryInput,\n                      {\n                        backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,\n                        borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n                        flexDirection: 'row',\n                        alignItems: 'center',\n                      },\n                    ]}\n                  >\n                    <Text variant=\"labelMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginRight: spacing.sm, minWidth: 56 }}>\n                      {t('pack')} {i + 1}\n                    </Text>\n                    <Calendar size={18} color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} strokeWidth={2} />\n                    <Text\n                      variant=\"bodyLarge\"\n                      color={d ? palette.onSurface : palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n                      style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm, flex: 1, letterSpacing: 1 }}\n                    >\n                      {d ? formatForDisplay(toISO(d)) : t('tapToSelectDate')}\n                    </Text>\n                  </Pressable>\n                ))}\n                {showPicker && editingPackIdx != null && (\n                  <DatePickerModal\n                    visible={showPicker}\n                    value={perPackExpiries[editingPackIdx]}\n                    onSelect={(date) => {\n                      setPerPackExpiries((prev) => prev.map((p, i) => (i === editingPackIdx ? date : p)));\n                    }}\n                    onDismiss={() => { setShowPicker(false); setEditingPackIdx(null); }}\n                  />\n                )}\n              </View>\n            )}\n\n            <Pressable\n              onPress={() => { setNoExpiry(!noExpiry); haptic.tap(); }}\n              style={styles.noExpiryRow}\n            >\n              <View\n                style={[\n                  styles.checkbox,\n                  {\n                    backgroundColor: noExpiry ? palette.primary : 'transparent',\n                    borderColor: noExpiry ? palette.primary : palette.outline,\n                  },\n                ]}\n              >\n                {noExpiry && <Check size={14} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={3} />}\n              </View>\n              <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n                {t('noExpiry')}\n              </Text>\n            </Pressable>\n          </View>\n        </ScrollView>\n\n        <View style={[styles.footer, { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant }]}>\n          <Button\n            label={changed ? t('saveChanges') : t('noChanges')}\n            onPress={submit}\n            loading={saving}\n            disabled={!changed}\n            size=\"lg\"\n            fullWidth\n            leadingIcon={<Check size={22} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={2.4} />}\n          />\n          <Button\n            label={t('cancel')}\n            variant=\"text\"\n            onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}\n            style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}\n          />\n        </View>\n      </View>\n    </KeyboardAvoidingView>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1 },\n  content: { padding: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.xl },\n  qtySection: { gap: spacing.lg, alignItems: 'stretch' },\n  expirySection: { gap: spacing.sm },\n  expiryInput: {\n    borderRadius: radius.md,\n    borderWidth: 1.5,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,\n    minHeight: 52,\n    fontSize: 18,\n    letterSpacing: 2,\n  },\n  noExpiryRow: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    gap: spacing.sm,\n    paddingVertical: spacing.xs,\n  },\n  checkbox: {\n    width: 22,\n    height: 22,\n    borderRadius: 4,\n    borderWidth: 2,\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n  },\n  footer: {\n    padding: spacing.xl,\n    borderTopWidth: 1,\n  },\n});\n"

  "new_string": "    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS inventory_stock_corrections (\n      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n      site_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n      barcode TEXT NOT NULL,\n      product_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n      old_qty NUMERIC,\n      new_qty NUMERIC NOT NULL,\n      old_expiry DATE,\n      new_expiry DATE,\n      -- Populated when the guard turned on \"different expiry per pack\".\n      -- One ISO date per unit in new_qty. When set, new_expiry is null.\n      new_expiries DATE[],\n      device_id TEXT,\n      guard_id TEXT REFERENCES guards(id),\n      guard_name TEXT,\n      corrected_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n      created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n    );\n    -- Idempotent add for installations that created the table before the\n    -- new_expiries column existed.\n    ALTER TABLE inventory_stock_corrections ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS new_expiries DATE[];"
}

  "old_string": "export type StockCorrectionRow = {\n  id: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  product_name: string;\n  old_qty: number | null;\n  new_qty: number;\n  old_expiry: string | null;\n  new_expiry: string | null;\n  device_id: string | null;\n  guard_id: string | null;\n  guard_name: string | null;\n  corrected_at: string;\n  created_at: string;\n};\n\nexport async function insertStockCorrection(input: {\n  id: string;\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  productName: string;\n  oldQty: number | null;\n  newQty: number;\n  oldExpiry?: string | null;\n  newExpiry?: string | null;\n  deviceId?: string | null;\n  guardId?: string | null;\n  guardName?: string | null;\n  correctedAt: string;\n}): Promise<{ inserted: boolean }> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_stock_corrections (\n        id, site_id, barcode, product_name, old_qty, new_qty,\n        old_expiry, new_expiry, device_id, guard_id, guard_name, corrected_at\n      )\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n      ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING\n    `,\n    args: [\n      input.id,\n      input.siteId,\n      input.barcode,\n      input.productName,\n      input.oldQty,\n      input.newQty,\n      input.oldExpiry ?? null,\n      input.newExpiry ?? null,\n      input.deviceId ?? null,\n      input.guardId ?? null,\n      input.guardName ?? null,\n      input.correctedAt,\n    ],\n  });\n  return { inserted: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };\n}\n\nexport async function getStockCorrection(\n  siteId: string,\n  id: string,\n): Promise<StockCorrectionRow | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT id, site_id, barcode, product_name, old_qty, new_qty,\n             to_char(old_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS old_expiry,\n             to_char(new_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS new_expiry,\n             device_id, guard_id, guard_name, corrected_at, created_at\n      FROM inventory_stock_corrections\n      WHERE site_id = ? AND id = ?\n      LIMIT 1\n    `,\n    args: [siteId, id],\n  });\n  return (result.rows[0] as StockCorrectionRow | undefined) ?? null;\n}\n\nexport async function listStockCorrections(\n  siteId: string,\n): Promise<StockCorrectionRow[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT id, site_id, barcode, product_name, old_qty, new_qty,\n             to_char(old_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS old_expiry,\n             to_char(new_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS new_expiry,\n             device_id, guard_id, guard_name, corrected_at, created_at\n      FROM inventory_stock_corrections\n      WHERE site_id = ?\n      ORDER BY corrected_at DESC\n    `,\n    args: [siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows as StockCorrectionRow[];\n}",
  "new_string": "export type StockCorrectionRow = {\n  id: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  product_name: string;\n  old_qty: number | null;\n  new_qty: number;\n  old_expiry: string | null;\n  new_expiry: string | null;\n  /** Comma-separated list of per-pack expiry ISO dates (read shape). */\n  new_expiries: string | null;\n  device_id: string | null;\n  guard_id: string | null;\n  guard_name: string | null;\n  corrected_at: string;\n  created_at: string;\n};\n\nexport async function insertStockCorrection(input: {\n  id: string;\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  productName: string;\n  oldQty: number | null;\n  newQty: number;\n  oldExpiry?: string | null;\n  newExpiry?: string | null;\n  /** Per-pack expiry dates when the guard turned on \"different per pack\". */\n  newExpiries?: string[] | null;\n  deviceId?: string | null;\n  guardId?: string | null;\n  guardName?: string | null;\n  correctedAt: string;\n}): Promise<{ inserted: boolean }> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  // Postgres DATE[] literal: '{2026-01-01,2026-02-15}'. The libsql/pg shim\n  // doesn't auto-box arrays so we hand-encode. Empty array → NULL.\n  const expiriesLiteral =\n    input.newExpiries && input.newExpiries.length > 0\n      ? `{${input.newExpiries.map((d) => `\"${d.replace(/\"/g, '')}\"`).join(\",\")}}`\n      : null;\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_stock_corrections (\n        id, site_id, barcode, product_name, old_qty, new_qty,\n        old_expiry, new_expiry, new_expiries,\n        device_id, guard_id, guard_name, corrected_at\n      )\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?::DATE[], ?, ?, ?, ?)\n      ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING\n    `,\n    args: [\n      input.id,\n      input.siteId,\n      input.barcode,\n      input.productName,\n      input.oldQty,\n      input.newQty,\n      input.oldExpiry ?? null,\n      input.newExpiry ?? null,\n      expiriesLiteral,\n      input.deviceId ?? null,\n      input.guardId ?? null,\n      input.guardName ?? null,\n      input.correctedAt,\n    ],\n  });\n  return { inserted: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };\n}\n\n// SELECT shape: render new_expiries as a comma-separated string so the\n// driver layer doesn't have to handle array unmarshalling.\nconst STOCK_CORRECTION_SELECT = `\n  id, site_id, barcode, product_name, old_qty, new_qty,\n  to_char(old_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS old_expiry,\n  to_char(new_expiry, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS new_expiry,\n  (\n    SELECT string_agg(to_char(d, 'YYYY-MM-DD'), ', ' ORDER BY d)\n    FROM unnest(new_expiries) AS d\n  ) AS new_expiries,\n  device_id, guard_id, guard_name, corrected_at, created_at\n`;\n\nexport async function getStockCorrection(\n  siteId: string,\n  id: string,\n): Promise<StockCorrectionRow | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT ${STOCK_CORRECTION_SELECT}\n      FROM inventory_stock_corrections\n      WHERE site_id = ? AND id = ?\n      LIMIT 1\n    `,\n    args: [siteId, id],\n  });\n  return (result.rows[0] as StockCorrectionRow | undefined) ?? null;\n}\n\nexport async function listStockCorrections(\n  siteId: string,\n): Promise<StockCorrectionRow[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT ${STOCK_CORRECTION_SELECT}\n      FROM inventory_stock_corrections\n      WHERE site_id = ?\n      ORDER BY corrected_at DESC\n    `,\n    args: [siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows as StockCorrectionRow[];\n}"
}

> AGENT

Done. The Edit Stock screen now mirrors the Receiving screen's expiry UX.

**What changed:**
- **field-app** `EditStockScreen.tsx`: added the `Different expiry per pack` toggle (only visible when qty > 1 and not "No expiry"). When on, the screen expands into N date-picker rows — one per unit, seeded with the current expiry. Toggle auto-disables if qty drops to 1 or "No expiry" is checked. Local `stock_levels.nearest_expiry` is set to the earliest of the per-pack dates on save. Payload sends `new_expiries: string[]` when per-pack is on, else `new_expiry: string`.
- **office-ops-engine**:
  - `inventory_stock_corrections` table gains `new_expiries DATE[]` (with an idempotent `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` for already-deployed DBs).
  - `insertStockCorrection` hand-encodes the Postgres array literal and casts via `?::DATE[]`.
  - `getStockCorrection` / `listStockCorrections` flatten the array to a comma-separated string in the SELECT so the dashboard doesn't have to parse array unmarshalling.
  - `POST /api/inventory/corrections` route validates each entry as `YYYY-MM-DD` and drops bad ones.
  - Sheet writer renders `new_expiries ?? new_expiry` in the "New Expiry" column of the `Corrections` tab.
- **ops-dashboard**: `StockCorrectionRow` gains `new_expiries`; History tab renders `r.new_expiries ?? r.new_expiry` so multi-pack corrections show as a comma-separated list in one cell.

**Test-on-device checklist** (couldn't verify these myself):
- Tap a stock item with qty 3 → screen opens with qty=3, the existing single expiry shown.
- Check the per-pack toggle → 3 date pickers appear, each seeded with the current date.
- Change one of them → Save lights up, hit it.
- Verify the History tab shows three comma-separated dates in the "New Expiry" column once sync completes.
- Verify the Sheet's `Corrections` tab does the same.
- Edge: drop qty to 1 with per-pack on → toggle should auto-collapse back to single picker.

Nothing committed or deployed.

36	/** Bigram (Dice coefficient) similarity — used to surface a single
37	 *  "Did you mean…" pill when nothing matched as a substring. Catches
38	 *  typos like "wagh bkri" → "wagh bakri" without flooding the user
39	 *  with low-quality matches. */
40	function similarity(a: string, b: string): number {
41	  const la = a.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
42	  const lb = b.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
43	  if (la === lb) return 1;
44	  if (la.length < 2 || lb.length < 2) return 0;
45	  const bg = (s: string) => {
46	    const out = new Set<string>();
47	    for (let i = 0; i < s.length - 1; i++) out.add(s.slice(i, i + 2));
48	    return out;
49	  };
50	  const A = bg(la);
51	  const B = bg(lb);
52	  let inter = 0;
53	  for (const x of A) if (B.has(x)) inter++;
54	  return (2 * inter) / (A.size + B.size);
55	}
56

101	  /** Build the dropdown list:
102	   *   1. With no query — show items expected on open orders first, then
103	   *      the rest, capped at MAX_VISIBLE.
104	   *   2. With a query — rank by match strength (exact > prefix >
105	   *      substring > category/HSN substring), capped at MAX_VISIBLE.
106	   *   3. Always compute the best fuzzy near-miss separately for the
107	   *      "Did you mean…" pill. */
108	  const { visible, suggestion } = useMemo(() => {
109	    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
110	    if (!q) {
111	      // No query — surface every catalog item. Items expected on open
112	      // orders float to the top so the most likely picks are one tap
113	      // away; the rest follow in alphabetical order.
114	      const expected: CanonicalProduct[] = [];
115	      const others: CanonicalProduct[] = [];
116	      for (const p of products) {
117	        (openItemMap[p.product_id] ? expected : others).push(p);
118	      }
119	      others.sort((a, b) => a.canonical_name.localeCompare(b.canonical_name));
120	      return {
121	        visible: [...expected, ...others],
122	        suggestion: null as CanonicalProduct | null,
123	      };
124	    }
125	
126	    type Scored = { p: CanonicalProduct; score: number };
127	    const scored: Scored[] = products.map((p) => {
128	      const name = p.canonical_name.toLowerCase();
129	      let score = 0;
130	      if (name === q) score = 3;
131	      else if (name.startsWith(q)) score = 2;
132	      else if (name.includes(q)) score = 1;
133	      else if ((p.category ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(q)) score = 0.5;
134	      else if ((p.hsn_code ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(q)) score = 0.5;
135	      return { p, score };
136	    });
137	
138	    const exactMatches = scored.filter((x) => x.score > 0);
139	    exactMatches.sort((a, b) => {
140	      if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
141	      const aExpected = openItemMap[a.p.product_id] ? 1 : 0;
142	      const bExpected = openItemMap[b.p.product_id] ? 1 : 0;
143	      if (bExpected !== aExpected) return bExpected - aExpected;
144	      return a.p.canonical_name.localeCompare(b.p.canonical_name);
145	    });
146	
147	    let suggestion: CanonicalProduct | null = null;
148	    if (exactMatches.length === 0) {
149	      let best: CanonicalProduct | null = null;
150	      let bestScore = 0;
151	      for (const p of products) {
152	        const s = similarity(query, p.canonical_name);
153	        if (s > bestScore) { bestScore = s; best = p; }
154	      }
155	      if (best && bestScore >= 0.35) suggestion = best;
156	    }
157	
158	    return {
159	      visible: exactMatches.map((x) => x.p),
160	      suggestion,
161	    };
162	  }, [products, openItemMap, query]);

80	 * masquerade as a learned scan. Most-recently learned wins when several
81	 * physical barcodes are mapped to the same product (multiple brands /
82	 * batches).
83	 */
84	export async function findBarcodeForProduct(
85	  productId: string,
86	): Promise<string | null> {
87	  const db = await getDb();
88	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ barcode: string }>(
89	    `SELECT barcode FROM product_barcodes
90	     WHERE product_id = ? AND barcode NOT LIKE 'catalog\\_%' ESCAPE '\\'
91	     ORDER BY learned_at DESC
92	     LIMIT 1`,
93	    [productId],
94	  );
95	  return row?.barcode ?? null;
96	}
97	
98	export async function upsertCanonicalProduct(product: CanonicalProduct): Promise<void> {
99	  const db = await getDb();
100	  await db.runAsync(
101	    `INSERT INTO canonical_products (product_id, canonical_name, category, hsn_code, unit, pack_size, has_barcode, updated_at)
102	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
103	     ON CONFLICT(product_id) DO UPDATE SET
104	       canonical_name = excluded.canonical_name,
105	       category = excluded.category,
106	       hsn_code = excluded.hsn_code,
107	       unit = excluded.unit,
108	       pack_size = excluded.pack_size,
109	       has_barcode = excluded.has_barcode,
110	       updated_at = excluded.updated_at`,
111	    [
112	      product.product_id,
113	      product.canonical_name,
114	      product.category,
115	      product.hsn_code,
116	      product.unit,
117	      product.pack_size,
118	      product.has_barcode ? 1 : 0,
119	      product.updated_at,
120	    ],
121	  );
122	}
123	
124	/**
125	 * Replace the entire canonical product catalog with fresh data
126	 * from the backend. Used during full sync.
127	 *
128	 * IMPORTANT: do NOT `DELETE FROM canonical_products` first — there is a
129	 * cascading FK from `product_barcodes`, so a blanket delete blows away
130	 * every learned barcode → product mapping the guard has built up.
131	 * Instead we upsert each remote row, then delete the ones the remote
132	 * no longer knows about.
133	 *
134	 * Per-row try/catch: one bad payload row used to abort the whole loop,
135	 * which left the local cache silently missing items further down the
136	 * list. Now we count failures and surface them so they can be diagnosed.
137	 */
138	export async function replaceCanonicalProducts(
139	  products: CanonicalProduct[],
140	): Promise<{ ok: number; failed: number; firstError?: string }> {
141	  const db = await getDb();
142	  let ok = 0;
143	  let failed = 0;
144	  let firstError: string | undefined;
145	  for (const p of products) {
146	    try {
147	      // Coerce fields the local schema marks NOT NULL — the server should
148	      // already enforce these, but a single bad row (e.g. unit migrated
149	      // from null) shouldn't drop the rest of the catalog.
150	      await upsertCanonicalProduct({
151	        product_id: String(p.product_id),
152	        canonical_name: String(p.canonical_name ?? ''),
153	        category: p.category ?? null,
154	        hsn_code: p.hsn_code ?? null,
155	        unit: p.unit ?? 'pcs',
156	        pack_size:
157	          typeof p.pack_size === 'number' && Number.isFinite(p.pack_size)
158	            ? p.pack_size
159	            : 1,
160	        has_barcode: p.has_barcode ? 1 : 0,
161	        updated_at: p.updated_at ?? Date.now(),
162	      });
163	      ok++;
164	    } catch (err) {
165	      failed++;
166	      if (!firstError) firstError = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
167	      // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
168	      console.warn(`[catalog] upsert failed for ${p.product_id}:`, err);
169	    }
170	  }
171	  if (products.length === 0) return { ok, failed, firstError }; // empty payload — keep what we have
172	
173	  // Drop products the remote no longer has. Bind ids as literals (the
174	  // list is small and ids are alphanumeric); a parameterised IN with
175	  // dynamic length is painful in expo-sqlite.
176	  const ids = products.map((p) => `'${String(p.product_id).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`).join(',');
177	  try {
178	    await db.execAsync(`DELETE FROM canonical_products WHERE product_id NOT IN (${ids})`);
179	  } catch (err) {
180	    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
181	    console.warn('[catalog] stale-cleanup delete failed:', err);
182	  }
183	  return { ok, failed, firstError };
184	}

> AGENT

Found it. The bug is a missing sync.

**Diagnosis** (`CatalogPickerScreen.tsx:184-188`):

```ts
let barcode = incomingBarcode;
if (!barcode) {
  const existing = await findBarcodeForProduct(product.product_id);
  barcode = existing ?? `catalog_${product.product_id}`;
}
```

`findBarcodeForProduct` (`db/catalog.ts:84`) queries the **local** `product_barcodes` table. That table only gets a row when **this guard, on this device** scans a barcode and picks the matching catalog item (via the `learnBarcode(...)` call earlier in `pick`). It is **never populated from the backend**.

Look at the sync side (`sync/syncService.ts:122` `syncCanonicalProducts`): we pull `/api/inventory/canonical-products`, which gives each product a boolean `has_barcode` — but not the actual barcode value. So the device knows "this product has a barcode somewhere" but not what it is.

Result: if a barcode mapping was created by **another guard, by the admin in the dashboard, or via a bill upload**, this guard's local DB has no row in `product_barcodes` for it. `findBarcodeForProduct` returns `null`, so the code falls back to `catalog_<product_id>`, and `ReceivingScreen` shows "No barcode yet".

**Fix options:**

1. **Extend the catalog payload (recommended).** Add `primary_barcode` (most-recent learned) to the `RemoteCanonicalProduct` API response and cache it on `canonical_products`. `pick()` reads it from the catalog row instead of `product_barcodes`. One round-trip, no new endpoint, zero per-product DB calls. Tradeoff: only one barcode per product is surfaced — fine because the system enforces one-barcode-per-product today (the `has_barcode` flag already exists for the same reason).

2. **Sync `product_barcodes` as its own table.** Mirror the full server table into local SQLite each sync cycle. More correct if you ever allow multiple barcodes per product, but adds a sync step and table size grows with every learned mapping.

3. **On-pick lookup.** When the guard taps an item with `has_barcode=1` and no local row, fire a quick `GET /api/inventory/canonical-products/:id/barcode` and use that. Cheapest to ship but adds a network hop on every catalog pick — bad on patchy Wi-Fi, which violates the offline-first rule.

I'd ship **#1**. Want me to implement it? If yes, the change is: extend `RemoteCanonicalProduct`, add `primary_barcode` column to local `canonical_products` (migration to schema v7), populate it in `listCanonicalProducts` server-side, and switch `pick()` to read it.

1	import { getDb, now } from './database';

3	export type KnownProduct = {
4	  barcode: string;
5	  name: string;
6	  category: string | null;
7	  unit: string | null;
8	  pack_size: number | null;
9	};
10	
11	/**
12	 * Union of every product the device knows about: locally-registered
13	 * products + every row mirrored from the remote stock cache. Used by
14	 * the fuzzy-suggest hook so the user can be offered an existing name
15	 * before they type it out from scratch (or misspell it).
16	 *
17	 * Dedups on barcode, preferring the locally-registered product entry
18	 * when both sources have it (it's the authoritative name on this device).
19	 */
20	export async function listKnownProducts(): Promise<KnownProduct[]> {
21	  const db = await getDb();
22	  const rows = await db.getAllAsync<KnownProduct>(`
23	    SELECT barcode, name, category, unit, pack_size FROM products
24	    UNION
25	    SELECT barcode, name, category, unit, NULL AS pack_size FROM stock_levels
26	    WHERE barcode NOT IN (SELECT barcode FROM products)
27	    ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE
28	  `);
29	  return rows;
30	}
31	
32	// ─── Canonical Product Catalog ───────────────────────────────────

35	  product_id: string;

39	  unit: string;
40	  pack_size: number;
41	  /** 1 if any barcode is mapped to this product on the server, else 0.
42	   *  Drives the "hide already-taken products" filter when registering a
43	   *  brand-new barcode. SQLite has no bool, so it's stored as 0/1. */
44	  has_barcode: number;
45	  updated_at: number;
46	};
47	
48	export async function listCanonicalProducts(): Promise<CanonicalProduct[]> {
49	  const db = await getDb();
50	  return db.getAllAsync<CanonicalProduct>(
51	    'SELECT * FROM canonical_products ORDER BY canonical_name',
52	  );
53	}
54	
55	export async function findCanonicalProductById(
56	  productId: string,
57	): Promise<CanonicalProduct | null> {
58	  const db = await getDb();
59	  return db.getFirstAsync<CanonicalProduct>(
60	    'SELECT * FROM canonical_products WHERE product_id = ?',
61	    [productId],
62	  );
63	}
64	
65	export async function findCanonicalProductByBarcode(
66	  barcode: string,
67	): Promise<CanonicalProduct | null> {
68	  const db = await getDb();
69	  return db.getFirstAsync<CanonicalProduct>(
70	    `SELECT cp.* FROM canonical_products cp
71	     JOIN product_barcodes pb ON pb.product_id = cp.product_id
72	     WHERE pb.barcode = ?`,
73	    [barcode],
74	  );
75	}
76

78	 * Return a real barcode already mapped to this product, if any.

80	 * masquerade as a learned scan. Most-recently learned wins when several

  "new_string": "// `has_barcode` is computed from product_barcodes so the field-app's\n// register-new-barcode flow can hide products that are already taken\n// (one-barcode-per-product rule, for now). `primary_barcode` returns the\n// most-recently learned real barcode so the no-barcode pick flow can\n// reuse it instead of falling back to a synthetic `catalog_*` id.\nconst CANONICAL_PRODUCT_COLUMNS = `\n  cp.*,\n  EXISTS (\n    SELECT 1 FROM product_barcodes pb\n    WHERE pb.product_id = cp.product_id AND pb.site_id = cp.site_id\n  ) AS has_barcode,\n  (\n    SELECT pb2.barcode FROM product_barcodes pb2\n    WHERE pb2.product_id = cp.product_id\n      AND pb2.site_id = cp.site_id\n      AND pb2.barcode NOT LIKE 'catalog\\\\_%' ESCAPE '\\\\'\n    ORDER BY pb2.learned_at DESC\n    LIMIT 1\n  ) AS primary_barcode\n`;"
}

  "old_string": "  if (version < 6) {\n    // Track whether each canonical product already has a barcode mapped\n    // server-side. The register-new-barcode suggestion list filters these\n    // out so two barcodes can't map to the same product (current rule).\n    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(\n      'PRAGMA table_info(canonical_products)',\n    );\n    const hasBarcodeCol = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'has_barcode');\n    if (!hasBarcodeCol) {\n      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE canonical_products ADD COLUMN has_barcode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;`);\n    }\n    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 6;`);\n    version = 6;\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "  if (version < 6) {\n    // Track whether each canonical product already has a barcode mapped\n    // server-side. The register-new-barcode suggestion list filters these\n    // out so two barcodes can't map to the same product (current rule).\n    const cols = await db.getAllAsync<{ name: string }>(\n      'PRAGMA table_info(canonical_products)',\n    );\n    const hasBarcodeCol = cols.some((c) => c.name === 'has_barcode');\n    if (!hasBarcodeCol) {\n      await db.execAsync(`ALTER TABLE canonical_products ADD COLUMN has_barcode INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;`);\n    }\n    await db.execAsync(`PRAGMA user_version = 6;`);\n    version = 6;\n  }\n\n  if (version < 7) {\n    // Cache the canonical product's primary (most-recent) barcode locally\n    // so the \"No barcode → catalog pick\" path can reuse an existing\n    // mapping created by another guard / admin / bill upload without a\n    // round-trip. }

Only fall back\n    // to the synthetic id when nothing is mapped yet — the format is\n    // stable per product_id so a later real-barcode learn keeps history\n    // and stock aligned.\n    let barcode = incomingBarcode;\n    if (!barcode) {\n      const existing = await findBarcodeForProduct(product.product_id);\n      barcode = existing ?? `catalog_${product.product_id}`;\n    }",

> AGENT

Clean.

**Summary of the fix:**

- **office-ops-engine** `db.ts`: `CANONICAL_PRODUCT_COLUMNS` now also selects `primary_barcode` — the most-recent non-synthetic barcode from `product_barcodes` for the product (`NOT LIKE 'catalog\_%'`). `CanonicalProduct` type gains `primary_barcode?: string | null`. This flows through every `listCanonicalProducts*` query the dashboard and field-app already use, no new endpoint.

- **field-app**:
  - `db/database.ts`: schema bumped to v7, idempotent `ALTER TABLE canonical_products ADD COLUMN primary_barcode TEXT`.
  - `db/catalog.ts`: `CanonicalProduct` type + `upsertCanonicalProduct` + `replaceCanonicalProducts` persist the new column.
  - `sync/api.ts`: `RemoteCanonicalProduct.primary_barcode?` added.
  - `sync/syncService.ts`: `syncCanonicalProducts` writes it to local cache.
  - `CatalogPickerScreen.pick()`: lookup order is now `incomingBarcode` → `product.primary_barcode` (from sync) → `findBarcodeForProduct` local fallback → synthetic `catalog_<id>`.

**Why the fallback still matters:** if a guard learns a barcode while offline, that mapping sits in local `product_barcodes` but the catalog sync hasn't seen it yet. The local lookup keeps that case working until the next sync.

**Test:**
1. From the dashboard, register a product with a barcode (or have any product that's already barcode-mapped server-side).
2. On a fresh device (or after `clearLocalData`), log in and let the sync run.
3. Scanner → No barcode → catalog → pick that product.
4. ReceivingScreen should show the real barcode chip (monospace), not the "No barcode yet" dashed pill.
5. Edge: if the device cache is older than the latest barcode mapping, force a refresh on the catalog picker (the SyncRefreshButton in the AppBar) — that's now the same trigger that fetches the new `primary_barcode` field.

Nothing committed or deployed.

1	import React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
2	import { ANONYMOUS_PRINCIPAL, can, Principal } from '../rbac';
3	import { api } from '../sync/api';
4	import { clearSession, GuardSession, getSession, hydrateSession, onSessionChange, setSession } from './session';
5	
6	type Ctx = {
7	  /** True once we've read AsyncStorage for any persisted session. */
8	  ready: boolean;
9	  session: GuardSession | null;
10	  principal: Principal;
11	  login: (input: { guardName: string; pin: string }) => Promise<void>;
12	  logout: () => Promise<void>;
13	};
14	
15	const AuthContext = createContext<Ctx | null>(null);
16	
17	function principalFromSession(session: GuardSession | null): Principal {
18	  if (!session) return ANONYMOUS_PRINCIPAL;
19	  // Backend doesn't yet return roles/permissions for the guard. Until it
20	  // does, every logged-in guard gets the inventory module's view +
21	  // capture permissions. Replace with server-supplied values when the
22	  // /api/guard/login response grows them.
23	  return {
24	    userId: session.guardId,
25	    roles: ['guard'],
26	    permissions: [
27	      'inventory.view',
28	      'inventory.receive',
29	      'inventory.issue',
30	      'inventory.register',
31	      'inventory.reorder',
32	    ],
33	  };
34	}
35	
36	export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
37	  const [session, setSessionState] = useState<GuardSession | null>(getSession());
38	  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
39	
40	  useEffect(() => {
41	    hydrateSession().then((s) => {
42	      setSessionState(s);
43	      setReady(true);
44	    });
45	    return onSessionChange(setSessionState);
46	  }, []);
47	
48	  const login = useCallback(async ({ guardName, pin }: { guardName: string; pin: string }) => {
49	    const res = await api.login({ guardName, pin });
50	    await setSession({
51	      token: res.token,
52	      guardId: res.guardId,
53	      guardName: res.guardName,
54	      language: res.language,
55	    });
56	  }, []);
57	
58	  const logout = useCallback(async () => {
59	    await clearSession();
60	  }, []);
61	
62	  return (
63	    <AuthContext.Provider
64	      value={{
65	        ready,
66	        session,
67	        principal: principalFromSession(session),
68	        login,
69	        logout,
70	      }}
71	    >
72	      {children}
73	    </AuthContext.Provider>
74	  );
75	}
76	
77	export function useAuth(): Ctx {
78	  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
79	  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be inside AuthProvider');
80	  return ctx;
81	}
82	
83	/**
84	 * Permission checker tied to the live session. Replaces the stub in
85	 * `rbac/permissions.ts` for use inside the React tree.
86	 */
87	export function useCan() {
88	  const { principal } = useAuth();
89	  return useCallback((permission: string) => can(principal, permission), [principal]);
90	}
91

> AGENT

Two separate things, only one is an app bug.

**HTTP 502 (one-off):** Server-side outage. Bifrost's proxy returned an HTML error page because `ops-engine` was down or restarting. Nothing for the field-app to fix; it correctly logged and moved on.

**HTTP 401 — "Invalid or expired session" (spamming):** Real bug. The guard's session token expired (or was revoked server-side), but the field-app **never clears the local session on a 401**. Look at the loop:

1. `flushOnce()` in `sync/syncService.ts:86` only skips backend pulls when `getSession()?.token` is null.
2. There's no global 401 handler in `sync/api.ts:46-64` — it just throws `ApiError`.
3. The caller (`syncCanonicalProducts`) catches it and logs a warning.
4. Token stays in `AsyncStorage`. Next sync tick (every `syncIntervalMs`) hits 401 again. Same with every network reconnect, every screen focus.

So you get one warning per sync tick, forever, until the user manually signs out — and meanwhile every outbox write also 401s and gets marked `failed`.

**Fix:** in `sync/api.ts`'s `request()`, detect status 401 and call `clearSession()`. Once the session is null, `flushOnce` skips reads, the `AuthProvider`'s `onSessionChange` listener fires, and `RootNavigator` re-renders into `LoginScreen` automatically (the gate at `RootNavigator.tsx:41` is already there). One-time fix; no extra plumbing needed.

Two small caveats worth deciding:

1. **Login endpoint itself returns 401 on bad PIN** — we must not clear the session in that case (there's no session to clear, but more importantly we don't want the side effect leaking into login). Easy: only clear when the request carried an `X-Guard-Token` header (i.e. `getSession()?.token` was set).
2. **Outbox rows mid-flush:** if a 401 hits during outbox dispatch, the rows get marked `failed`. After re-login, the user can manually retry from the Sync Queue UI. Acceptable for v1, or I can re-queue them automatically on session change — your call.

Want me to ship the fix (auto-clear on 401 + auto-redirect to Login)? And do you want failed-due-to-401 outbox rows auto-retried after re-login, or leave them in the manual queue?

> AGENT

Done. The `development` profile now pins both env vars explicitly — same shape as `preview`.

**A few things to note:**

1. **This only affects EAS builds.** When you run `npm start` (Metro reloading JS onto an already-installed dev-client APK), the env vars baked into that APK still apply — they were set at *build* time, not at JS-reload time. So if your currently-installed dev-client APK was built before this change, it's still using whatever URL it had then. You'll need to rebuild the dev-client APK via EAS for the new env to take effect.

2. **The `config.ts` default is still the same ngrok URL**, so even without rebuilding the APK, Metro reloads pick up the right value via the fallback. The eas.json change is belt-and-braces.

3. **ngrok URLs are ephemeral.** Free ngrok tunnels rotate every restart. If/when this URL changes, you'll need to update **both** places: `field-app/src/config.ts` and `field-app/eas.json`. Worth grepping for the URL string before any future tunnel restart — or moving the tunnel to a stable subdomain if you have an ngrok paid plan.

Want me to add a quick helper or doc note about updating both places, or leave it as is?

1	import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
2	
3	const CACHE_KEY_PREFIX = '@auxilio-cache-time:';
4	const CACHE_TTL = 30 * 1000; // 30 seconds
5	
6	export async function getLastSyncTime(screenName: string): Promise<number> {
7	  try {
8	    const stored = await AsyncStorage.getItem(`${CACHE_KEY_PREFIX}${screenName}`);
9	    return stored ? parseInt(stored, 10) : 0;
10	  } catch {
11	    return 0;
12	  }
13	}
14	
15	export async function setLastSyncTime(screenName: string): Promise<void> {
16	  try {
17	    await AsyncStorage.setItem(`${CACHE_KEY_PREFIX}${screenName}`, Date.now().toString());
18	  } catch {
19	    // Silent fail - caching is best-effort
20	  }
21	}
22	
23	export function isCacheStale(lastSyncTime: number): boolean {
24	  return Date.now() - lastSyncTime > CACHE_TTL;
25	}
26	
27	export function clearAllSyncTimes(): Promise<void> {
28	  return AsyncStorage.multiRemove([
29	    `${CACHE_KEY_PREFIX}home`,
30	    `${CACHE_KEY_PREFIX}orders`,
31	    `${CACHE_KEY_PREFIX}alerts`,
32	    `${CACHE_KEY_PREFIX}stock`,
33	    `${CACHE_KEY_PREFIX}catalog`,
34	  ]);
35	}

> AGENT

Here's the full picture of what's cached, where, and how it refreshes.

## What's cached

**Persistent SQLite tables** (survive app restart, wiped only by Settings → "Clear local data"):

| Table | Purpose | Direction |
|---|---|---|
| `canonical_products` | Product catalog (name, unit, pack_size, hsn, has_barcode, primary_barcode) | server → device |
| `product_barcodes` | barcode → product mappings | device-local (learned via scan) |
| `orders` + `order_items` | Open vendor orders | server → device |
| `stock_levels` | Per-product on_hand + nearest_expiry | server → device |
| `products` | Legacy locally-registered products | device-local |
| `receipts` | Captured receipts | device-local write log |
| `expiry_tracking` | Per-receipt expiry timeline | device-local write log |
| `activity_log` | Audit trail | device-local write log |
| `outbox` | Pending writes queue | device → server |

**AsyncStorage:**
- `@auxilio-session` — guard token + name + language (set on login, cleared on logout)
- `@auxilio-cache-time:<screen>` — last-fetch timestamp per screen namespace (catalog, stock, orders, alerts, home)
- Theme, i18n language

## When each cache refreshes

**On app boot** (`App.tsx:33`):
1. `getDb()` runs migrations.
2. `resetLocalCacheOnce()` wipes if a version sentinel changed.
3. `startSync()` — immediate `flushOnce()` + a 30-second timer + a network-reconnect listener.

**`flushOnce()` cycle** (every 30s while the app is open, plus on reconnect):
1. Recover any orphaned `sending` outbox rows.
2. Push the outbox batch.
3. **If logged in**, pull `canonical_products` (full replace) and open `orders`.
4. Stock is **not** pulled here — only on screen focus.

**On login** — and this is the gap worth calling out — there is **no eager fetch**. `login()` just stores the token. You then wait up to 30 seconds for the next `flushOnce()` tick to pull catalog + orders, and stock only loads when you open the Stock screen. This is why a freshly-logged-in user can stare at empty lists for half a minute.

**On screen focus** — each screen runs its own "stale?" check against `cacheTime.ts` (TTL = 30s):
- **StockScreen**: `flushOnce()` → if cache stale (>30s) `api.fetch.stock()` + `replaceStockFromRemote()`.
- **CatalogPickerScreen**: `useFocusEffect` → if cache stale, `syncCanonicalProducts()` + `syncOrders()`.
- **AlertsScreen / OrdersScreen / HomeScreen**: same pattern.
- **EditStockScreen / DispenseScreen / ReceivingScreen**: read local only, no fetch.

**Manual refresh:** pull-to-refresh on Stock/Orders/Alerts forces the fetch; "Sync Now" in the Outbox runs `flushOnce()`; the SyncRefreshButton on the Catalog Picker forces a catalog+orders pull.

**Never refreshed from server:**
- `receipts`, `expiry_tracking`, `activity_log` — these flow outbound only.
- `products` — legacy local-only table.
- `product_barcodes` — only populated by local `learnBarcode()`. Server-side mappings reach the device via `canonical_products.primary_barcode` (the fix we just shipped).

## The hole in the flow

The user-facing problem the question is hinting at: **login doesn't warm the cache**. Right now the flow is:

```
LoginScreen → setSession → Home renders → empty data for ~30s → first tick pulls catalog+orders → Stock screen still empty until you open it
```

The clean fix is to add a **post-login warm-up**: in `AuthProvider.login()`, after `setSession()`, await a one-shot `warmCache()` that runs in parallel:
- `syncCanonicalProducts()`
- `syncOrders()`
- `api.fetch.stock()` → `replaceStockFromRemote()`
- `api.fetch.alerts()` (optional — Alerts screen could keep its own fetch)

…and shows a "Loading…" or progress UI in place of letting the user reach an empty Home. If any of them fail, surface a recoverable error instead of silently leaving the cache empty.

Two design questions before I implement:

1. **Blocking vs. background:** block the user on the Login screen with a spinner until warm-up finishes? Or let them land on Home immediately and show per-tile skeletons until each piece arrives? I'd vote blocking (it's a one-time cost per session and avoids the "empty screen → suddenly populated" jank that's worse on low-end devices).

2. **Offline login:** if the warm-up fetch fails (no network), do we (a) refuse the login, (b) accept it and let the user work on stale local data, or (c) accept only if local cache exists, otherwise refuse? The current code accepts every login regardless, so (b) is least surprising; (c) is safer.

Want me to ship the warm-up? If yes, which option on each?

> DEVELOPER

1 : let them login and show the skeleton. 
2 : offline login would not work, we need proper login. 
Lets plan a proper implementation first, I want that whenever the user goes to no barcode option, they see the list of items from the catalog, they should not be required to refresh or resync the list to get the list in which the latest data of items like linked to barcode and all is present. Also in stock page, no page in the app should have stale data, we need to ensure it. how can we achieve it with smooth UX and fool proof implementation, where nothing breaks.

> AGENT

Good — "plan first" is the right call here, this touches a lot. Here's the design.

## Principle: stale-while-revalidate + just-in-time verification

Two layers, both required:

- **Background freshness (cheap, runs on every focus):** render local cache instantly, always refetch in the background, smoothly swap. Screen is never blank if cache exists.
- **Just-in-time verification (the foolproof part):** for actions that *commit* state based on what the user is looking at (catalog pick, stock edit, dispense), do a single-row fetch right before the action. Without this, you can never guarantee freshness — a 1-second-old cache is still stale if the admin linked a barcode 500ms ago.

Background freshness gives a smooth UX. JIT verification is what makes it foolproof. Neither alone is enough.

## What changes, by area

### 1. Post-login warm-up (skeleton, not blocking)

In `AuthProvider.login()`, after `setSession()`:
- Fire `warmCache()` async (don't await — user lands on Home immediately).
- `warmCache()` = parallel `syncCanonicalProducts()` + `syncOrders()` + `api.fetch.stock()` + `api.fetch.alerts()`.
- Add a `useCacheStatus(key)` hook returning `'cold' | 'warm' | 'refreshing' | 'error' | 'offline'`. Home tiles + list screens render `Skeleton` for `cold`, real data otherwise. Already half-built — extend `cacheTime.ts` with a status field.

### 2. Drop the 30s TTL gate; always refetch on focus

`isCacheStale()` is the wrong primitive — it leads to "I just opened the screen, it's not stale yet, no fetch" which is exactly the bug class you want gone.

Replace with: every focus triggers a refetch. The TTL becomes a *throttle* (don't refetch if a fetch already fired <2s ago) instead of a *gate*. Result: open → see data → it refreshes → smooth swap.

Show a subtle "Refreshing…" indicator (existing SyncRefreshButton can spin) so the user knows something's happening but it doesn't block them.

### 3. Catalog picker — guaranteed-fresh pick

`CatalogPickerScreen`:
- On focus: render local cache + fire `syncCanonicalProducts()` always (per #2).
- **On `pick(product)`:** before navigating to ReceivingScreen, fire `GET /api/inventory/canonical-products/:id` (new single-row endpoint). Use that response's `primary_barcode`, `unit`, `pack_size`, `canonical_name` for the nav params.
- If the JIT fetch succeeds: navigate with verified data. The cached row was the visual hint; the freshly-fetched row is what actually commits.
- If the JIT fetch fails (offline / 5xx): show "Couldn't verify product — try again when online" and **block the pick**. Don't fall back to stale data, because that's how the bug you reported happens.

This is the single most important change for the no-barcode flow you described.

### 4. Stock and edit screens — JIT for the same reason

- **StockScreen** on focus: render local + refetch (per #2).
- **EditStockScreen** on open: render local `on_hand` + `nearest_expiry` immediately so the steppers don't lag, **and** fire a single-row `GET /api/inventory/stock/:barcode` in parallel. If the fetched count differs from local, show inline: "Stock has been updated to N since you opened this screen. Use new count? [Yes / Keep my edit]". User decides; nothing silently overwrites.
- **DispenseScreen** on item-select: same JIT fetch. Critical because dispensing 10 from a stock of 8 is a real bug.

### 5. Post-write invalidation (closes the 30s gap)

Right now if you dispense, the local cache reflects it (optimistic) but the next other-device sync waits up to 30s. We can't fix the *other* device without server push, but we can fix this device:

In `flushOnce()`, after a successful receipt/issue/correction, refetch the affected caches (stock for all three; orders for receipt). Means: after submitting, the next screen open sees the server's view, not just your optimistic write.

### 6. Connectivity-aware UX

- A thin global "Offline" banner when `Network.getNetworkStateAsync().isConnected === false`. Persistent until network returns.
- All JIT fetches fail-closed (block the action) when offline. Reads stay open from cache.
- Login already requires network (your rule #2). Add a clear error message ("Login needs internet") when the network call itself fails, distinct from "wrong PIN".

### 7. Auto-logout on 401 (the warning-spam fix from earlier)

Still needs to ship as part of this work. Any authed request that comes back 401 calls `clearSession()`. Without this, "stale data" includes "stale auth" and the warm-up loop spins forever.

### 8. Backend change required

One new endpoint:
- `GET /api/inventory/canonical-products/:productId` — single row with `has_barcode` + `primary_barcode` computed live (not cached). Used by the catalog picker's JIT verification.

The single-row stock endpoint already kind of exists via `findStock` but isn't exposed; needs `GET /api/inventory/stock/:barcode`. Trivial to add.

## What I'm explicitly NOT proposing now

- **WebSocket/SSE for server push.** This would eliminate the polling window entirely (server tells device "catalog changed, refetch"), but it's a real architecture change — auth on the socket, reconnection logic, fan-out from write paths. Worth doing when you outgrow polling; not today.
- **Incremental catalog sync** (only changed rows since last sync). Worth it when catalog crosses ~1k items. Today's full-replace is fine.
- **Cross-device awareness.** None of this fixes "Guard A on tablet sees stale data while Guard B on phone just dispensed". Only server push solves that. JIT verification on Guard A's *next action* catches it, which is the bar I think we're shooting for.

## State machine per cache

Every cache (`catalog`, `stock`, `orders`, `alerts`) carries one of:

```
cold        → never fetched this session → Skeleton
warm        → fetched once → show data, no indicator
refreshing  → warm + fetch in flight → show data + spinner
error       → last fetch failed → show data + retry banner
offline     → network down → show data + offline banner
```

Render is always non-blocking — `cold` is the only state that hides real content, and only because there's nothing to show.

## Risk: where this could break

- **JIT fetch latency on slow Wi-Fi.** Pick → 2-second wait → frustration. Mitigation: show a tiny inline spinner on the tapped row, time out at 3s, fall back to "Couldn't verify" message. Need to measure on actual warehouse Wi-Fi.
- **Server overhead.** Two extra single-row endpoints, hit once per pick / per edit-open. Should be fine; these are point-lookup queries on indexed columns. Worth verifying after wiring.
- **User who taps very fast.** Pick → JIT fires → user is already on ReceivingScreen by the time it comes back. Solution: don't navigate until JIT resolves (with the inline spinner). UX trade-off but it's the foolproof choice you asked for.

---

## Order of implementation (if you approve)

1. Backend: add `GET /api/inventory/canonical-products/:id` and `GET /api/inventory/stock/:barcode`.
2. Field-app: 401 auto-clear (kills the spam now).
3. Field-app: `warmCache()` on login + `useCacheStatus()` hook + Home/Stock skeletons.
4. Field-app: drop `isCacheStale` gate, replace with always-refetch-on-focus + throttle.
5. Field-app: JIT verification in CatalogPickerScreen `pick()`.
6. Field-app: JIT verification in EditStockScreen + DispenseScreen.
7. Field-app: post-write invalidation in `flushOnce()`.
8. Field-app: offline banner + JIT fail-closed when offline.

Each step is independently shippable and testable. I'd merge them as one logical change but build in this order so a partial state still works.

---

Three things to confirm before I start:

1. **JIT-blocking on pick:** spinner on the tapped row until the single-row fetch resolves, then navigate. You good with that small delay being the price of "guaranteed fresh"? (My recommendation: yes.)
2. **EditStock conflict prompt:** when the JIT fetch shows stock changed since you opened the screen, show an inline "Use new count? [Yes / Keep my edit]" prompt. Or just always show the latest and ditch the prompt?
3. **Offline reads:** today, screens show cached data with no warning when offline. After this work, do you want the offline banner *plus* a per-screen "Showing data from {timestamp}" line, or just the global banner?
