> AGENT

Honest answer on all three.

## SSE — CPU and 100-user scale

**Idle CPU cost is negligible.** SSE = each client holds a long-lived HTTP/1.1 connection. On Bun/Node, an idle connection is one file descriptor in `epoll`/`kqueue`, one TLS session, and a few small JS objects. For 100 connections:

- ~10-50 MB RAM total
- <1% CPU at rest
- ~100 file descriptors

This is *nothing*. A single Bun/Node process can hold ~10k idle SSE connections comfortably. 100 is two orders of magnitude under that.

**The actual cost is the fan-out on writes.** When a receipt/dispense/correction is inserted, the server needs to push an event to every connected device on that site. Per push: ~200 bytes of JSON, sub-millisecond CPU.

Realistic load for you: roughly 200 writes/hour across 100 guards → 20,000 pushes/hour → ~5.5 pushes/second across all connections. Trivial.

Peak burst (a delivery being received, 50 scans in 2 minutes): without debouncing, 50 × 100 = 5000 pushes in 2 min. With **per-topic debouncing** (coalesce changes in a 500ms window into one "refetch hint"), it drops to ~10 × 100 = 1000 pushes. Still nothing.

**The cost is not what you'll trip on. Three other things are:**

1. **Bifrost ingress.** nginx/Cloudflare in front of `ops-engine` may buffer or close long-lived connections by default. Need `X-Accel-Buffering: no`, `Cache-Control: no-cache`, bumped `proxy_read_timeout`, and a heartbeat write every 15-30s to keep proxies happy. Must verify on Bifrost dev before committing to SSE.
2. **Multi-process pub/sub.** If `ops-engine` ever scales beyond one process (it's single right now), writes in process A must fan out to clients on process B. Cheapest backbone is **Postgres `LISTEN/NOTIFY`** — you already have Postgres, no new infra. Redis pub/sub if you outgrow LISTEN/NOTIFY's payload limit (~8KB per notification).
3. **Reconnection storm.** When `ops-engine` restarts, all 100 clients reconnect simultaneously. Need jittered backoff on the client (random 0-5s delay before retry) to avoid hammering the server. Easy to add, easy to forget.

## SSE — will it handle 100 staff?

Yes, comfortably. Even 1000 would be fine on the current `ops-engine` footprint. The constraint isn't connection count, it's the items above (ingress config, pub/sub if you go multi-process, mobile reconnect logic).

**Mobile-specific gotchas:**

- React Native doesn't have a stock `EventSource`. You need `react-native-sse` (actively maintained, decent) or roll your own with `fetch` + `ReadableStream`. The first option is fine.
- Android suspends network when the app backgrounds. The connection will silently die after some seconds. You need to detect close and reconnect on foreground. SSE's built-in `Last-Event-ID` header lets the server replay missed events from a small in-memory buffer, so users don't miss anything during a 30s background.
- Token expiry on a long-lived stream. Server must close the stream with a clear reason code on 401-equivalent; client treats it the same as the polling 401 → clear session, kick to Login.
- Battery: SSE on Wi-Fi is essentially free. On cellular, the always-open socket prevents the radio from deep-sleeping, but it's still less battery than 30s polling.

## SSE — does it really fix cross-device staleness?

For **online** guards, yes. Guard B dispenses → server inserts → server emits `{ site_id, topic: "stock", barcode: "X" }` → Guard A's device receives within ms → device refetches stock for barcode X → screen updates.

For **offline** guards, no — they need JIT verification on their next action when they come back online. **SSE does not replace JIT.** It complements it:

- SSE closes the polling window for online users (the ~30s lag is gone).
- JIT closes the gap for offline users and for the narrow race between "I see fresh data" and "I commit an action" (which can still be milliseconds even with SSE).

You need both. The good news: with SSE in place, JIT fetches almost always return "no change" — it becomes a safety net, not the primary freshness mechanism.

## Incremental catalog sync — yes, easy

Backend: `GET /api/inventory/canonical-products?since=<ISO>` returns rows where `updated_at > since`. Client tracks the max `updated_at` from the last sync and sends it next time.

**Edge case to handle:** deletes. Full-replace catches them implicitly (rows missing from the response get dropped locally). Incremental sync misses them.

Two clean options:

1. **Periodic full-replace as reconciliation.** Run incremental sync most of the time; once an hour, run a full sync to catch deletes and recover from any drift.
2. **Tombstones.** Add `deleted_at` to `canonical_products`. Incremental sync returns soft-deleted rows so the client can drop them. Cleaner but needs a schema change and write-path edits.

I'd ship **#1** — it's a 5-line change on the client and zero schema work.

## Cost summary

| Feature | Backend cost | Mobile cost | Engineering effort |
|---|---|---|---|
| SSE for 100 users | Negligible | Slight battery hit on cellular; needs library + reconnect logic | 2-3 days focused + ~1 week soaking on real devices |
| Incremental catalog sync | One extra query column in WHERE | None | ~2 hours |
| Cross-device freshness without SSE | None extra | None | Not achievable without SSE/WebSocket |

## My recommendation

Ship the JIT verification plan first (steps 1-8 from the planning doc) — it's the foolproof layer and it works without SSE. Once that's solid and you've measured real warehouse Wi-Fi latency on JIT calls, layer SSE on top to eliminate the polling window. Incremental catalog sync can come in parallel; it's small and independent.

The risk with going SSE-first is that the mobile reconnect logic is genuinely tricky on Android, and if it's flaky, you've made freshness *worse* than the current polling. JIT gives you a correctness floor first, then SSE makes the UX feel real-time on top of that floor.

Want me to add an SSE section + the incremental-sync plan to the .md file?

SSE on top of it eliminates the polling window and makes the UX feel real-time. **Ship JIT first**, measure real-Wi-Fi latency, then layer SSE.\n\n### Will it scale to 100 staff?\n\nYes, comfortably. Idle SSE connection cost is essentially zero:\n\n- Each connection: ~one file descriptor, one TLS session, a small JS object, a periodic heartbeat write.\n- 100 connections: ~10-50 MB RAM total, <1% CPU at rest.\n- A single Bun/Node process holds ~10k idle SSE connections without straining. 100 is two orders of magnitude under that.\n\nThe real cost is **fan-out on writes**. Realistic load:\n\n- ~200 writes/hour across 100 guards → ~20k pushes/hour → ~5.5 pushes/second. Each push ~200 bytes JSON, sub-ms CPU. Trivial.\n- Peak burst (delivery scan, 50 receipts in 2 minutes): without debouncing 5000 pushes; with per-topic debouncing (coalesce changes in a 500 ms window into one \"refetch hint\") drops to ~1000. Still nothing.\n\n### What it does NOT fix\n\nSSE only helps **online** guards. Offline guards still need JIT verification on their next action when they reconnect. **SSE complements JIT; it does not replace it.** With SSE in place, JIT fetches usually return \"no change\" and become a safety net instead of the primary freshness mechanism.\n\n### What we need to do for SSE\n\n1. **Pub/sub backbone.** Use Postgres `LISTEN/NOTIFY` — already have Postgres, no new infra. Move to Redis if a notification ever needs to be >8 KB (it won't for our hint-only events). Required if `ops-engine` ever scales beyond one process; cheap to do upfront anyway.\n2. **Bifrost ingress configuration.** Verify SSE survives the proxy. Required:\n   - `X-Accel-Buffering: no` (response header)\n   - `Cache-Control: no-cache` (response header)\n   - Bumped `proxy_read_timeout` on the ingress\n   - Heartbeat write every 15-30 s to keep proxies from closing the stream\n   - Must be tested on Bifrost dev before committing.\n3. **Mobile client.** `react-native-sse` (actively maintained) or a hand-rolled `fetch` + `ReadableStream`. The first is fine.\n4. **Reconnect logic.**\n   - **Jittered backoff** on reconnect (random 0-5 s) — prevents 100 clients hammering the server simultaneously when `ops-engine` restarts.\n   - **Foreground reconnect** — Android suspends network when the app backgrounds; the connection silently dies. Detect on foreground and reconnect.\n   - **`Last-Event-ID` resume** — server keeps a small in-memory ring buffer of recent events per site; on reconnect the client sends `Last-Event-ID` and server replays anything missed in the gap. Prevents missed events during ~30 s backgrounding.\n5. **Auth on the stream.** Carry the session token in the URL or initial connect. On token expiry, server closes the stream with a recognisable reason; client treats it like the polling 401 — clear session, kick to Login.\n6. **Write-path integration.** Every write path (receipt, issue, dispense, correction, catalog upsert, barcode learn) calls a small `emitChange(siteId, topic, payload)` helper after `COMMIT`. Topics: `stock`, `orders`, `catalog`, `alerts`. Payload is a hint only (\"stock changed for barcode X\"), not the new value — client refetches.\n7. **Client dispatcher.** SSE messages invalidate the matching cache; TanStack-style \"subscribe to topic\" hook so screens auto-refetch when their topic fires.\n\n### What it costs\n\n| Concern | Cost |\n|---|---|\n| Server CPU idle | <1% |\n| Server RAM | 10-50 MB for 100 connections |\n| Mobile battery on Wi-Fi | Negligible (less than 30 s polling) |\n| Mobile battery on cellular | Slight (radio can't deep-sleep) |\n| Mobile data | Minimal — heartbeats + small JSON hints |\n| Engineering effort | 2-3 days focused work + ~1 week soaking on real device fleet to shake out reconnect bugs |\n\n### Real risks\n\n- **Bifrost ingress closes streams.** Most common SSE deployment failure. Verify before commitment.\n- **Reconnect storm on restart.** Without jittered backoff, all clients reconnect at once. Easy to add, easy to forget.\n- **Flaky Android reconnect could make freshness WORSE than current polling.** This is why JIT ships first — it gives a correctness floor SSE can fail back to.\n- **Token rotation mid-stream.** If guard PINs rotate or sessions expire on the server, mid-stream auth failures need a clean recovery path. Same handler as the 401 auto-clear.\n\n## Phase 2: Incremental catalog sync\n\nTrivial to add and orthogonal to the rest. Can ship in parallel with SSE.\n\n- Backend: `GET /api/inventory/canonical-products?since=<ISO>` returns rows where `updated_at > since`.\n- Client: track the max `updated_at` from the last sync; send it next time.\n\n**Edge case: deletes.** Full-replace catches them implicitly (rows missing from the response get dropped locally). Incremental sync misses them.\n\nTwo options:\n\n1. **Periodic full-replace as reconciliation.** Run incremental sync most of the time; once an hour, run a full sync to catch deletes and recover from any drift. *5-line client change, zero schema work.*\n2. **Tombstones.** Add `deleted_at` to `canonical_products`; incremental sync returns soft-deleted rows so the client drops them. Cleaner but needs schema + write-path edits.\n\n**Ship #1.** Move to #2 only if drift becomes a real problem at scale.\n\n## Explicitly out of scope (still)\n\n- **WebSocket** (vs SSE). SSE is one-way (server → client), which is exactly what we need (hints to refetch). WebSocket adds bidirectional capability we don't use, plus more reconnection complexity. Stick with SSE.\n- **Server-side caching of catalog responses.** Premature optimisation; the queries are cheap."
}
```

@Planning-docs/field_app_cache_freshness_plan.md read through the doc, and tell me what things we are implementing, how we are implementing, and what things would change.

> AGENT

Here's a synthesis of the doc, in the three lenses you asked for.

## What we're implementing

**Phase 1 — JIT + warm-up (correctness floor).** Eight items:

1. Two new backend endpoints for single-row lookups (catalog and stock).
2. 401 auto-clear in the field-app's API client (stops the warning spam from the dead-token loop).
3. Post-login cache warm-up + a state machine (`cold/warm/refreshing/error/offline`) per cache + skeletons on cold screens.
4. Replace the 30-second TTL gate with "always refetch on focus, throttled".
5. JIT verification in the catalog picker — single-row fetch right before navigating to ReceivingScreen.
6. JIT verification in EditStock and Dispense screens — single-row fetch on open, with a conflict prompt if the count moved.
7. Post-write invalidation — when the outbox successfully flushes a receipt/issue/dispense/correction, the device immediately refetches the affected caches instead of waiting for the next timer tick.
8. Global "Offline" banner + JIT fails-closed when offline.

**Phase 2 — SSE + incremental sync (real-time + scale).** Ships only after Phase 1 has soaked for ~1 week. Seven items:

9. Incremental catalog sync (`?since=<ISO>`) + hourly full-replace reconciliation for deletes.
10. Verify SSE survives Bifrost ingress (this is a go/no-go check, not work).
11. Backend `emitChange()` helper on every write path.
12. Backend SSE endpoint with Postgres `LISTEN/NOTIFY` backbone + `Last-Event-ID` resume.
13. Field-app `react-native-sse` client with jittered backoff, foreground reconnect, resume.
14. Client dispatcher that maps SSE topic → cache invalidation → focus-path refetch.
15. Tear down the 30s polling timer once SSE is proven (keep as fallback for 2-4 weeks).

## How we're implementing it (the mechanism)

**Two-layer design.** Background freshness gives smooth UX; JIT gives correctness. Neither alone suffices.

- **Background freshness** = stale-while-revalidate. Render the local SQLite cache instantly, fire a refetch in parallel, smoothly swap. Throttle (skip if a fetch fired in the last ~2s) replaces the TTL gate (skip if data is "young enough"). This change inverts the current logic — instead of "is the cache stale?" we ask "is a fetch already in flight?".

- **JIT verification** = single-row server fetch right before any committing action. This is the foolproof layer. Examples:
  - Catalog pick → `GET /api/inventory/canonical-products/:id` → use the response's `primary_barcode` / `unit` / `pack_size` for the navigation params, ignore the cached row's values for those fields.
  - Edit Stock open → `GET /api/inventory/stock/:barcode` → if server count ≠ cached count, surface the conflict prompt before user can save.
  - Dispense item-select → same as above; blocks "dispense 10 from stock of 8".
  - JIT fail-closed when offline. Reads still come from cache; *actions* block with "Couldn't verify — try again when online".

**Post-write invalidation.** Today `flushOnce()` pushes the outbox and may pull catalog/orders but doesn't refetch stock after our own writes. Change: after each successful dispatch of a write that affects shared state, refetch the matching cache(s). One device's writes immediately propagate to its own screens; other devices catch up via JIT on their next action (or instantly via SSE in Phase 2).

**State machine per cache.** Every cache (`catalog`, `stock`, `orders`, `alerts`) is in one of five states, computed by the new `useCacheStatus(key)` hook. `cold` is the only state that hides real content (it triggers Skeleton); every other state shows data with at most a subtle indicator. Renders are never blocked.

**401 auto-clear.** Centralised in the API client's `request()` function. Any authed request returning 401 calls `clearSession()`, which `AuthProvider` already watches via `onSessionChange`; `RootNavigator` then re-renders into `LoginScreen`. One exception: requests with no session token (i.e. the login call itself) must not trigger the clear — login failures surface as `ApiError` to the LoginScreen as today.

**SSE (Phase 2).** Server emits hints, not data — payloads are tiny ("stock changed for barcode X"), the client refetches. Postgres `LISTEN/NOTIFY` is the pub/sub backbone (no new infra). Per-topic debouncing (500ms window) coalesces bursts. `Last-Event-ID` + in-memory ring buffer handles backgrounded reconnects without missing events. Jittered backoff on reconnect prevents storms when ops-engine restarts.

## What changes (file-by-file impact)

### office-ops-engine

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `src/mastra/modules/inventory/routes.ts` | Add `GET /api/inventory/canonical-products/:productId` (single row, live `primary_barcode`). Add `GET /api/inventory/stock/:barcode` (single row, nearest expiry included). |
| `src/mastra/modules/inventory/db.ts` | Add `findCanonicalProductByIdFull(siteId, productId)` that returns the same `CANONICAL_PRODUCT_COLUMNS` shape (with `has_barcode` + `primary_barcode`) as the list query — single row instead of all. Add `findStockWithExpiry(siteId, barcode)` joining nearest expiry from receipts. |

Phase 2 adds:

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `src/mastra/modules/inventory/routes.ts` | Add `GET /api/inventory/canonical-products?since=<ISO>` (incremental). Add `GET /api/inventory/events?siteId=...&token=...` (SSE stream). |
| `src/mastra/modules/inventory/events.ts` (new) | `emitChange(siteId, topic, payload)` helper + `LISTEN/NOTIFY` subscription manager + per-connection ring buffer for `Last-Event-ID` resume. |
| Write-path handlers (receipts, issues, mismatch-flags, corrections, learn-barcode, canonical upserts) | Call `emitChange()` after the DB commit. |

### field-app

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `src/sync/api.ts` | Central 401 handler in `request()` → `clearSession()` if a token was present. Add `api.fetch.canonicalProduct(id)` and `api.fetch.stockOne(barcode)`. |
| `src/sync/cacheTime.ts` | Drop `isCacheStale()`. Replace with `getInFlightFetch(key)` / `setInFlightFetch(key, ts)` + a `THROTTLE_MS = 2000` guard. Per-cache state machine helpers. |
| `src/sync/warmCache.ts` (new) | Orchestrates the post-login parallel fetch. Updates per-cache status as each completes. |
| `src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx` | After `setSession()` in `login()`, fire `warmCache()` (no await). User navigates to Home immediately. |
| `src/hooks/useCacheStatus.ts` (new) | Returns `'cold' \| 'warm' \| 'refreshing' \| 'error' \| 'offline'` for a given key. Subscribed by every list screen. |
| `src/modules/inventory/screens/StockScreen.tsx` | Drop the `isCacheStale` check in `load()`. Always call `fetchRemote` on focus (throttled). Render Skeleton when status is `cold`, "Refreshing…" indicator when `refreshing`. |
| `src/modules/inventory/screens/CatalogPickerScreen.tsx` | Same focus pattern. `pick(product)` becomes async: show inline spinner on the tapped row, call `api.fetch.canonicalProduct(id)`, use response fields for nav params, fail-closed with a toast if it errors. 3s timeout. |
| `src/modules/inventory/screens/EditStockScreen.tsx` | On open, fire `api.fetch.stockOne(barcode)` in parallel with the local read. If server `on_hand` ≠ local `originalQty`, show the inline "Use new count?" prompt. Same for `nearest_expiry`. |
| `src/modules/inventory/screens/DispenseScreen.tsx` | Same JIT pattern on item-select. Block proceeding until the fetch resolves; recompute the `max` on the QtyStepper from the verified count. |
| `src/sync/syncService.ts` | In `flushOnce()`, after each successful dispatch of `receipt`/`issue`/`dispense`/`stock_correction`, refetch the affected caches (stock for all four; orders for receipt). |
| `src/components/OfflineBanner.tsx` (new) | Thin global banner shown whenever `Network.getNetworkStateAsync().isConnected === false`. Mounted in `RootNavigator` above the navigator. |
| `src/i18n/strings.ts` | New keys: `offline`/`offlineBanner`, `refreshing`, `couldntVerify`, `stockUpdatedPrompt`, `useNewCount`, `keepMyEdit`. |

Phase 2 adds:

| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `src/sync/events.ts` (new) | `react-native-sse` client. Manages connection, jittered backoff (0-5s), foreground reconnect, `Last-Event-ID` persistence in AsyncStorage. |
| `src/sync/eventDispatch.ts` (new) | Maps SSE topic → cache invalidation. Triggers the same focus-path refetch as a manual focus would. |
| `App.tsx` | Start the SSE client alongside `startSync()`. |
| Catalog sync code | Use `since` parameter when last sync timestamp is known; full-replace once an hour. |

## What does NOT change

- **SQLite schema.** No migrations needed. The `primary_barcode` column from the prior fix is already in place. All freshness work is in-flight cache plumbing, not persisted state.
- **The outbox.** Writes still go through the existing optimistic-then-flush pattern. Only addition is the post-write invalidation hook.
- **The login flow itself.** `api.login()` stays the same; warm-up is just an extra side effect of `AuthProvider.login()` after `setSession()`.
- **The existing 30s polling timer** stays in Phase 1 (it's now a safety net under always-refetch-on-focus). Only torn down in Phase 2 after SSE is proven.
- **Dashboard and ops-engine architecture.** Two thin endpoints added; everything else untouched.

## Gating: open decisions still pending

These are at the bottom of the doc and block step 5 / step 6:

1. **JIT-blocking on catalog pick** — spinner on the tapped row, navigate only after fetch resolves. (Doc recommends yes.)
2. **EditStock conflict prompt** vs silent update to latest. (Doc recommends prompt.)
3. **Offline reads UX** — global banner only, or also per-screen "Showing data from {timestamp}" line. (Doc recommends global only.)

Confirm those three and I can start on step 1 (the two backend endpoints) immediately.

  "old_string": "export async function findCanonicalProduct(\n  productId: string,\n): Promise<CanonicalProduct | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `SELECT * FROM canonical_products WHERE product_id = ? LIMIT 1`,\n    args: [productId],\n  });\n  return (result.rows[0] as CanonicalProduct | undefined) ?? null;\n}",
  "new_string": "export async function findCanonicalProduct(\n  productId: string,\n): Promise<CanonicalProduct | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `SELECT * FROM canonical_products WHERE product_id = ? LIMIT 1`,\n    args: [productId],\n  });\n  return (result.rows[0] as CanonicalProduct | undefined) ?? null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Single-row catalog read with live-computed `has_barcode` and\n * `primary_barcode` — same shape as `listCanonicalProducts`. Used by the\n * field-app's JIT verification on catalog pick. Site-scoped so a\n * mis-typed productId from another site returns null rather than the\n * wrong product.\n */\nexport async function findCanonicalProductFull(\n  siteId: string,\n  productId: string,\n): Promise<CanonicalProduct | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT ${CANONICAL_PRODUCT_COLUMNS}\n      FROM canonical_products cp\n      WHERE cp.site_id = ? AND cp.product_id = ?\n      LIMIT 1\n    `,\n    args: [siteId, productId],\n  });\n  return (result.rows[0] as CanonicalProduct | undefined) ?? null;\n}"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/inventory/db.ts",
  "old_string": "export async function findStock(\n  siteId: string,\n  barcode: string,\n): Promise<InventoryStockRow | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `SELECT * FROM inventory_stock WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? LIMIT 1`,\n    args: [siteId, barcode],\n  });\n  return (result.rows[0] as InventoryStockRow | undefined) ?? null;\n}",
  "new_string": "export async function findStock(\n  siteId: string,\n  barcode: string,\n): Promise<InventoryStockRow | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `SELECT * FROM inventory_stock WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? LIMIT 1`,\n    args: [siteId, barcode],\n  });\n  return (result.rows[0] as InventoryStockRow | undefined) ?? null;\n}\n\nexport type InventoryStockJitRow = InventoryStockRow & {\n  /** ISO YYYY-MM-DD; min of future receipt expiries for this barcode.\n   *  Null if none on file. Best-effort — corrections do not feed back into\n   *  this today, which matches the field-app's local convention. */\n  nearest_expiry: string | null;\n};\n\n/**\n * Single-row stock + pack_size + nearest-future-expiry. Used by the\n * field-app's JIT verification on EditStock / Dispense screens. The\n * stock row itself is authoritative; the expiry is a hint.\n */\nexport async function findStockJit(\n  siteId: string,\n  barcode: string,\n): Promise<InventoryStockJitRow | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT ${STOCK_SELECT},\n        (\n          SELECT to_char(MIN(r.expiry_date), 'YYYY-MM-DD')\n          FROM inventory_receipts r\n          WHERE r.site_id = s.site_id\n            AND r.barcode = s.barcode\n            AND r.expiry_date IS NOT NULL\n            AND r.expiry_date >= CURRENT_DATE\n        ) AS nearest_expiry\n      ${STOCK_JOINS}\n      WHERE s.site_id = ? AND s.barcode = ?\n      LIMIT 1\n    `,\n    args: [siteId, barcode],\n  });\n  return (result.rows[0] as InventoryStockJitRow | undefined) ?? null;\n}"
}

  "new_string": "  // JIT verification — single-row catalog read with live-computed\n  // `has_barcode` and `primary_barcode`. Called by the field-app's\n  // catalog picker right before navigating to ReceivingScreen, so the\n  // user is committing against the freshest server state (catches\n  // barcode mappings learned by another guard / admin / bill flow that\n  // hadn't been pulled by the periodic sync yet).\n  //\n  // Slug must be a bare product_id (no further slashes) and not one of\n  // the exact-match siblings (`/barcode`, `/learn-barcode`) which are\n  // handled above with their own routes.\n  if (\n    pathname.startsWith(\"/api/inventory/canonical-products/\") &&\n    method === \"GET\"\n  ) {\n    const slug = pathname.replace(\"/api/inventory/canonical-products/\", \"\");\n    if (\n      slug.length > 0 &&\n      !slug.includes(\"/\") &&\n      slug !== \"barcode\" &&\n      slug !== \"learn-barcode\"\n    ) {\n      const auth = await authenticate(req, res);\n      if (!auth) return true;\n      const product = await findCanonicalProductFull(auth.siteId, slug);\n      if (!product) {\n        json(res, 404, { error: `Catalog product \"${slug}\" not found on this site` });\n        return true;\n      }\n      json(res, 200, { product });\n      return true;\n    }\n    // Slug shape didn't match — fall through so the `/aliases` and\n    // `/barcodes` sub-routes below can match.\n  }\n\n  // ── Compliance: date-wise audit log of received items ────────\n  // GET /api/inventory/compliance?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD"

1	/**
2	 * Session store for the guard's PIN-based login.
3	 *
4	 * Persists `{ token, guardId, guardName, language }` in AsyncStorage and
5	 * notifies subscribers when it changes. Network code (`sync/api.ts`)
6	 * reads the current token; the navigation root subscribes to know
7	 * whether to render the Login screen or the main stack.
8	 */
9	
10	import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
11	
12	const STORAGE_KEY = 'app.session';
13	
14	export type GuardSession = {
15	  token: string;
16	  guardId: string;
17	  guardName: string;
18	  language: 'hindi' | 'english';
19	};
20	
21	type Listener = (session: GuardSession | null) => void;
22	
23	let current: GuardSession | null = null;
24	let hydrated = false;
25	const listeners = new Set<Listener>();
26	
27	export async function hydrateSession(): Promise<GuardSession | null> {
28	  if (hydrated) return current;
29	  hydrated = true;
30	  try {
31	    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
32	    if (raw) current = JSON.parse(raw) as GuardSession;
33	  } catch {
34	    current = null;
35	  }
36	  notify();
37	  return current;
38	}
39	
40	export function getSession(): GuardSession | null {
41	  return current;
42	}
43	
44	export async function setSession(s: GuardSession): Promise<void> {
45	  current = s;
46	  await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(s));
47	  notify();
48	}
49	
50	export async function clearSession(): Promise<void> {
51	  current = null;
52	  await AsyncStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
53	  notify();
54	}
55	
56	export function onSessionChange(l: Listener): () => void {
57	  listeners.add(l);
58	  l(current);
59	  return () => listeners.delete(l);
60	}
61	
62	function notify() {
63	  for (const l of listeners) l(current);
64	}
65	
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/sync/api.ts",
  "old_string": "async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {\n  const deviceId = await getDeviceId();\n  const session = getSession();\n  const headers: Record<string, string> = {\n    'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n    'X-Device-Id': deviceId,\n    'X-Site-Id': config.siteId,\n    ...((init?.headers as Record<string, string>) ?? {}),\n  };\n  if (session?.token) headers['X-Guard-Token'] = session.token;\n\n  const res = await fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}${path}`, { ...init, headers });\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');\n    const body = text.slice(0, 500);\n    throw new ApiError(res.status, ApiError.format(res.status, path, body), body);\n  }\n  return res.json() as Promise<T>;\n}",
  "new_string": "async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {\n  const deviceId = await getDeviceId();\n  const session = getSession();\n  const headers: Record<string, string> = {\n    'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n    'X-Device-Id': deviceId,\n    'X-Site-Id': config.siteId,\n    ...((init?.headers as Record<string, string>) ?? {}),\n  };\n  const sentToken = session?.token ?? null;\n  if (sentToken) headers['X-Guard-Token'] = sentToken;\n\n  const res = await fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}${path}`, { ...init, headers });\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');\n    const body = text.slice(0, 500);\n    // 401 with a sent token means the session expired or was revoked\n    // server-side. Clear it centrally so:\n    //  - the polling/warm-up loop stops spamming \"Invalid or expired\n    //    session\" warnings on every tick,\n    //  - the AuthProvider's onSessionChange listener fires and the\n    //    RootNavigator drops back to LoginScreen.\n    // Skip when no token was sent (the login call itself surfaces 401\n    // via ApiError to the LoginScreen; there's no session to clear).\n    if (res.status === 401 && sentToken) {\n      // Fire-and-forget — clearing is best-effort and shouldn't block\n      // the error path.\n      void clearSession().catch(() => {});\n    }\n    throw new ApiError(res.status, ApiError.format(res.status, path, body), body);\n  }\n  return res.json() as Promise<T>;\n}"
}

1	import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';

3	import { QueueBadge } from '../components/QueueBadge';

5	import { RefreshControl, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
6	import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
7	import { Button, Card, IconButton, Skeleton, spacing, Text } from '../../../design';
8	import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
9	import { getSession } from '../../../auth/session';
10	import { listOpenOrders, upsertOrdersFromRemote } from '../../../db/orders';
11	import { listLowOrOut, listStock, replaceStockFromRemote } from '../../../db/stock';
12	import { useT } from '../../../i18n';
13	import { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';
14	import { api } from '../../../sync/api';

19	
20	type Stats = {

22	  stock: number;
23	  alerts: number;
24	};
25	
26	export function HomeScreen({ navigation }: Props) {
27	  const t = useT();
28	  const { palette } = useTheme();
29	  const [stats, setStats] = useState<Stats | null>(null);
30	  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
31	
32	  const fetchRemote = useCallback(async () => {
33	    await flushOnce().catch(() => {});
34	
35	    try {
36	      const remote = await api.fetch.stock();
37	      await replaceStockFromRemote(
38	        remote.map((r) => ({
39	          barcode: r.barcode,
40	          name: r.name,
41	          category: r.category,
42	          unit: r.unit,
43	          pack_size: r.pack_size != null ? Number(r.pack_size) : null,
44	          on_hand: Number(r.on_hand),
45	          threshold: Number(r.threshold),
46	        })),
47	      );
48	    } catch {
49	      // offline — keep local cache
50	    }
51	
52	    try {
53	      const remoteOrders = await api.fetch.orders();
54	      await upsertOrdersFromRemote(remoteOrders).catch(() => {});
55	    } catch {
56	      // offline — keep local cache
57	    }
58	
59	    await setLastSyncTime('home');
60	  }, []);
61	
62	  const load = useCallback(async (forceRefresh = false) => {
63	    const lastSync = await getLastSyncTime('home');
64	    const stale = isCacheStale(lastSync);
65	
66	    if (forceRefresh || stale) {
67	      await fetchRemote();
68	    }
69	
70	    const [stock, openOrders, alerts] = await Promise.all([
71	      listStock(),
72	      listOpenOrders(),
73	      listLowOrOut(),
74	    ]);
75	    setStats({ stock: stock.length, orders: openOrders.length, alerts: alerts.length });
76	  }, [fetchRemote]);
77	
78	  useEffect(() => {
79	    load(false);
80	    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', () => load(false));
81	    return unsub;
82	  }, [navigation, load]);
83	
84	  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
85	    setRefreshing(true);
86	    try {
87	      await load(true);
88	    } finally {
89	      setRefreshing(false);
90	    }
91	  }, [load]);
92	
93	  return (

95	      <ScrollView
96	        contentContainerStyle={styles.scroll}
97	        refreshControl={
98	          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} tintColor={palette.primary} />
99	        }
100	      >
101	        <View style={styles.header}>
102	          <View style={styles.headerRow}>
103	            <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
104	              <Text variant="headlineMedium">
105	                {t('greeting')}, {getSession()?.guardName ?? ''}
106	              </Text>
107	              <Text variant="bodyLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
108	                {t('appName')}
109	              </Text>

113	        </View>

157	          />

186	  value?: number;

188	  sub: string;

192	  onPress: () => void;

196	  const { palette } = useTheme();
197	  return (

201	          {label.toUpperCase()}
202	        </Text>

204	          {trailing}

208	            strokeWidth={2.2}
209	          />

211	      </View>
212	      {loading ? (
213	        <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs, marginBottom: spacing.xs }}>
214	          <Skeleton width={64} height={36} />
215	        </View>
216	      ) : value !== undefined ? (
217	        <Text
218	          variant="displayMedium"
219	          color={tone === 'warn' ? palette.error : palette.onSurface}
220	          style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}
221	        >
222	          {value}
223	        </Text>
224	      ) : (
225	        <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />
226	      )}
227	      <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
228	        {sub}
229	      </Text>
230	    </Card>
231	  );
232	}
233	
234	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
235	  safe: { flex: 1 },
236	  scroll: { paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl },
237	  header: { paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl, paddingTop: spacing.lg, paddingBottom: spacing.xl },

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/sync/cacheStatus.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Per-cache render state.\n *\n * Every read screen (Home tiles, Stock list, Catalog picker, Orders,\n * Alerts) needs to know two things to render correctly:\n *\n *   1. Is there anything in local SQLite yet? (The screen reads this\n *      itself.)\n *   2. What is the network layer doing about it right now? (Driven by\n *      this module.)\n *\n * Five states cover every combination cleanly:\n *\n *   cold        Never fetched this session. Render Skeleton.\n *   warm        Fetched at least once; no fetch in flight. Render data.\n *   refreshing  Fetch in flight. If data exists, render it + indicator;\n *               if not, render Skeleton.\n *   error       Last fetch failed. Render data if any + retry banner;\n *               if not, render error placeholder.\n *   offline     Network down. Render data if any + offline banner;\n *               if not, render \"offline, no cache\".\n *\n * The store is module-singleton (no React context overhead) and notifies\n * subscribers per-key, so a Stock-state change does not re-render every\n * Home tile.\n */\n\nimport { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\n\nexport type CacheKey = 'catalog' | 'stock' | 'orders' | 'alerts';\n\nexport type CacheState = 'cold' | 'warm' | 'refreshing' | 'error' | 'offline';\n\nconst states = new Map<CacheKey, CacheState>();\ntype Listener = (state: CacheState) => void;\nconst listeners = new Map<CacheKey, Set<Listener>>();\n\nexport function getCacheState(key: CacheKey): CacheState {\n  return states.get(key) ?? 'cold';\n}\n\nexport function setCacheState(key: CacheKey, state: CacheState): void {\n  if (states.get(key) === state) return;\n  states.set(key, state);\n  const ls = listeners.get(key);\n  if (!ls) return;\n  for (const l of ls) {\n    try { l(state); } catch { /* listener errors must not break the notifier */ }\n  }\n}\n\nexport function onCacheStateChange(key: CacheKey, l: Listener): () => void {\n  let set = listeners.get(key);\n  if (!set) {\n    set = new Set();\n    listeners.set(key, set);\n  }\n  set.add(l);\n  return () => { set!.delete(l); };\n}\n\n/** React hook — re-renders the calling component when this key's state\n *  transitions. Initial value is the current state (no flash). */\nexport function useCacheStatus(key: CacheKey): CacheState {\n  const [s, setS] = useState<CacheState>(() => getCacheState(key));\n  useEffect(() => onCacheStateChange(key, setS), [key]);\n  return s;\n}\n\n/** Helpers callers use to drive the state machine. Centralised so the\n *  transitions stay consistent across warmCache, per-screen refetches,\n *  and post-write invalidation. */\nexport const cache = {\n  refreshing: (key: CacheKey) => setCacheState(key, 'refreshing'),\n  warm: (key: CacheKey) => setCacheState(key, 'warm'),\n  error: (key: CacheKey) => setCacheState(key, 'error'),\n  offline: (key: CacheKey) => setCacheState(key, 'offline'),\n};\n\n/** Reset every cache to cold. Called on logout so the next session\n *  starts clean (no stale \"warm\" hint from a different guard's data). */\nexport function resetAllCacheStates(): void {\n  const keys: CacheKey[] = ['catalog', 'stock', 'orders', 'alerts'];\n  for (const k of keys) setCacheState(k, 'cold');\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/sync/warmCache.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Post-login cache warm-up.\n *\n * Fires every read-side fetch in parallel so the user lands on Home\n * with skeletons that fill in as each request returns. Non-blocking by\n * design — the caller (AuthProvider.login / App rehydrate) does NOT\n * await this.\n *\n * Each cache transitions independently:\n *   cold → refreshing → (warm | error | offline)\n *\n * One failed cache does not block the others. 401 is handled centrally\n * in sync/api.ts (clears the session and the AuthProvider unmounts the\n * navigator), so we don't see that path here.\n */\n\nimport { cache, type CacheKey } from './cacheStatus';\nimport { api } from './api';\nimport { replaceStockFromRemote } from '../db/stock';\nimport { syncCanonicalProducts, syncOrders } from './syncService';\nimport { getSession } from '../auth/session';\n\n/** Heuristic: distinguish \"no network\" from \"server said no\" so the UI\n *  can show an offline banner vs. a retry banner. The exact fetch error\n *  text differs across RN/Hermes/iOS/Android; match the common shapes. */\nfunction isOfflineError(err: unknown): boolean {\n  const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n  return (\n    msg.includes('Network request failed') ||\n    msg.includes('TypeError: Network') ||\n    msg.includes('Unable to resolve host')\n  );\n}\n\nasync function warmOne(key: CacheKey, fn: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {\n  cache.refreshing(key);\n  try {\n    await fn();\n    cache.warm(key);\n  } catch (err) {\n    if (isOfflineError(err)) {\n      cache.offline(key);\n    } else {\n      cache.error(key);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nasync function warmStock(): Promise<void> {\n  const remote = await api.fetch.stock();\n  await replaceStockFromRemote(\n    remote.map((r) => ({\n      barcode: r.barcode,\n      name: r.name,\n      category: r.category,\n      unit: r.unit,\n      pack_size: r.pack_size != null ? Number(r.pack_size) : null,\n      on_hand: Number(r.on_hand),\n      threshold: Number(r.threshold),\n    })),\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Run all four warm-up fetches in parallel. Safe to call multiple times;\n * the per-cache state transitions are idempotent. No-op if no session.\n */\nexport async function warmCache(): Promise<void> {\n  if (!getSession()?.token) return;\n  await Promise.allSettled([\n    warmOne('catalog', async () => { await syncCanonicalProducts(); }),\n    warmOne('orders', async () => { await syncOrders(); }),\n    warmOne('stock', warmStock),\n    // Alerts is computed from the stock cache (listLowOrOut reads\n    // stock_levels). Treat alerts state as a mirror of stock so the\n    // Home alerts tile follows the same skeleton timeline.\n    warmOne('alerts', async () => { /* no-op; mirrors stock */ }),\n  ]);\n}\n"
}

'cold';\n}\n\nexport function setCacheState(key: CacheKey, state: CacheState): void {\n  if (states.get(key) === state) return;\n  states.set(key, state);\n  const ls = listeners.get(key);\n  if (!ls) return;\n  for (const l of ls) {\n    try { l(state); } catch { /* listener errors must not break the notifier */ }\n  }\n}\n\nexport function onCacheStateChange(key: CacheKey, l: Listener): () => void {\n  let set = listeners.get(key);\n  if (!set) {\n    set = new Set();\n    listeners.set(key, set);\n  }\n  set.add(l);\n  return () => { set!.delete(l); };\n}\n\n/** React hook — re-renders the calling component when this key's state\n *  transitions. Initial value is the current state (no flash). */\nexport function useCacheStatus(key: CacheKey): CacheState {\n  const [s, setS] = useState<CacheState>(() => getCacheState(key));\n  useEffect(() => onCacheStateChange(key, setS), [key]);\n  return s;\n}\n\n/** Helpers callers use to drive the state machine. Centralised so the\n *  transitions stay consistent across warmCache, per-screen refetches,\n *  and post-write invalidation. */\nexport const cache = {\n  refreshing: (key: CacheKey) => setCacheState(key, 'refreshing'),\n  warm: (key: CacheKey) => setCacheState(key, 'warm'),\n  error: (key: CacheKey) => setCacheState(key, 'error'),\n  offline: (key: CacheKey) => setCacheState(key, 'offline'),\n};\n\n/** Reset every cache to cold. Called on logout so the next session\n *  starts clean (no stale \"warm\" hint from a different guard's data). */\nexport function resetAllCacheStates(): void {\n  const keys: CacheKey[] = ['catalog', 'stock', 'orders', 'alerts'];\n  for (const k of keys) setCacheState(k, 'cold');\n}",
  "new_string": "import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\n\nexport type CacheKey = 'catalog' | 'stock' | 'orders' | 'alerts';\n\nexport type CacheState = 'cold' | 'warm' | 'refreshing' | 'error' | 'offline';\n\nexport type CacheStatus = {\n  /** Current network-layer state for this key. */\n  state: CacheState;\n  /** Has any fetch for this key ever succeeded this session? Drives\n   *  Skeleton-vs-data decisions independently of `state` — once true, a\n   *  subsequent `refreshing` doesn't re-skeleton, it just adds a\n   *  spinner on top of the existing data. */\n  hasEverBeenWarm: boolean;\n};\n\nconst ALL_KEYS: readonly CacheKey[] = ['catalog', 'stock', 'orders', 'alerts'];\n\nconst states = new Map<CacheKey, CacheState>();\nconst everWarm = new Map<CacheKey, boolean>();\ntype Listener = (status: CacheStatus) => void;\nconst listeners = new Map<CacheKey, Set<Listener>>();\n\nfunction statusOf(key: CacheKey): CacheStatus {\n  return {\n    state: states.get(key) ?? 'cold',\n    hasEverBeenWarm: everWarm.get(key) ?? false,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function getCacheStatus(key: CacheKey): CacheStatus {\n  return statusOf(key);\n}\n\nfunction setStateInternal(key: CacheKey, state: CacheState, alsoMarkWarm: boolean): void {\n  const prev = states.get(key);\n  const prevWarm = everWarm.get(key) ?? false;\n  if (prev === state && (!alsoMarkWarm || prevWarm)) return;\n  states.set(key, state);\n  if (alsoMarkWarm) everWarm.set(key, true);\n  const status = statusOf(key);\n  const ls = listeners.get(key);\n  if (!ls) return;\n  for (const l of ls) {\n    try { l(status); } catch { /* listener errors must not break the notifier */ }\n  }\n}\n\nexport function onCacheStateChange(key: CacheKey, l: Listener): () => void {\n  let set = listeners.get(key);\n  if (!set) {\n    set = new Set();\n    listeners.set(key, set);\n  }\n  set.add(l);\n  return () => { set!.delete(l); };\n}\n\n/** React hook — re-renders the calling component when this key's status\n *  transitions. Initial value is the current status (no flash). */\nexport function useCacheStatus(key: CacheKey): CacheStatus {\n  const [s, setS] = useState<CacheStatus>(() => statusOf(key));\n  useEffect(() => onCacheStateChange(key, setS), [key]);\n  return s;\n}\n\n/** Helpers callers use to drive the state machine. Centralised so the\n *  transitions stay consistent across warmCache, per-screen refetches,\n *  and post-write invalidation. */\nexport const cache = {\n  refreshing: (key: CacheKey) => setStateInternal(key, 'refreshing', false),\n  // `warm` also flips hasEverBeenWarm permanently — subsequent\n  // refreshes won't re-skeleton the screen.\n  warm: (key: CacheKey) => setStateInternal(key, 'warm', true),\n  error: (key: CacheKey) => setStateInternal(key, 'error', false),\n  offline: (key: CacheKey) => setStateInternal(key, 'offline', false),\n};\n\n/** Reset every cache to cold AND clear the hasEverBeenWarm flag.\n *  Called when the session ends (logout / 401 auto-clear) so the next\n *  session starts truly cold. */\nexport function resetAllCacheStates(): void {\n  for (const k of ALL_KEYS) {\n    states.set(k, 'cold');\n    everWarm.set(k, false);\n    const status = statusOf(k);\n    const ls = listeners.get(k);\n    if (!ls) continue;\n    for (const l of ls) {\n      try { l(status); } catch { /* swallow */ }\n    }\n  }\n}"
}

}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/sync/refetch.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Stale-while-revalidate background refetch.\n *\n * Replaces the old `isCacheStale` gate (TTL-based \"skip if fresh enough\")\n * with an \"always refetch on focus, throttled to avoid back-to-back\n * hammering\" model.\n *\n * Two protections:\n *   1. **Throttle** — if a fetch for this key fired in the last\n *      `THROTTLE_MS`, skip silently. Stops a navigation burst from\n *      firing N parallel requests when the user taps Stock → back → Stock\n *      quickly.\n *   2. **Inflight dedupe** — if a fetch is already in flight, return the\n *      same promise instead of starting another. Two screens (Home and\n *      Stock) waking up simultaneously each only get one network call\n *      between them.\n *\n * State transitions are driven through `cache.*` so every screen that\n * subscribes via `useCacheStatus` sees the change.\n */\n\nimport { cache, type CacheKey } from './cacheStatus';\n\nconst THROTTLE_MS = 2000;\n\nconst lastFetchAt = new Map<CacheKey, number>();\nconst inflight = new Map<CacheKey, Promise<void>>();\n\n/** Same heuristic as warmCache.ts — keep them in sync if you tweak it. */\nfunction isOfflineError(err: unknown): boolean {\n  const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n  return (\n    msg.includes('Network request failed') ||\n    msg.includes('TypeError: Network') ||\n    msg.includes('Unable to resolve host')\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Run `fn` to refetch this key. Returns the in-flight promise if one is\n * already running; resolves immediately (no-op) if throttled.\n *\n * Always-fetch-on-focus callers can `await` this safely — by the time\n * it resolves either (a) a fresh fetch completed and the cache is warm,\n * (b) we deduped onto an in-flight fetch and that resolved, or (c) the\n * throttle skipped because a fetch fired <THROTTLE_MS ago.\n */\nexport async function refetchThrottled(\n  key: CacheKey,\n  fn: () => Promise<void>,\n): Promise<void> {\n  const existing = inflight.get(key);\n  if (existing) return existing;\n\n  const last = lastFetchAt.get(key) ?? 0;\n  if (Date.now() - last < THROTTLE_MS) return;\n\n  cache.refreshing(key);\n  const p = (async () => {\n    try {\n      await fn();\n      cache.warm(key);\n    } catch (err) {\n      // 401 is handled centrally in sync/api.ts (it clears the session;\n      // we'll get unmounted before this state matters).\n      if (isOfflineError(err)) cache.offline(key);\n      else cache.error(key);\n    } finally {\n      lastFetchAt.set(key, Date.now());\n      inflight.delete(key);\n    }\n  })();\n  inflight.set(key, p);\n  return p;\n}\n\n/**\n * Force the next refetch to bypass the throttle. Used by pull-to-refresh\n * and post-write invalidation so a user-initiated refresh is never\n * dropped silently.\n */\nexport function invalidateRefetchThrottle(key: CacheKey): void {\n  lastFetchAt.set(key, 0);\n}\n\n/** Clear all throttles. Logout / 401 auto-clear — keeps no residue from\n *  the previous session. */\nexport function resetAllRefetchThrottles(): void {\n  lastFetchAt.clear();\n  // Don't clear `inflight` — in-flight promises will still resolve and\n  // clean themselves up via the finally{} above.\n}\n"
}
```

  "new_string": "export function StockScreen({ navigation }: Props) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const [rows, setRows] = useState<StockRow[]>([]);\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');\n  const [category, setCategory] = useState('All');\n  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('All');\n  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);\n  const stockStatus = useCacheStatus('stock');\n  // Skeleton only while we have NEVER successfully warmed the stock\n  // cache this session AND we're not currently sitting on a known\n  // failure state (error / offline) — in those terminal states we'd\n  // rather show whatever's in local SQLite than a forever-spinner.\n  const showInitialSkeleton =\n    !stockStatus.hasEverBeenWarm &&\n    stockStatus.state !== 'error' &&\n    stockStatus.state !== 'offline';"

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/modules/inventory/screens/StockScreen.tsx",
  "old_string": "  const fetchRemote = useCallback(async () => {\n    await flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n    try {\n      const remote = await api.fetch.stock();\n      await replaceStockFromRemote(\n        remote.map((r) => ({\n          barcode: r.barcode,\n          name: r.name,\n          category: r.category,\n          unit: r.unit,\n          pack_size: r.pack_size != null ? Number(r.pack_size) : null,\n          on_hand: Number(r.on_hand),\n          threshold: Number(r.threshold),\n        })),\n      );\n      await setLastSyncTime('stock');\n    } catch {\n      // Offline fallback\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const load = useCallback(async (forceRefresh = false) => {\n    const lastSync = await getLastSyncTime('stock');\n    const stale = isCacheStale(lastSync);\n\n    if (forceRefresh || stale) {\n      await fetchRemote();\n    }\n\n    setRows(await listStock());\n    setInitialLoading(false);\n  }, [fetchRemote]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    load(false);\n    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', () => load(false));\n    return unsub;\n  }, [navigation, load]);\n\n  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {\n    setRefreshing(true);\n    try {\n      await load(true);\n    } finally {\n      setRefreshing(false);\n    }\n  }, [load]);",
  "new_string": "  // Pull the latest stock from the server and replace the local cache.\n  // Throws on failure so refetchThrottled() can flip the cache state to\n  // error / offline appropriately. flushOnce() runs first so any\n  // just-queued local writes hit the server before this read.\n  const fetchRemote = useCallback(async () => {\n    await flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n    const remote = await api.fetch.stock();\n    await replaceStockFromRemote(\n      remote.map((r) => ({\n        barcode: r.barcode,\n        name: r.name,\n        category: r.category,\n        unit: r.unit,\n        pack_size: r.pack_size != null ? Number(r.pack_size) : null,\n        on_hand: Number(r.on_hand),\n        threshold: Number(r.threshold),\n      })),\n    );\n  }, []);\n\n  const readLocal = useCallback(async () => {\n    setRows(await listStock());\n  }, []);\n\n  // Always refetch on focus; the throttle in refetchThrottled stops a\n  // back-tap burst from firing N parallel requests.\n  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {\n    await refetchThrottled('stock', fetchRemote);\n    await readLocal();\n  }, [fetchRemote, readLocal]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    void readLocal();\n    void refresh();\n    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', () => { void refresh(); });\n    // Subscribe to cache state changes too — warmCache or\n    // post-write invalidation in flushOnce can update the cache from\n    // outside this screen; re-read SQLite so the list reflects it.\n    const unsubCache = onCacheStateChange('stock', (s) => {\n      if (s.state === 'warm') void readLocal();\n    });\n    return () => { unsub(); unsubCache(); };\n  }, [navigation, refresh, readLocal]);\n\n  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {\n    // Pull-to-refresh is a user-initiated request — never skip it.\n    invalidateRefetchThrottle('stock');\n    setRefreshing(true);\n    try {\n      await refresh();\n    } finally {\n      setRefreshing(false);\n    }\n  }, [refresh]);"
}

29	
30	type OpenOrderItem = {
31	  product_id: string;
32	  expected_qty: number;
33	  received_qty: number;
34	};
35	
36	/** Bigram (Dice coefficient) similarity — used to surface a single
37	 *  "Did you mean…" pill when nothing matched as a substring. Catches
38	 *  typos like "wagh bkri" → "wagh bakri" without flooding the user
39	 *  with low-quality matches. */
40	function similarity(a: string, b: string): number {
41	  const la = a.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
42	  const lb = b.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
43	  if (la === lb) return 1;
44	  if (la.length < 2 || lb.length < 2) return 0;
45	  const bg = (s: string) => {
46	    const out = new Set<string>();
47	    for (let i = 0; i < s.length - 1; i++) out.add(s.slice(i, i + 2));
48	    return out;
49	  };
50	  const A = bg(la);
51	  const B = bg(lb);
52	  let inter = 0;
53	  for (const x of A) if (B.has(x)) inter++;
54	  return (2 * inter) / (A.size + B.size);
55	}
56	
57	export function CatalogPickerScreen({ navigation, route }: Props) {
58	  const { palette } = useTheme();
59	  const t = useT();
60	  const incomingBarcode = route.params?.barcode;
61	  const [products, setProducts] = useState<CanonicalProduct[]>([]);
62	  const [openItemMap, setOpenItemMap] = useState<Record<string, OpenOrderItem>>({});
63	  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
64	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
65	
66	  const loadFromCache = useCallback(async () => {
67	    const all = await listCanonicalProducts();
68	    setProducts(all);
69	
70	    const db = await getDb();
71	    const rows = await db.getAllAsync<OpenOrderItem>(
72	      `SELECT oi.product_id, oi.expected_qty, oi.received_qty
73	       FROM order_items oi
74	       JOIN orders o ON o.id = oi.order_id
75	       WHERE o.status = 'open' AND oi.product_id IS NOT NULL`,
76	    );
77	    const map: Record<string, OpenOrderItem> = {};
78	    for (const r of rows) map[r.product_id] = r;
79	    setOpenItemMap(map);
80	    setLoading(false);
81	  }, []);
82	
83	  const refresh = useCallback(async (forceRefresh = false) => {
84	    const lastSync = await getLastSyncTime('catalog');
85	    const stale = isCacheStale(lastSync);
86	
87	    if (forceRefresh || stale) {
88	      await Promise.all([syncCanonicalProducts(), syncOrders()]);
89	      await setLastSyncTime('catalog');
90	    }
91	
92	    await loadFromCache();
93	  }, [loadFromCache]);
94	
95	  useFocusEffect(
96	    useCallback(() => {
97	      void refresh(false);
98	    }, [refresh]),
99	  );
100

  "new_string": "export function CatalogPickerScreen({ navigation, route }: Props) {\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const t = useT();\n  const incomingBarcode = route.params?.barcode;\n  const [products, setProducts] = useState<CanonicalProduct[]>([]);\n  const [openItemMap, setOpenItemMap] = useState<Record<string, OpenOrderItem>>({});\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');\n  const catalogStatus = useCacheStatus('catalog');\n  // Show the picker skeleton only while we've never loaded a catalog\n  // in this session AND we're not in a terminal failure state (where\n  // we'd rather show whatever local SQLite has, even if empty).\n  const showSkeleton =\n    !catalogStatus.hasEverBeenWarm &&\n    catalogStatus.state !== 'error' &&\n    catalogStatus.state !== 'offline';\n\n  const loadFromCache = useCallback(async () => {\n    const all = await listCanonicalProducts();\n    setProducts(all);\n\n    const db = await getDb();\n    const rows = await db.getAllAsync<OpenOrderItem>(\n      `SELECT oi.product_id, oi.expected_qty, oi.received_qty\n       FROM order_items oi\n       JOIN orders o ON o.id = oi.order_id\n       WHERE o.status = 'open' AND oi.product_id IS NOT NULL`,\n    );\n    const map: Record<string, OpenOrderItem> = {};\n    for (const r of rows) map[r.product_id] = r;\n    setOpenItemMap(map);\n  }, []);\n\n  // Always refetch on focus. The throttle in refetchThrottled stops a\n  // navigate-back-then-forward burst from firing N parallel pulls.\n  const refresh = useCallback(async (forceRefresh = false) => {\n    if (forceRefresh) {\n      invalidateRefetchThrottle('catalog');\n      invalidateRefetchThrottle('orders');\n    }\n    await Promise.all([\n      refetchThrottled('catalog', async () => { await syncCanonicalProducts(); }),\n      refetchThrottled('orders', async () => { await syncOrders(); }),\n    ]);\n    await loadFromCache();\n  }, [loadFromCache]);\n\n  useFocusEffect(\n    useCallback(() => {\n      void loadFromCache();\n      void refresh(false);\n      // When the catalog or orders cache warms from any source (warmCache,\n      // background sync, post-write invalidation), refresh the local list.\n      const unsubCatalog = onCacheStateChange('catalog', (s) => {\n        if (s.state === 'warm') void loadFromCache();\n      });\n      const unsubOrders = onCacheStateChange('orders', (s) => {\n        if (s.state === 'warm') void loadFromCache();\n      });\n      return () => { unsubCatalog(); unsubOrders(); };\n    }, [loadFromCache, refresh]),\n  );"
}

1	import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
2	import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
3	import { RefreshControl, SectionList, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
4	import { AppBar, Card, Skeleton, spacing, StatusPill, Text } from '../../../design';
5	import { getOrderItems, listOpenOrders, Order, OrderItem, upsertOrdersFromRemote } from '../../../db/orders';
6	import { useT } from '../../../i18n';
7	import { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';
8	import { api } from '../../../sync/api';
9	import { flushOnce } from '../../../sync/syncService';
10	import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
11	import { getLastSyncTime, setLastSyncTime, isCacheStale } from '../../../sync/cacheTime';
12	
13	type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Orders'>;
14	type Enriched = Order & { items: OrderItem[] };
15	type Bucket = 'arrived' | 'awaited' | 'done';
16	
17	function bucketOf(o: Enriched): Bucket {
18	  if (o.items.length === 0) return 'awaited';
19	  const allDone = o.items.every((i) => i.received_qty >= i.expected_qty);
20	  if (allDone) return 'done';
21	  const anyReceived = o.items.some((i) => i.received_qty > 0);
22	  return anyReceived ? 'arrived' : 'awaited';
23	}
24	
25	const SECTION_ORDER: Bucket[] = ['arrived', 'awaited', 'done'];
26	
27	export function OrdersScreen({ navigation }: Props) {
28	  const t = useT();
29	  const { palette } = useTheme();
30	  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Enriched[]>([]);
31	  const [initialLoading, setInitialLoading] = useState(true);
32	  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
33	
34	  const fetchRemote = useCallback(async () => {
35	    await flushOnce().catch(() => {});
36	
37	    try {
38	      const remote = await api.fetch.orders();
39	      await upsertOrdersFromRemote(remote).catch(() => {});
40	      setOrders(
41	        remote.map((o) => ({
42	          id: o.id,
43	          vendor: o.vendor,
44	          expected_at: o.expected_at ? new Date(o.expected_at).getTime() : null,
45	          status: o.status,
46	          updated_at: Date.now(),
47	          items: o.items.map((i) => ({
48	            id: i.id,
49	            order_id: i.order_id,
50	            barcode: i.barcode,
51	            product_name: i.product_name,
52	            expected_qty: Number(i.expected_qty),
53	            received_qty: Number(i.received_qty),
54	          })),
55	        })),
56	      );
57	      await setLastSyncTime('orders');
58	      return true;
59	    } catch {
60	      return false;
61	    }
62	  }, []);
63	
64	  const load = useCallback(async (forceRefresh = false) => {
65	    const lastSync = await getLastSyncTime('orders');
66	    const stale = isCacheStale(lastSync);
67	
68	    if (forceRefresh || stale) {
69	      const success = await fetchRemote();
70	      if (success) {
71	        setInitialLoading(false);
72	        return;
73	      }
74	    }
75	
76	    const list = await listOpenOrders();
77	    setOrders(
78	      await Promise.all(list.map(async (o) => ({ ...o, items: await getOrderItems(o.id) })))
79	    );
80	    setInitialLoading(false);
81	  }, [fetchRemote]);
82	
83	  useEffect(() => {
84	    load(false);
85	    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', () => load(false));
86	    return unsub;
87	  }, [navigation, load]);
88	
89	  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
90	    setRefreshing(true);
91	    try {
92	      await load(true);
93	    } finally {
94	      setRefreshing(false);
95	    }
96	  }, [load]);
97	
98	  const sections = useMemo(() => {
99	    const groups: Record<Bucket, Enriched[]> = { arrived: [], awaited: [], done: [] };
100	    for (const o of orders) groups[bucketOf(o)].push(o);
101	    const titleFor: Record<Bucket, string> = {
102	      arrived: 'Arrived',
103	      awaited: t('awaited'),
104	      done: t('doneToday'),
105	    };
106	    return SECTION_ORDER.filter((b) => groups[b].length > 0).map((b) => ({
107	      title: titleFor[b],
108	      bucket: b,
109	      data: groups[b],
110	    }));
111	  }, [orders, t]);
112	
113	  return (
114	    <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
115	      <AppBar title={t('receiving')} subtitle={t('expectedToday')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />
116	      {initialLoading && orders.length === 0 ? (
117	        <View style={styles.list}>
118	          <View style={[styles.sectionHeader, { marginBottom: spacing.sm }]}>
119	            <Skeleton width="35%" height={14} />
120	          </View>
121	          {[0, 1].map((i) => (
122	            <View key={i} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.md }}>
123	              <Card tone="filled" padding="lg">
124	                <View style={styles.head}>
125	                  <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: 6 }}>
126	                    <Skeleton width="50%" height={22} />
127	                    <Skeleton width="35%" height={14} />
128	                  </View>
129	                  <Skeleton width={64} height={24} rounded="pill" />
130	                </View>
131	                <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />
132	                <Skeleton width="100%" height={16} />
133	                <View style={{ height: spacing.sm }} />
134	                <Skeleton width="80%" height={16} />
135	              </Card>
136	            </View>
137	          ))}
138	        </View>
139	      ) : (
140	      <SectionList
141	        sections={sections}
142	        keyExtractor={(o) => o.id}
143	        contentContainerStyle={styles.list}
144	        refreshControl={
145	          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} tintColor={palette.primary} />
146	        }
147	        stickySectionHeadersEnabled={false}
148	        renderSectionHeader={({ section }) => (
149	          <View style={styles.sectionHeader}>
150	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
151	              {section.title.toUpperCase()} · {section.data.length}
152	            </Text>
153	          </View>
154	        )}
155	        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />}
156	        SectionSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.lg }} />}
157	        renderItem={({ item, section }) => {
158	          const bucket = (section as unknown as { bucket: Bucket }).bucket;
159	          return (
160	            <Card tone="filled" padding="lg">
161	              <View style={styles.head}>
162	                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
163	                  <Text variant="titleLarge">{item.vendor ?? 'Order'}</Text>
164	                  {item.expected_at ? (
165	                    <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
166	                      Due {new Date(item.expected_at).toLocaleDateString()}
167	                    </Text>
168	                  ) : null}
169	                </View>
170	                <StatusPill
171	                  label={
172	                    bucket === 'done'
173	                      ? t('done')
174	                      : `${item.items.length} ${t('items')}`
175	                  }
176	                  tone={bucket === 'done' ? 'success' : bucket === 'arrived' ? 'warn' : 'neutral'}
177	                />
178	              </View>
179	
180	              <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />
181	              {item.items.map((i, idx) => {
182	                const itemDone = i.received_qty >= i.expected_qty;
183	                return (
184	                  <View
185	                    key={i.id}
186	                    style={[
187	                      styles.itemRow,
188	                      idx < item.items.length - 1 && { borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: palette.outlineVariant },
189	                    ]}
190	                  >
191	                    <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
192	                      <Text variant="bodyLarge" color={itemDone ? palette.onSurfaceVariant : palette.onSurface}>
193	                        {i.product_name}
194	                      </Text>
195	                    </View>
196	                    <Text
197	                      variant="titleMedium"
198	                      color={itemDone ? palette.primary : palette.onSurface}
199	                    >
200	                      {i.received_qty} / {i.expected_qty}
201	                    </Text>
202	                  </View>
203	                );
204	              })}
205	            </Card>
206	          );
207	        }}
208	        ListEmptyComponent={
209	          <View style={styles.empty}>
210	            <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
211	              No deliveries scheduled
212	            </Text>
213	            <Text
214	              variant="bodyLarge"
215	              color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
216	              style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.sm }}
217	            >
218	              Orders appear here once the office processes a bill.
219	            </Text>
220	          </View>
221	        }
222	      />
223	      )}
224	    </View>
225	  );
226	}
227	
228	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
229	  safe: { flex: 1 },
230	  list: { padding: spacing.xl, paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl },
231	  sectionHeader: { paddingBottom: spacing.sm },
232	  head: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: spacing.md },
233	  itemRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: spacing.sm, gap: spacing.md },
234	  empty: { padding: spacing.xxl },
235	});
236

  "old_string": "export function OrdersScreen({ navigation }: Props) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Enriched[]>([]);\n  const [initialLoading, setInitialLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);\n\n  const fetchRemote = useCallback(async () => {\n    await flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n\n    try {\n      const remote = await api.fetch.orders();\n      await upsertOrdersFromRemote(remote).catch(() => {});\n      setOrders(\n        remote.map((o) => ({\n          id: o.id,\n          vendor: o.vendor,\n          expected_at: o.expected_at ? new Date(o.expected_at).getTime() : null,\n          status: o.status,\n          updated_at: Date.now(),\n          items: o.items.map((i) => ({\n            id: i.id,\n            order_id: i.order_id,\n            barcode: i.barcode,\n            product_name: i.product_name,\n            expected_qty: Number(i.expected_qty),\n            received_qty: Number(i.received_qty),\n          })),\n        })),\n      );\n      await setLastSyncTime('orders');\n      return true;\n    } catch {\n      return false;\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const load = useCallback(async (forceRefresh = false) => {\n    const lastSync = await getLastSyncTime('orders');\n    const stale = isCacheStale(lastSync);\n\n    if (forceRefresh || stale) {\n      const success = await fetchRemote();\n      if (success) {\n        setInitialLoading(false);\n        return;\n      }\n    }\n\n    const list = await listOpenOrders();\n    setOrders(\n      await Promise.all(list.map(async (o) => ({ ...o, items: await getOrderItems(o.id) })))\n    );\n    setInitialLoading(false);\n  }, [fetchRemote]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    load(false);\n    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', () => load(false));\n    return unsub;\n  }, [navigation, load]);\n\n  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {\n    setRefreshing(true);\n    try {\n      await load(true);\n    } finally {\n      setRefreshing(false);\n    }\n  }, [load]);",
  "new_string": "export function OrdersScreen({ navigation }: Props) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Enriched[]>([]);\n  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);\n  const ordersStatus = useCacheStatus('orders');\n  const showSkeleton =\n    !ordersStatus.hasEverBeenWarm &&\n    ordersStatus.state !== 'error' &&\n    ordersStatus.state !== 'offline';\n\n  const fetchRemote = useCallback(async () => {\n    await flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n    const remote = await api.fetch.orders();\n    await upsertOrdersFromRemote(remote).catch(() => {});\n  }, []);\n\n  const readLocal = useCallback(async () => {\n    const list = await listOpenOrders();\n    setOrders(\n      await Promise.all(list.map(async (o) => ({ ...o, items: await getOrderItems(o.id) })))\n    );\n  }, []);\n\n  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {\n    await refetchThrottled('orders', fetchRemote);\n    await readLocal();\n  }, [fetchRemote, readLocal]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    void readLocal();\n    void refresh();\n    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', () => { void refresh(); });\n    const unsubCache = onCacheStateChange('orders', (s) => {\n      if (s.state === 'warm') void readLocal();\n    });\n    return () => { unsub(); unsubCache(); };\n  }, [navigation, refresh, readLocal]);\n\n  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {\n    invalidateRefetchThrottle('orders');\n    setRefreshing(true);\n    try {\n      await refresh();\n    } finally {\n      setRefreshing(false);\n    }\n  }, [refresh]);"
}
