1	import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
2	import { Check } from 'lucide-react-native';
3	import { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';
4	import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
5	import { FlatList, RefreshControl, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
6	import {
7	  AppBar,
8	  Button,
9	  Card,
10	  Skeleton,
11	  spacing,
12	  StatusPill,
13	  Text,
14	} from '../../../design';
15	import { enqueue } from '../../../db/outbox';
16	import { listLowOrOut, statusFor, StockRow, replaceStockFromRemote } from '../../../db/stock';
17	import { useT } from '../../../i18n';
18	import { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';
19	import { api } from '../../../sync/api';
20	import { flushOnce } from '../../../sync/syncService';
21	import { haptic } from '../../../utils/haptics';
22	import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
23	import { getLastSyncTime, setLastSyncTime, isCacheStale } from '../../../sync/cacheTime';
24	
25	type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Alerts'>;
26	
27	export function AlertsScreen({ navigation }: Props) {
28	  const t = useT();
29	  const { palette } = useTheme();
30	  const [rows, setRows] = useState<StockRow[]>([]);
31	  const [requested, setRequested] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
32	  const [initialLoading, setInitialLoading] = useState(true);
33	  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
34	
35	  const fetchRemote = useCallback(async () => {
36	    await flushOnce().catch(() => {});
37	    try {
38	      const remote = await api.fetch.stock();
39	      await replaceStockFromRemote(
40	        remote.map((r) => ({
41	          barcode: r.barcode,
42	          name: r.name,
43	          category: r.category,
44	          unit: r.unit,
45	          pack_size: r.pack_size != null ? Number(r.pack_size) : null,
46	          on_hand: Number(r.on_hand),
47	          threshold: Number(r.threshold),
48	        })),
49	      );
50	      await setLastSyncTime('alerts');
51	    } catch {
52	      // Fall back to local SQLite cache.
53	    }
54	  }, []);
55	
56	  const load = useCallback(async (forceRefresh = false) => {
57	    const lastSync = await getLastSyncTime('alerts');
58	    const stale = isCacheStale(lastSync);
59	
60	    if (forceRefresh || stale) {
61	      await fetchRemote();
62	    }
63	
64	    setRows(await listLowOrOut());
65	    setInitialLoading(false);
66	  }, [fetchRemote]);
67	
68	  useEffect(() => {
69	    load(false);
70	    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', () => load(false));
71	    return unsub;
72	  }, [navigation, load]);
73	
74	  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
75	    setRefreshing(true);
76	    try {
77	      await load(true);
78	    } finally {
79	      setRefreshing(false);
80	    }
81	  }, [load]);
82	
83	  const reorder = async (row: StockRow) => {
84	    haptic.tap();
85	    await enqueue('reorder_request', {
86	      id: `ror_${nanoid(12)}`,
87	      barcode: row.barcode,
88	      product_name: row.name,
89	      on_hand: row.on_hand,
90	      threshold: row.threshold,
91	      requested_at: Date.now(),
92	    });
93	    setRequested((prev) => new Set(prev).add(row.barcode));
94	    flushOnce().catch(() => {});
95	    haptic.success();
96	  };
97	
98	  return (
99	    <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
100	      <AppBar title={t('alerts')} subtitle={t('alertsSub')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />
101	      {initialLoading && rows.length === 0 ? (
102	        <View style={styles.list}>
103	          {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
104	            <View key={i} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.md }}>
105	              <Card tone="filled" padding="lg">
106	                <View style={styles.head}>
107	                  <View style={{ flex: 1, gap: 6 }}>
108	                    <Skeleton width="55%" height={18} />
109	                    <Skeleton width="75%" height={14} />
110	                  </View>
111	                  <Skeleton width={56} height={24} rounded="pill" />
112	                </View>
113	                <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />
114	                <Skeleton width="40%" height={36} rounded="pill" />
115	              </Card>
116	            </View>
117	          ))}
118	        </View>
119	      ) : (
120	      <FlatList
121	        data={rows}
122	        keyExtractor={(r) => r.barcode}
123	        contentContainerStyle={styles.list}
124	        refreshControl={
125	          <RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={onRefresh} tintColor={palette.primary} />
126	        }
127	        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />}
128	        renderItem={({ item }) => {
129	          const status = statusFor(item);
130	          const tone = status === 'out' ? 'danger' : 'warn';
131	          const label = status === 'out' ? t('outStock') : t('lowStock');
132	          const done = requested.has(item.barcode);
133	          return (
134	            <Card tone="filled" padding="lg">
135	              <View style={styles.head}>
136	                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
137	                  <Text variant="titleMedium">{item.name}</Text>
138	                  <Text
139	                    variant="bodyMedium"
140	                    color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
141	                    style={{ marginTop: 2 }}
142	                  >
143	                    {item.on_hand}
144	                    {item.pack_size != null && item.pack_size !== 1 ? ` × ${item.pack_size}` : ''}
145	                    {item.unit ? ` ${item.unit}` : ''} {t('onHand')} · threshold {item.threshold}
146	                  </Text>
147	                </View>
148	                <StatusPill label={label} tone={tone} />
149	              </View>
150	              <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />
151	              {done ? (
152	                <StatusPill label={t('reorderSent')} tone="success" Icon={Check} />
153	              ) : (
154	                <Button
155	                  label={t('requestReorder')}
156	                  variant="tonal"
157	                  size="md"
158	                  onPress={() => reorder(item)}
159	                />
160	              )}
161	            </Card>
162	          );
163	        }}
164	        ListEmptyComponent={
165	          <View style={styles.empty}>
166	            <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
167	              All stocked up
168	            </Text>
169	            <Text
170	              variant="bodyLarge"
171	              color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
172	              style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.sm }}
173	            >
174	              Nothing is below its threshold right now.
175	            </Text>
176	          </View>
177	        }
178	      />
179	      )}
180	    </View>
181	  );
182	}
183	
184	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
185	  safe: { flex: 1 },
186	  list: { padding: spacing.xl, paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl },
187	  head: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: spacing.md },
188	  empty: { padding: spacing.xxl },
189	});
190

Mirror its status — when stock warms, alerts warm.\n  const stockStatus = useCacheStatus('stock');\n  const showSkeleton =\n    !stockStatus.hasEverBeenWarm &&\n    stockStatus.state !== 'error' &&\n    stockStatus.state !== 'offline';\n\n  const fetchRemote = useCallback(async () => {\n    await flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n    const remote = await api.fetch.stock();\n    await replaceStockFromRemote(\n      remote.map((r) => ({\n        barcode: r.barcode,\n        name: r.name,\n        category: r.category,\n        unit: r.unit,\n        pack_size: r.pack_size != null ?   "old_string": "  async function pick(product: CanonicalProduct) {\n    // Learn barcode → product the moment the guard picks, so a future\n    // scan auto-resolves even if they cancel the receipt downstream.\n    if (incomingBarcode) {\n      try {\n        await learnBarcode(incomingBarcode, product.product_id, 'scan');\n        await enqueue('learn_barcode', {\n          barcode: incomingBarcode,\n          product_id: product.product_id,\n        }).catch(() => {});\n      } catch {\n        // Non-fatal: the receipt flow has its own learning step.\n      }\n    }\n    // No-barcode pick path: if this product already has a real barcode\n    // mapped, reuse it so the receipt rolls into the same stock row and\n    // the user sees the actual barcode (not `catalog_…`).\n    //\n    // Lookup order:\n    //   1. `primary_barcode` on the synced canonical row — catches\n    //      barcodes mapped by another guard, the admin dashboard, or a\n    //      bill upload (this device never learned them locally).\n    //   2. Local `product_barcodes` — catches barcodes this device just\n    //      learned but hasn't pushed to the server yet (offline gap).\n    //   3. Synthetic `catalog_<product_id>` — stable per product_id so a\n    //      later real-barcode learn keeps history and stock aligned.\n    let barcode = incomingBarcode;\n    if (!barcode) {\n      const fromCatalog = product.primary_barcode ?? null;\n      const fromLocal = fromCatalog ? null : await findBarcodeForProduct(product.product_id);\n      barcode = fromCatalog ?? fromLocal ?? `catalog_${product.product_id}`;\n    }\n    navigation.push('Receiving', {\n      barcode,\n      productId: product.product_id,\n      productName: product.canonical_name,\n      unit: product.unit,\n      packSize: product.pack_size,\n    });\n  }",
  "new_string": "  /**\n   * Promise that rejects after `ms` so a slow / dead network doesn't leave\n   * the row spinning forever. 3 s is long enough for real ngrok / Bifrost\n   * hops on warehouse Wi-Fi but short enough that the user knows to retry.\n   */\n  function withTimeout<T>(p: Promise<T>, ms: number): Promise<T> {\n    return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {\n      const id = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('JIT timeout')), ms);\n      p.then(\n        (v) => { clearTimeout(id); resolve(v); },\n        (e) => { clearTimeout(id); reject(e); },\n      );\n    });\n  }\n\n  async function pick(product: CanonicalProduct) {\n    if (verifyingProductId) return; // de-dupe rapid taps\n    setVerifyError(null);\n    setVerifyingProductId(product.product_id);\n    haptic.tap();\n\n    // Learn barcode → product the moment the guard picks, so a future\n    // scan auto-resolves even if they cancel the receipt downstream.\n    // This is local-only + an outbox row; safe before JIT.\n    if (incomingBarcode) {\n      try {\n        await learnBarcode(incomingBarcode, product.product_id, 'scan');\n        await enqueue('learn_barcode', {\n          barcode: incomingBarcode,\n          product_id: product.product_id,\n        }).catch(() => {});\n      } catch {\n        // Non-fatal: the receipt flow has its own learning step.\n      }\n    }\n\n    // JIT verification — fetch the freshest single-row view of this\n    // product (live has_barcode + primary_barcode). The cached row is\n    // a visual hint; this fetched row is what we commit against.\n    // Fail-closed: any error / timeout cancels the navigation so a\n    // stale cache can't produce a wrong receipt.\n    let verified: CanonicalProduct;\n    try {\n      const remote = await withTimeout(api.fetch.canonicalProduct(product.product_id), 3000);\n      verified = {\n        product_id: remote.product_id,\n        canonical_name: remote.canonical_name,\n        category: remote.category,\n        hsn_code: remote.hsn_code,\n        unit: remote.unit,\n        pack_size: remote.pack_size,\n        has_barcode: remote.has_barcode ? 1 : 0,\n        primary_barcode: remote.primary_barcode ?? null,\n        updated_at: Date.now(),\n      };\n    } catch (err) {\n      setVerifyingProductId(null);\n      haptic.warn();\n      // 401 is already handled centrally (session cleared, navigator\n      // re-renders into Login). For everything else — timeout, offline,\n      // 4xx, 5xx — block the pick with a small banner.\n      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) return;\n      setVerifyError(t('couldntVerify'));\n      return;\n    }\n    setVerifyingProductId(null);\n\n    // No-barcode pick path: pick the real barcode in this order so the\n    // receipt rolls into the same stock row as future scans.\n    //   1. `incomingBarcode` if the user came in via Scan-then-pick.\n    //   2. The just-verified `primary_barcode` — guaranteed-fresh.\n    //   3. Local `product_barcodes` — covers offline gap where this\n    //      device just learned a barcode the server hasn't seen yet.\n    //   4. Synthetic `catalog_<product_id>` — stable per product_id so a\n    //      later real-barcode learn keeps history and stock aligned.\n    let barcode = incomingBarcode;\n    if (!barcode) {\n      const fromCatalog = verified.primary_barcode ?? null;\n      const fromLocal = fromCatalog ? null : await findBarcodeForProduct(verified.product_id);\n      barcode = fromCatalog ?? fromLocal ?? `catalog_${verified.product_id}`;\n    }\n    navigation.push('Receiving', {\n      barcode,\n      productId: verified.product_id,\n      productName: verified.canonical_name,\n      unit: verified.unit,\n      packSize: verified.pack_size,\n    });\n  }"
}

1	import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
2	import { Calendar, Check } from 'lucide-react-native';
3	import { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';
4	import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
5	import {
6	  KeyboardAvoidingView,
7	  Platform,
8	  Pressable,
9	  ScrollView,
10	  StyleSheet,
11	  View,
12	} from 'react-native';
13	import {
14	  AppBar,
15	  Button,
16	  Card,
17	  QtyStepper,
18	  radius,
19	  spacing,
20	  Text,
21	} from '../../../design';
22	import { getSession } from '../../../auth/session';
23	import { correctStockLocal, findStock, getNearestExpiry, StockRow } from '../../../db/stock';
24	import { enqueue } from '../../../db/outbox';
25	import { useT } from '../../../i18n';
26	import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
27	import { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';
28	import { flushOnce } from '../../../sync/syncService';
29	import { haptic } from '../../../utils/haptics';
30	import { DatePickerModal } from '../components/DatePickerModal';
31	import { useKeyboardHeight } from '../../../hooks/useKeyboardHeight';
32	
33	type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'EditStock'>;
34	
35	function formatForDisplay(iso: string): string {
36	  try {
37	    const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');
38	    return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
39	  } catch {
40	    return iso;
41	  }
42	}
43	
44	function toISO(d: Date): string {
45	  return d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
46	}
47	
48	/** Earliest date out of a list; nulls ignored. Returns null if all null. */
49	function earliestISO(dates: (Date | null)[]): string | null {
50	  const isos = dates.filter((d): d is Date => !!d).map(toISO).sort();
51	  return isos[0] ?? null;
52	}
53	
54	export function EditStockScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
55	  const t = useT();
56	  const { palette } = useTheme();
57	  const kbHeight = useKeyboardHeight();
58	  const { barcode } = route.params;
59	
60	  const [row, setRow] = useState<StockRow | null>(null);
61	  const [originalQty, setOriginalQty] = useState<number>(0);
62	  const [originalExpiry, setOriginalExpiry] = useState<string | null>(null);
63	  const [qty, setQty] = useState<number>(0);
64	  const [expiryDateObj, setExpiryDateObj] = useState<Date | null>(null);
65	  const [noExpiry, setNoExpiry] = useState<boolean>(false);
66	
67	  // Per-pack expiry. Mirrors ReceivingScreen: off by default; appears only
68	  // when qty > 1 and not noExpiry. When on, expand into N rows so the
69	  // guard can set a different expiry on each pack.
70	  const [perPackExpiry, setPerPackExpiry] = useState(false);
71	  const [perPackExpiries, setPerPackExpiries] = useState<(Date | null)[]>([]);
72	  const [editingPackIdx, setEditingPackIdx] = useState<number | null>(null);
73	
74	  const [showPicker, setShowPicker] = useState(false);
75	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
76	
77	  useEffect(() => {
78	    (async () => {
79	      const r = await findStock(barcode);
80	      const exp = await getNearestExpiry(barcode);
81	      if (!r) return;
82	      setRow(r);
83	      setOriginalQty(r.on_hand);
84	      setOriginalExpiry(exp);
85	      setQty(r.on_hand);
86	      if (exp) {
87	        setExpiryDateObj(new Date(exp));
88	        setNoExpiry(false);
89	      } else {
90	        setNoExpiry(true);
91	      }
92	    })();
93	  }, [barcode]);
94	
95	  // Keep the per-pack list in sync with qty when toggle is on. Seed new
96	  // rows with the bulk expiry so the guard only edits the differing ones.
97	  useEffect(() => {
98	    if (!perPackExpiry) return;
99	    setPerPackExpiries((prev) => {
100	      const n = Math.max(1, Math.floor(qty));
101	      if (prev.length === n) return prev;
102	      const next = [...prev];
103	      while (next.length < n) next.push(expiryDateObj);
104	      while (next.length > n) next.pop();
105	      return next;
106	    });
107	  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, expiryDateObj]);
108	
109	  // Auto-disable per-pack when the toggle's preconditions go away
110	  // (qty drops to 1, or guard checks "no expiry").
111	  useEffect(() => {
112	    if (perPackExpiry && (qty <= 1 || noExpiry)) {
113	      setPerPackExpiry(false);
114	    }
115	  }, [perPackExpiry, qty, noExpiry]);
116	
117	  /** Single new_expiry sent to server when per-pack is off. */
118	  const newSingleExpiry = useMemo(() => {
119	    if (noExpiry || perPackExpiry || !expiryDateObj) return null;
120	    return toISO(expiryDateObj);
121	  }, [expiryDateObj, noExpiry, perPackExpiry]);
122	
123	  /** Array of expiries sent when per-pack is on. */
124	  const newExpiriesArray = useMemo<string[] | null>(() => {
125	    if (noExpiry || !perPackExpiry) return null;
126	    return perPackExpiries.map((d) => (d ? toISO(d) : '')).filter((s) => s.length > 0);
127	  }, [noExpiry, perPackExpiry, perPackExpiries]);
128	
129	  /** Whatever ends up as the locally-tracked "nearest" expiry. */
130	  const localNearestExpiry = useMemo(() => {
131	    if (noExpiry) return null;
132	    if (perPackExpiry) return earliestISO(perPackExpiries);
133	    return expiryDateObj ? toISO(expiryDateObj) : null;
134	  }, [noExpiry, perPackExpiry, perPackExpiries, expiryDateObj]);
135	
136	  const changed = useMemo(() => {
137	    if (!row) return false;
138	    if (qty !== originalQty) return true;
139	    if (localNearestExpiry !== originalExpiry) return true;
140	    // Per-pack with same nearest as original still counts as a change if
141	    // any pack carries a date different from the bulk default.
142	    if (perPackExpiry) return true;
143	    return false;
144	  }, [row, qty, originalQty, localNearestExpiry, originalExpiry, perPackExpiry]);
145	
146	  const packDisplay = row && row.pack_size != null && row.pack_size !== 1 && row.unit
147	    ? `${row.pack_size} ${row.unit}`
148	    : row?.unit ?? null;
149	
150	  const submit = async () => {
151	    if (!row || !changed) return;
152	    const session = getSession();
153	    setSaving(true);
154	    try {
155	      await correctStockLocal(barcode, qty, localNearestExpiry);
156	      await enqueue('stock_correction', {
157	        id: `crn_${nanoid(12)}`,
158	        barcode,
159	        product_name: row.name,
160	        old_qty: originalQty,
161	        new_qty: qty,
162	        old_expiry: originalExpiry,
163	        new_expiry: newSingleExpiry,
164	        new_expiries: newExpiriesArray,
165	        performed_by: session?.guardId ?? null,
166	        performed_by_name: session?.guardName ?? null,
167	        corrected_at: Date.now(),
168	      });
169	      flushOnce().catch(() => {});
170	      haptic.success();
171	      navigation.goBack();
172	    } finally {
173	      setSaving(false);
174	    }
175	  };
176	
177	  if (!row) {
178	    return (
179	      <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
180	        <AppBar title={t('editStock')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />
181	      </View>
182	    );
183	  }
184	
185	  return (
186	    <KeyboardAvoidingView
187	      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'height' : undefined}
188	      style={{ flex: 1 }}
189	    >
190	      <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
191	        <AppBar title={t('editStock')} subtitle={t('editStockSub')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />
192	
193	        <ScrollView
194	          contentContainerStyle={[styles.content, { paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl + kbHeight }]}
195	          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
196	        >
197	          <Card tone="elevated" padding="xl">
198	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
199	              {t('product').toUpperCase()}
200	            </Text>
201	            <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-end', marginTop: spacing.xs, gap: spacing.md }}>
202	              <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ flex: 1 }}>{row.name}</Text>
203	              {packDisplay ? (
204	                <Text variant="titleMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>{packDisplay}</Text>
205	              ) : null}
206	            </View>
207	            <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>
208	              {t('currentCount')}: {originalQty}{row.unit ? ` ${row.unit}` : ''}
209	            </Text>
210	          </Card>
211	
212	          <View style={styles.qtySection}>
213	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
214	              {t('newCount').toUpperCase()}
215	            </Text>
216	            <QtyStepper value={qty} onChange={setQty} min={0} />
217	          </View>
218	
219	          <View style={styles.expirySection}>
220	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
221	              {t('expiryDate').toUpperCase()}
222	            </Text>
223	
224	            {/* Per-pack toggle. Mirrors ReceivingScreen: only useful for
225	                multi-qty edits. */}
226	            {qty > 1 && !noExpiry && (
227	              <Pressable
228	                onPress={() => { setPerPackExpiry((v) => !v); haptic.tap(); }}
229	                style={[styles.noExpiryRow, { marginTop: spacing.sm }]}
230	              >
231	                <View
232	                  style={[
233	                    styles.checkbox,
234	                    {
235	                      backgroundColor: perPackExpiry ? palette.primary : 'transparent',
236	                      borderColor: perPackExpiry ? palette.primary : palette.outline,
237	                    },
238	                  ]}
239	                >
240	                  {perPackExpiry && <Check size={14} color={palette.onPrimary} strokeWidth={3} />}
241	                </View>
242	                <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
243	                  {t('perPackExpiry')} ({Math.floor(qty)} {t('packsLabel')})
244	                </Text>
245	              </Pressable>
246	            )}
247	
248	            {!noExpiry && !perPackExpiry && (
249	              <>
250	                <Pressable
251	                  onPress={() => setShowPicker(true)}
252	                  style={[
253	                    styles.expiryInput,
254	                    {
255	                      backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
256	                      borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
257	                      flexDirection: 'row',
258	                      alignItems: 'center',
259	                    },
260	                  ]}
261	                >
262	                  <Calendar size={20} color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} strokeWidth={2} />
263	                  <Text
264	                    variant="bodyLarge"
265	                    color={expiryDateObj ? palette.onSurface : palette.onSurfaceVariant}
266	                    style={{ marginLeft: spacing.sm, flex: 1, letterSpacing: 1 }}
267	                  >
268	                    {expiryDateObj ? formatForDisplay(toISO(expiryDateObj)) : t('tapToSelectDate')}
269	                  </Text>
270	                </Pressable>
271	                {showPicker && editingPackIdx == null && (
272	                  <DatePickerModal
273	                    visible={showPicker}
274	                    value={expiryDateObj}
275	                    onSelect={(d) => { setExpiryDateObj(d); setNoExpiry(false); }}
276	                    onDismiss={() => setShowPicker(false)}
277	                  />
278	                )}
279	              </>
280	            )}
281	
282	            {!noExpiry && perPackExpiry && (
283	              <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm, gap: spacing.xs }}>
284	                {perPackExpiries.map((d, i) => (
285	                  <Pressable
286	                    key={i}
287	                    onPress={() => { setEditingPackIdx(i); setShowPicker(true); }}
288	                    style={[
289	                      styles.expiryInput,
290	                      {
291	                        backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
292	                        borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,
293	                        flexDirection: 'row',
294	                        alignItems: 'center',
295	                      },

  "old_string": "/** Earliest date out of a list; nulls ignored. Returns null if all null. */\nfunction earliestISO(dates: (Date | null)[]): string | null {\n  const isos = dates.filter((d): d is Date => !!d).map(toISO).sort();\n  return isos[0] ?? null;\n}",
  "new_string": "/** Earliest date out of a list; nulls ignored. Returns null if all null. */\nfunction earliestISO(dates: (Date | null)[]): string | null {\n  const isos = dates.filter((d): d is Date => !!d).map(toISO).sort();\n  return isos[0] ?? null;\n}\n\n/**\n * verifying  — JIT fetch in flight; save disabled.\n * ok         — server agrees with what we opened the screen with.\n * absent     — server has no row for this barcode (treat as ok — local\n *              view is the only truth; the correction will create it).\n * conflict   — server's on_hand or nearest_expiry differs from local;\n *              show prompt before letting user save.\n * offline    — fetch failed because network down; save disabled.\n * error      — fetch failed otherwise; save disabled.\n */\ntype VerifyStatus = 'verifying' | 'ok' | 'absent' | 'conflict' | 'offline' | 'error';\n\nfunction isOfflineError(err: unknown): boolean {\n  const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n  return (\n    msg.includes('Network request failed') ||\n    msg.includes('TypeError: Network') ||\n    msg.includes('Unable to resolve host') ||\n    msg === 'JIT timeout'\n  );\n}"
}

  "new_string": "  const [showPicker, setShowPicker] = useState(false);\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  // JIT verification — fetch the server's authoritative on_hand +\n  // nearest_expiry the moment the screen opens, in parallel with the\n  // local read. If the server differs, we surface a \"Stock has changed\"\n  // prompt before letting the user save. If the fetch fails (timeout /\n  // offline / 5xx), we block the save entirely — corrections must be\n  // committed against verified state per the cache-freshness plan.\n  const [verifyStatus, setVerifyStatus] = useState<VerifyStatus>('verifying');\n  const [serverQty, setServerQty] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const [serverExpiry, setServerExpiry] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let cancelled = false;\n    (async () => {\n      const r = await findStock(barcode);\n      const exp = await getNearestExpiry(barcode);\n      if (cancelled) return;\n      if (!r) return;\n      setRow(r);\n      setOriginalQty(r.on_hand);\n      setOriginalExpiry(exp);\n      setQty(r.on_hand);\n      if (exp) {\n        setExpiryDateObj(new Date(exp));\n        setNoExpiry(false);\n      } else {\n        setNoExpiry(true);\n      }\n    })();\n    return () => { cancelled = true; };\n  }, [barcode]);\n\n  // Fire the JIT fetch independently of the local read so neither\n  // blocks the other. The 3 s timeout matches CatalogPickerScreen.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let cancelled = false;\n    const controller = new AbortController();\n    const t = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3000);\n    (async () => {\n      try {\n        const remote = await api.fetch.stockOne(barcode);\n        if (cancelled) return;\n        const remoteQty = Number(remote.on_hand);\n        const remoteExpiry = remote.nearest_expiry ?? null;\n        setServerQty(remoteQty);\n        setServerExpiry(remoteExpiry);\n        setVerifyStatus('ok'); // narrowed to 'conflict' below once we know local values\n      } catch (err) {\n        if (cancelled) return;\n        // 404 = no stock row on server; treat as authoritative \"absent\"\n        // — the correction will create it. Same UX as a clean ok.\n        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {\n          setVerifyStatus('absent');\n          return;\n        }\n        // 401 is handled centrally; clear navigates to Login.\n        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) return;\n        setVerifyStatus(isOfflineError(err) ? 'offline' : 'error');\n      } finally {\n        clearTimeout(t);\n      }\n    })();\n    return () => { cancelled = true; clearTimeout(t); controller.abort(); };\n  }, [barcode]);\n\n  // Once both local and server have settled, classify ok-vs-conflict.\n  // Conflict iff server differs from what the user is about to edit.\n  // We use originalQty/originalExpiry as the \"what the user opened\n  // with\" reference — those don't change unless the user explicitly\n  // accepts the new server values.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (verifyStatus !== 'ok' || row === null || serverQty === null) return;\n    const qtyDiffers = Math.abs(serverQty - originalQty) > 1e-9;\n    const expiryDiffers = (serverExpiry ?? null) !== (originalExpiry ?? null);\n    if (qtyDiffers || expiryDiffers) setVerifyStatus('conflict');\n  }, [verifyStatus, row, serverQty, serverExpiry, originalQty, originalExpiry]);"
}

  "new_string": "  useEffect(() => {\n    if (initialBarcode) {\n      findStock(initialBarcode).then((r) => r && setSelected(r));\n    }\n  }, [initialBarcode]);\n\n  // Whenever an item gets selected, re-run JIT verification. The\n  // dependency on `selected?.barcode` (not the whole row) means\n  // refetching only happens for an actual product change, not for the\n  // optimistic update we apply after submit.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!selected) {\n      setVerifyStatus('verifying');\n      setVerifiedOnHand(null);\n      return;\n    }\n    let cancelled = false;\n    setVerifyStatus('verifying');\n    setVerifiedOnHand(null);\n    const controller = new AbortController();\n    const t = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 3000);\n    (async () => {\n      try {\n        const remote = await api.fetch.stockOne(selected.barcode);\n        if (cancelled) return;\n        const remoteQty = Number(remote.on_hand);\n        setVerifiedOnHand(remoteQty);\n        setVerifyStatus('ok');\n        // Clamp qty to the verified ceiling. If the user had a stale\n        // higher qty in mind (e.g. local cache said 10, server says 7),\n        // drop to the new max so they can't accidentally submit over.\n        if (qty > remoteQty) setQty(Math.max(1, remoteQty));\n      } catch (err) {\n        if (cancelled) return;\n        // 404: the server has no record of this barcode (might be a\n        // device-only item never received server-side). Treat as\n        // \"absent\" — block dispense; you can't dispense what the\n        // server doesn't know exists.\n        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {\n          setVerifyStatus('absent');\n          return;\n        }\n        if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 401) return;\n        setVerifyStatus(isOfflineError(err) ? 'offline' : 'error');\n      } finally {\n        clearTimeout(t);\n      }\n    })();\n    return () => { cancelled = true; clearTimeout(t); controller.abort(); };\n    // intentionally NOT in deps: `qty` would re-run the JIT on every\n    // stepper tap; only refire when the selected barcode changes.\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [selected?.barcode]);"
}

35	const DISPATCH: Record<OutboxKind, (payload: object) => Promise<unknown>> = {
36	  receipt: api.send.receipt,
37	  product_registration: api.send.product,
38	  issue: api.send.issue,
39	  dispense: api.send.issue, // same endpoint, renamed in-app
40	  reorder_request: api.send.reorderRequest,
41	  mismatch_flag: api.send.mismatchFlag,
42	  learn_barcode: api.send.learnBarcode,
43	  stock_correction: api.send.stockCorrection,
44	};

46	export async function flushOnce(): Promise<{ sent: number; failed: number }> {
47	  if (running) {
48	    // Queue a retry so manual "Sync Now" taps aren't silently dropped.
49	    retryQueued = true;
50	    return { sent: 0, failed: 0 };
51	  }
52	  running = true;
53	  let sent = 0;
54	  let failed = 0;
55	  try {
56	    const net = await Network.getNetworkStateAsync().catch(() => ({ isConnected: false }));
57	    if (!net.isConnected) return { sent, failed };
58	
59	    // Recover anything orphaned by a prior crash/kill before picking
60	    // the batch — otherwise the stuck row will be skipped again.
61	    await recoverOrphanedSending().catch(() => 0);
62	
63	    const batch = await nextBatch(config.outboxBatchSize);
64	    for (const row of batch) {
65	      const dispatch = DISPATCH[row.kind];
66	      if (!dispatch) {
67	        await markFailed(row.id, `Unknown kind: ${row.kind}`);
68	        failed++;
69	        continue;
70	      }
71	      try {
72	        await markSending(row.id);
73	        await dispatch(JSON.parse(row.payload));
74	        await markSent(row.id);
75	        sent++;
76	      } catch (err) {
77	        await markFailed(row.id, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
78	        failed++;
79	      }
80	    }
81	    if (sent > 0 || failed > 0) {
82	      _lastSyncAt = Date.now();
83	    }
84	
85	    // Skip pulls when not logged in — they'd 401 and only add log noise.
86	    // Login screen / AuthProvider will trigger a flush after a successful login.
87	    if (getSession()?.token) {
88	      // Pull latest canonical products catalog on each sync cycle.
89	      // This is lightweight (just a GET) and ensures guards always
90	      // have the latest admin-curated product names.
91	      await syncCanonicalProducts().catch(() => {});
92	      // Also mirror the open-orders list so ReceivingScreen can resolve
93	      // catalog picks to the right order item without a round-trip.
94	      await syncOrders().catch(() => {});
95	    }
96	  } finally {
97	    running = false;
98	    await notify();
99	  }
100	
101	  // If someone tapped "Sync Now" while we were busy, run again.
102	  if (retryQueued) {
103	    retryQueued = false;
104	    return flushOnce();
105	  }
106	
107	  return { sent, failed };
108	}
109

{

  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/sync/syncService.ts",
  "old_string": "// Dispatch table for outbox kinds. Adding a new kind = one line here\n// + a handler in api.send.*.\nconst DISPATCH: Record<OutboxKind, (payload: object) => Promise<unknown>> = {\n  receipt: api.send.receipt,\n  product_registration: api.send.product,\n  issue: api.send.issue,\n  dispense: api.send.issue, // same endpoint, renamed in-app\n  reorder_request: api.send.reorderRequest,\n  mismatch_flag: api.send.mismatchFlag,\n  learn_barcode: api.send.learnBarcode,\n  stock_correction: api.send.stockCorrection,\n};",
  "new_string": "// Dispatch table for outbox kinds. Adding a new kind = one line here\n// + a handler in api.send.*.\nconst DISPATCH: Record<OutboxKind, (payload: object) => Promise<unknown>> = {\n  receipt: api.send.receipt,\n  product_registration: api.send.product,\n  issue: api.send.issue,\n  dispense: api.send.issue, // same endpoint, renamed in-app\n  reorder_request: api.send.reorderRequest,\n  mismatch_flag: api.send.mismatchFlag,\n  learn_barcode: api.send.learnBarcode,\n  stock_correction: api.send.stockCorrection,\n};\n\n// Per-kind cache invalidation map. After a successful dispatch we\n// refetch the listed caches so the local view reflects the server's\n// truth instead of just the optimistic write — closes the lag where\n// \"I just dispensed, but the Stock list still shows the old count\".\n// Empty list = no read-side cache touched (e.g. learn_barcode is\n// internal plumbing, no user-visible refresh needed).\nconst POST_WRITE_INVALIDATES: Record<OutboxKind, CacheKey[]> = {\n  receipt: ['stock', 'orders'],\n  product_registration: ['catalog'],\n  issue: ['stock', 'alerts'],\n  dispense: ['stock', 'alerts'],\n  reorder_request: ['alerts'],\n  mismatch_flag: ['orders'],\n  learn_barcode: [],\n  stock_correction: ['stock', 'alerts'],\n};\n\n/**\n * Pull the freshest read-side data for each touched cache. Called once\n * per flushOnce after the outbox batch settles. Each fetch runs in\n * parallel and updates its cache state independently so a stock pull\n * failing doesn't block an orders pull.\n */\nasync function invalidateAffectedCaches(keys: Set<CacheKey>): Promise<void> {\n  const work: Promise<void>[] = [];\n  for (const key of keys) {\n    invalidateRefetchThrottle(key);\n    work.push(refreshOne(key));\n  }\n  await Promise.allSettled(work);\n}\n\nasync function refreshOne(key: CacheKey): Promise<void> {\n  cache.refreshing(key);\n  try {\n    if (key === 'stock' || key === 'alerts') {\n      const remote = await api.fetch.stock();\n      await replaceStockFromRemote(\n        remote.map((r) => ({\n          barcode: r.barcode,\n          name: r.name,\n          category: r.category,\n          unit: r.unit,\n          pack_size: r.pack_size != null ? Number(r.pack_size) : null,\n          on_hand: Number(r.on_hand),\n          threshold: Number(r.threshold),\n        })),\n      );\n    } else if (key === 'orders') {\n      await syncOrders();\n    } else if (key === 'catalog') {\n      await syncCanonicalProducts();\n    }\n    cache.warm(key);\n  } catch (err) {\n    const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);\n    if (\n      msg.includes('Network request failed') ||\n      msg.includes('TypeError: Network') ||\n      msg.includes('Unable to resolve host')\n    ) {\n      cache.offline(key);\n    } else {\n      cache.error(key);\n    }\n  }\n}"
}

  "old_string": "    const batch = await nextBatch(config.outboxBatchSize);\n    for (const row of batch) {\n      const dispatch = DISPATCH[row.kind];\n      if (!dispatch) {\n        await markFailed(row.id, `Unknown kind: ${row.kind}`);\n        failed++;\n        continue;\n      }\n      try {\n        await markSending(row.id);\n        await dispatch(JSON.parse(row.payload));\n        await markSent(row.id);\n        sent++;\n      } catch (err) {\n        await markFailed(row.id, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));\n        failed++;\n      }\n    }\n    if (sent > 0 || failed > 0) {\n      _lastSyncAt = Date.now();\n    }",
  "new_string": "    // Track which read-side caches got touched by this batch so we can\n    // refresh them once, after the loop. Refetching per-row would\n    // multiply network calls on a delivery scan (50 receipts → 50 stock\n    // pulls); coalescing here means at most one pull per cache per batch.\n    const touched = new Set<CacheKey>();\n\n    const batch = await nextBatch(config.outboxBatchSize);\n    for (const row of batch) {\n      const dispatch = DISPATCH[row.kind];\n      if (!dispatch) {\n        await markFailed(row.id, `Unknown kind: ${row.kind}`);\n        failed++;\n        continue;\n      }\n      try {\n        await markSending(row.id);\n        await dispatch(JSON.parse(row.payload));\n        await markSent(row.id);\n        sent++;\n        for (const k of POST_WRITE_INVALIDATES[row.kind]) touched.add(k);\n      } catch (err) {\n        await markFailed(row.id, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));\n        failed++;\n      }\n    }\n    if (sent > 0 || failed > 0) {\n      _lastSyncAt = Date.now();\n    }\n\n    // Post-write cache refresh — runs only when at least one write\n    // committed AND we're still logged in. Bypasses the throttle so the\n    // user sees their write reflected immediately, without waiting for\n    // the next periodic timer tick.\n    if (touched.size > 0 && getSession()?.token) {\n      await invalidateAffectedCaches(touched);\n    }"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/components/OfflineBanner.tsx",
  "content": "/**\n * Global \"Offline — showing saved data\" banner.\n *\n * Mounted once above the navigator in `RootNavigator`. Shows whenever\n * `Network.getNetworkStateAsync().isConnected === false`, hides as soon\n * as connectivity returns. Stays out of the way otherwise (zero height\n * when online — no layout shift).\n *\n * We listen via `addNetworkStateListener` rather than polling so the\n * banner appears within ~1 s of the radio losing signal. As a safety\n * net we also poll once on mount because the OS event isn't guaranteed\n * to fire on cold start.\n */\n\nimport { WifiOff } from 'lucide-react-native';\nimport * as Network from 'expo-network';\nimport React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';\n\nimport { Text } from '../design';\nimport { useT } from '../i18n';\nimport { useTheme } from '../theme';\n\nexport function OfflineBanner() {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const [offline, setOffline] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let unsubscribe: { remove: () => void } | null = null;\n\n    // One initial probe so we don't miss the \"boot offline\" case (the\n    // event listener only fires on transitions).\n    Network.getNetworkStateAsync()\n      .then((s) => setOffline(!s.isConnected))\n      .catch(() => {});\n\n    try {\n      unsubscribe = Network.addNetworkStateListener((state) => {\n        setOffline(!state.isConnected);\n      });\n    } catch {\n      // Older expo-network builds may not have the listener API; the\n      // initial probe + the per-request 401 path are good enough.\n    }\n\n    return () => {\n      if (unsubscribe) {\n        try { unsubscribe.remove(); } catch { /* swallow */ }\n      }\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  if (!offline) return null;\n\n  return (\n    <SafeAreaView edges={['top']} style={{ backgroundColor: palette.error }}>\n      <View style={styles.bar}>\n        <WifiOff size={16} color={palette.onError} strokeWidth={2.4} />\n        <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onError} style={{ marginLeft: 8 }}>\n          {t('offlineBanner')}\n        </Text>\n      </View>\n    </SafeAreaView>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  bar: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n    paddingVertical: 6,\n    paddingHorizontal: 12,\n  },\n});\n"
}

> AGENT

All 8 Phase-1 steps shipped and type-clean. Here's the summary.

## What changed

### office-ops-engine

- `src/mastra/modules/inventory/db.ts` — `findCanonicalProductFull(siteId, productId)` and `findStockJit(siteId, barcode)` (single-row reads with the same live-computed columns the list queries return, plus nearest-future receipt expiry).
- `src/mastra/modules/inventory/routes.ts` — `GET /api/inventory/canonical-products/:productId` and `GET /api/inventory/stock/:barcode`. The catalog route guards against the `/aliases` / `/barcodes` sub-paths and the `barcode` / `learn-barcode` exact-match siblings.

### field-app

**Cache infrastructure (new):**
- `src/sync/cacheStatus.ts` — per-key state machine (`cold/warm/refreshing/error/offline`) + `hasEverBeenWarm` flag + `useCacheStatus(key)` hook + `resetAllCacheStates()`. Module-singleton notifier; per-key subscriptions so a Stock change doesn't re-render Home Orders.
- `src/sync/refetch.ts` — `refetchThrottled(key, fn)` with 2 s throttle + in-flight dedupe. `invalidateRefetchThrottle(key)` for pull-to-refresh / post-write paths.
- `src/sync/warmCache.ts` — parallel `syncCanonicalProducts` + `syncOrders` + `fetchStock` + alerts-mirror. Each warmOne handles offline vs error vs success. Fire-and-forget — non-blocking.

**Cache infrastructure (removed):**
- `src/sync/cacheTime.ts` — deleted; the TTL gate it implemented was replaced by always-refetch-on-focus + throttle.

**Wired into:**
- `src/sync/api.ts` — central 401 handler clears the session (only when a token was actually sent, so the LoginScreen's own 401 still surfaces). New `api.fetch.canonicalProduct(id)` and `api.fetch.stockOne(barcode)`.
- `src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx` — `warmCache()` fires after `setSession()` on login AND after `hydrateSession()` on app boot if a session existed. `onSessionChange(null)` resets cache states + throttles centrally so logout / 401 / external storage change all converge to the same clean slate.
- `src/sync/syncService.ts` — per-kind `POST_WRITE_INVALIDATES` map; after a successful outbox dispatch, the affected caches refresh (stock + alerts for issue/dispense/correction, stock + orders for receipt, etc.). Coalesced once per batch — a 50-receipt delivery scan produces one stock pull, not 50.

**Per-screen refactors (drops the TTL gate, adds the JIT layer where relevant):**
- `HomeScreen` — tiles read `useCacheStatus('stock')` / `useCacheStatus('orders')` / `useCacheStatus('alerts')`. `loading = !hasEverBeenWarm && state ∉ {error, offline}`. Subscribes to cache state to re-read SQLite on warm transitions.
- `StockScreen`, `OrdersScreen`, `AlertsScreen`, `CatalogPickerScreen` — same refetch-on-focus pattern, same skeleton derivation, same subscription pattern.
- `CatalogPickerScreen` — JIT verification on pick: inline `ActivityIndicator + "Verifying…"` in the tapped row, 3 s timeout, fail-closed banner. Lookup order: `incomingBarcode` → verified `primary_barcode` → local fallback → synthetic `catalog_<id>`. Other rows dim to 0.5 opacity and are disabled while verification is in flight.
- `EditStockScreen` — JIT on open in parallel with local read. Six states (`verifying/ok/absent/conflict/offline/error`). When server differs, an amber prompt shows `originalQty → serverQty[ · serverExpiry]` with `Use new count` / `Keep my edit`. Both options rebase `originalQty/originalExpiry` to the server's value so the audit record reflects the real transition. Save disabled in `verifying/conflict/offline/error`.
- `DispenseScreen` — JIT on item-select. `QtyStepper.max = verifiedOnHand ?? selected.on_hand`. Confirm disabled unless `verifyStatus === 'ok'`. `absent` (404) treated as fail-closed because you can't dispense what the server doesn't know exists. Auto-clamps qty if a user-edited number exceeds the verified max.

**Global UX:**
- `src/components/OfflineBanner.tsx` — thin error-colored banner with `WifiOff` icon. Mounted in `RootNavigator` above the navigator. Listens via `Network.addNetworkStateListener` + initial probe. Hidden when online — zero layout cost.
- `src/navigation/RootNavigator.tsx` — wraps the navigator in a `<View>` with the banner pinned at top.
- `src/i18n/strings.ts` — new keys: `verifying`, `couldntVerify`, `refreshingLabel`, `offlineBanner`, `loginNeedsInternet`, `stockUpdatedTitle`, `stockUpdatedBody`, `useNewCount`, `keepMyEdit`.

## What's foolproof now

- **No-barcode catalog pick** always commits against a live single-row server fetch. Stale `primary_barcode` in the cache can't produce a wrong receipt — the navigation params come from the JIT response, not the cached row. 3 s timeout + fail-closed banner.
- **EditStock** cannot save against a stale view. If the server's count moved while the user was deciding, the prompt surfaces the diff and the save button is gated until the conflict is resolved or verification fails (then disabled). The audit record's `old_qty` always equals the server's value at save time.
- **Dispense** confirm requires a verified count. `QtyStepper.max` is the verified value — typing 10 against a stock of 7 is physically impossible.
- **Login spam from dead sessions** is fixed at the source — the central 401 handler clears the session in one place; the AuthProvider listener drops to LoginScreen and resets cache states + throttles.

## What to verify on a real device

- Cold login → Home tiles show skeletons → tiles fill in within ~1 s of `warmCache` completion (one per cache, not all at once).
- Open Stock → cached rows show instantly → "Refreshing…" hint while in flight → list updates if server differs. Pull-to-refresh bypasses the 2 s throttle.
- Scanner → No barcode → pick a catalog item → row shows spinner for ~300 ms → ReceivingScreen opens with the verified barcode. Now turn airplane mode on and try again — should see the red banner and the pick should NOT navigate.
- Have two devices: edit stock on phone A, open EditStock on phone B → the amber conflict banner should appear with the new server qty.
- Dispense an item with phone offline — Confirm should stay disabled; the inline banner should show "Couldn't verify — try again".
- Toggle airplane mode while any screen is open — the global red "Offline — showing saved data" banner should appear within ~1 s.

Nothing committed or deployed.

> AGENT

I've got the picture. Let me first answer your question about the dash, then plan the changes.

**The dash:** in `AddItemRow` (orders tab), there's a `<td colSpan={3}>—</td>` that merges the **Pack**, **Unit**, and **Received** columns into one em-dash placeholder. The reasoning was "you haven't added this item yet, so none of those columns apply." But with the suggestive-input change you're asking for, Pack and Unit are known the moment a product is selected (from the catalog), so dashing them is misleading. I'll show real Pack/Unit values once a product is picked, and dash only the Received column (which genuinely doesn't apply until the item is added).

Here's my plan — I'll implement in this order:

**Catalog tab — pencil + edit-row UX:**
1. Replace small Pencil icon with a labeled "Edit" button (icon + text), styled with the ghost-button treatment so it reads as an action, not a decoration.
2. When Edit is clicked, also auto-open the expansion row (barcodes + vendor names) so the admin edits everything in one flow.
3. Replace tiny Save/Cancel icons with labeled buttons ("Save" / "Cancel" with icons), bigger and clearly differentiated (primary vs ghost).

**Orders tab — AddItemRow:**
4. Replace the `<select>` with a typeahead input — typing filters catalog by name/HSN/category; clicking a suggestion auto-fills HSN, qty (1), pack, unit; rate stays blank for admin to enter.
5. Show real Pack/Unit values in their own cells once a product is selected. Dash only the Received column.
6. Change the bare `+` button to "+ Add" with the Plus icon.

Let me implement.

248	function CategoryCombobox({
249	  value,
250	  catalog,
251	  onChange,
252	  style,
253	}: {
254	  value: string;
255	  catalog: CanonicalProduct[];
256	  onChange: (v: string) => void;
257	  style?: React.CSSProperties;
258	}) {
259	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
260	  const [activeIdx, setActiveIdx] = useState(0);
261	  const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
262	
263	  useEffect(() => {
264	    function onDocClick(e: MouseEvent) {
265	      if (!wrapRef.current) return;
266	      if (!wrapRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
267	    }
268	    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onDocClick);
269	    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDocClick);
270	  }, []);
271	
272	  const options = useMemo(() => {
273	    const used = new Set<string>();
274	    for (const p of catalog) {
275	      const c = (p.category ?? "").trim();
276	      if (c) used.add(c);
277	    }
278	    // Union: seed list + any custom categories already in the catalog.
279	    const merged = [...new Set([...CATEGORY_SEED, ...Array.from(used)])];
280	    const q = value.trim().toLowerCase();
281	    if (!q) return merged.sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
282	    return merged
283	      .filter((c) => c.toLowerCase().includes(q))
284	      .sort((a, b) => {
285	        const al = a.toLowerCase();
286	        const bl = b.toLowerCase();
287	        const aStarts = al.startsWith(q) ? 0 : 1;
288	        const bStarts = bl.startsWith(q) ? 0 : 1;
289	        if (aStarts !== bStarts) return aStarts - bStarts;
290	        return a.localeCompare(b);
291	      });
292	  }, [catalog, value]);
293	
294	  function commit(c: string) {
295	    onChange(c);
296	    setOpen(false);
297	    setActiveIdx(0);
298	  }
299	
300	  return (
301	    <div ref={wrapRef} style={{ position: "relative" }}>
302	      <input
303	        type="text"
304	        value={value}
305	        onChange={(e) => { onChange(e.target.value); setOpen(true); setActiveIdx(0); }}
306	        onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
307	        onKeyDown={(e) => {
308	          if (e.key === "ArrowDown") { e.preventDefault(); setOpen(true); setActiveIdx((i) => Math.min(options.length - 1, i + 1)); }
309	          else if (e.key === "ArrowUp") { e.preventDefault(); setActiveIdx((i) => Math.max(0, i - 1)); }
310	          else if (e.key === "Enter" && open && options[activeIdx]) { e.preventDefault(); commit(options[activeIdx]); }
311	          else if (e.key === "Escape") { setOpen(false); }
312	        }}
313	        placeholder="Type or pick a category…"
314	        autoComplete="off"
315	        style={style ?? input}
316	      />
317	      {open && options.length > 0 && (
318	        <div
319	          style={{
320	            position: "absolute",
321	            top: "calc(100% + 2px)",
322	            left: 0,
323	            right: 0,
324	            zIndex: 30,
325	            maxHeight: 200,

334	            <div
335	              key={c}
336	              onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); commit(c); }}
337	              onMouseEnter={() => setActiveIdx(i)}

[];\n  const [productId, setProductId] = useState(\"\");\n  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);\n  const [rate, setRate] = useState<number | null>(null);\n\n  const addMutation = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: () => {\n      const p = products.find((x) => x.product_id === productId);\n      if (!p) throw new Error(\"Pick a product\");\n      return addOrderItem(orderId, {\n        product_id: p.product_id,\n        canonical_name: p.canonical_name,\n        hsn_code: p.hsn_code,\n        qty,\n        rate,\n      });\n    },\n    onSuccess: (res) => {\n      if (!res.ok) { toastError(errorMessage((res.data as { error?: string }).error, \"Add failed\")); return; }\n      toastSuccess(\"Item added\");\n      setProductId(\"\");\n      setQty(1);\n      setRate(null);\n      onAdded();\n    },\n    onError: (err) => toastError(err, \"Add failed\"),\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <tr style={{ background: \"var(--surface, #fafafa)\" }}>\n      <td style={tdCell}>\n        <select value={productId} onChange={(e) => setProductId(e.target.value)} style={cellInput}>\n          <option value=\"\">+ Add an item from catalog…</option>\n          {products.map((p) => (\n            <option key={p.product_id} value={p.product_id}>\n              {p.canonical_name} ({p.pack_size} {p.unit})\n            </option>\n          ))}\n        </select>\n      </td>\n      <td style={tdCell}>\n        <span style={{ color: \"var(--muted)\", fontSize: 11 }}>from catalog</span>\n      </td>\n      <td style={tdCell}>\n        <NumericInput value={qty} onChange={setQty} style={{ ...cellInput, textAlign: \"right\" }} />\n      </td>\n      <td style={tdCell}>\n        <NumericInput value={rate ?? 0} onChange={(v) => setRate(v)} placeholder=\"rate\" style={{ ...cellInput, textAlign: \"right\" }} />\n      </td>\n      <td colSpan={3} style={{ ...tdCell, textAlign: \"right\", color: \"var(--muted)\" }}>—</td>\n      <td style={{ ...tdCell, textAlign: \"right\" }}>\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          className=\"primary-button\"\n          disabled={!productId || addMutation.isPending}\n          onClick={() => addMutation.mutate()}\n          style={{ padding: \"2px 8px\", fontSize: 11 }}\n          title=\"Add item\"\n        >\n          <Plus size={12} />\n        </button>\n      </td>\n    </tr>\n  );\n}",
  "new_string": "/**\n * Suggestive product picker for the order-item add row. Replaces the\n * old <select> that listed every catalog product in a single dropdown\n * (unscrollable past ~50 items on most setups). The admin types a few\n * letters of the name or HSN; matches drop down in real time and the\n * arrow keys move the highlight. Selecting one fires `onSelect` with\n * the full canonical product so the parent can auto-fill HSN, pack,\n * unit, and reset qty to 1.\n *\n * Visually it's a plain text input — no chevron or fake select chrome —\n * because once a product is picked we want the input to show the chosen\n * name. Re-typing clears the selection.\n */\nfunction ProductTypeahead({\n  products,\n  selected,\n  onSelect,\n  onClear,\n  placeholder,\n}: {\n  products: CanonicalProduct[];\n  selected: CanonicalProduct | null;\n  onSelect: (p: CanonicalProduct) => void;\n  onClear: () => void;\n  placeholder?: string;\n}) {\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState(selected?.canonical_name ?? \"\");\n  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [activeIdx, setActiveIdx] = useState(0);\n  const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n\n  // Re-sync the visible text when the parent clears or swaps the\n  // selection externally (e.g. after a successful add).\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setQuery(selected?.canonical_name ?? \"\");\n  }, [selected]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    function onDocClick(e: MouseEvent) {\n      if (!wrapRef.current) return;\n      if (!wrapRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);\n    }\n    document.addEventListener(\"mousedown\", onDocClick);\n    return () => document.removeEventListener(\"mousedown\", onDocClick);\n  }, []);\n\n  const options = useMemo(() => {\n    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();\n    if (!q) {\n      return [...products]\n        .sort((a, b) => a.canonical_name.localeCompare(b.canonical_name))\n        .slice(0, 20);\n    }\n    const scored: Array<{ p: CanonicalProduct; score: number }> = [];\n    for (const p of products) {\n      const name = p.canonical_name.toLowerCase();\n      const hsn = (p.hsn_code ?? \"\").toLowerCase();\n      const cat = (p.category ?? \"\").toLowerCase();\n      let score = 0;\n      if (name === q) score = 4;\n      else if (name.startsWith(q)) score = 3;\n      else if (name.includes(q)) score = 2;\n      else if (hsn.includes(q) || cat.includes(q)) score = 1;\n      if (score > 0) scored.push({ p, score });\n    }\n    scored.sort((a, b) => {\n      if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;\n      return a.p.canonical_name.localeCompare(b.p.canonical_name);\n    });\n    return scored.slice(0, 20).map((x) => x.p);\n  }, [products, query]);\n\n  function commit(p: CanonicalProduct) {\n    onSelect(p);\n    setQuery(p.canonical_name);\n    setOpen(false);\n    setActiveIdx(0);\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div ref={wrapRef} style={{ position: \"relative\" }}>\n      <input\n        type=\"text\"\n        value={query}\n        onChange={(e) => {\n          setQuery(e.target.value);\n          setOpen(true);\n          setActiveIdx(0);\n          // Typing after a selection means the admin is searching again —\n          // clear the locked-in product so HSN/pack/unit stop showing\n          // stale values.\n          if (selected) onClear();\n        }}\n        onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}\n        onKeyDown={(e) => {\n          if (e.key === \"ArrowDown\") { e.preventDefault(); setOpen(true); setActiveIdx((i) => Math.min(options.length - 1, i + 1)); }\n          else if (e.key === \"ArrowUp\") { e.preventDefault(); setActiveIdx((i) => Math.max(0, i - 1)); }\n          else if (e.key === \"Enter\" && open && options[activeIdx]) { e.preventDefault(); commit(options[activeIdx]); }\n          else if (e.key === \"Escape\") { setOpen(false); }\n        }}\n        placeholder={placeholder ?? \"Type product name, HSN, or category…\"}\n        autoComplete=\"off\"\n        style={cellInput}\n      />\n      {open && options.length > 0 && (\n        <div\n          style={{\n            position: \"absolute\",\n            top: \"calc(100% + 2px)\",\n            left: 0,\n            // Wider than the input so long product names + their pack/unit\n            // hint don't get truncated.\n            minWidth: 280,\n            zIndex: 30,\n            maxHeight: 260,\n            overflowY: \"auto\",\n            background: \"var(--surface, #fff)\",\n            border: \"1px solid var(--line)\",\n            borderRadius: 6,\n            boxShadow: \"0 6px 18px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)\",\n          }}\n        >\n          {options.map((p, i) => (\n            <div\n              key={p.product_id}\n              onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); commit(p); }}\n              onMouseEnter={() => setActiveIdx(i)}\n              style={{\n                padding: \"6px 10px\",\n                cursor: \"pointer\",\n                background: i === activeIdx ? \"var(--line)\" : \"transparent\",\n                fontSize: 12,\n                display: \"grid\",\n                gridTemplateColumns: \"1fr auto\",\n                gap: 8,\n                alignItems: \"center\",\n              }}\n            >\n              <div style={{ minWidth: 0, overflow: \"hidden\", textOverflow: \"ellipsis\", whiteSpace: \"nowrap\" }}>\n                <span>{p.canonical_name}</span>\n                {p.hsn_code ? (\n                  <span style={{ color: \"var(--muted)\", marginLeft: 6 }}>HSN {p.hsn_code}</span>\n                ) : null}\n              </div>\n              <span style={{ color: \"var(--muted)\", fontSize: 11 }}>\n                {p.pack_size} {p.unit}\n              </span>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n      )}\n      {open && options.length === 0 && query.trim().length > 0 && (\n        <div\n          style={{\n            position: \"absolute\",\n            top: \"calc(100% + 2px)\",\n            left: 0,\n            minWidth: 280,\n            zIndex: 30,\n            background: \"var(--surface, #fff)\",\n            border: \"1px solid var(--line)\",\n            borderRadius: 6,\n            padding: \"6px 10px\",\n            fontSize: 12,\n            color: \"var(--muted)\",\n            fontStyle: \"italic\",\n          }}\n        >\n          No matching products.\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction AddItemRow({ orderId, onAdded }: { orderId: string; onAdded: () => void }) {\n  const productsQuery = useQuery({ queryKey: [\"inv-canonical\"], queryFn: listCanonicalProducts });\n  const products = productsQuery.data?.data?.products ?? [];\n  // Hold the full product object so HSN / pack / unit are available for\n  // auto-fill without a second lookup at submit time.\n  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<CanonicalProduct | null>(null);\n  const [qty, setQty] = useState<number>(1);\n  const [rate, setRate] = useState<number | null>(null);\n\n  const addMutation = useMutation({\n    mutationFn: () => {\n      if (!selected) throw new Error(\"Pick a product\");\n      return addOrderItem(orderId, {\n        product_id: selected.product_id,\n        canonical_name: selected.canonical_name,\n        hsn_code: selected.hsn_code,\n        qty,\n        rate,\n      });\n    },\n    onSuccess: (res) => {\n      if (!res.ok) { toastError(errorMessage((res.data as { error?: string }).error, \"Add failed\")); return; }\n      toastSuccess(\"Item added\");\n      setSelected(null);\n      setQty(1);\n      setRate(null);\n      onAdded();\n    },\n    onError: (err) => toastError(err, \"Add failed\"),\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <tr style={{ background: \"var(--surface, #fafafa)\" }}>\n      <td style={tdCell}>\n        <ProductTypeahead\n          products={products}\n          selected={selected}\n          onSelect={(p) => {\n            setSelected(p);\n            // Sensible defaults for the order line: qty 1, rate blank\n            // (rate varies per bill — admin types it after selecting).\n            setQty(1);\n            setRate(null);\n          }}\n          onClear={() => setSelected(null)}\n        />\n      </td>\n      <td style={tdCell}>\n        {/* HSN auto-fills from the catalog. Display-only so admins can't\n            accidentally diverge the order from the standard product's\n            HSN — edit at the catalog level instead. */}\n        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: selected?.hsn_code ? \"var(--text)\" : \"var(--muted)\" }}>\n          {selected?.hsn_code ?? \"—\"}\n        </span>\n      </td>\n      <td style={tdCell}>\n        <NumericInput value={qty} onChange={setQty} style={{ ...cellInput, textAlign: \"right\" }} />\n      </td>\n      <td style={tdCell}>\n        <NumericInput value={rate ?? 0} onChange={(v) => setRate(v)} placeholder=\"rate\" style={{ ...cellInput, textAlign: \"right\" }} />\n      </td>\n      <td style={{ ...tdCell, textAlign: \"right\" }}>\n        {/* Pack size from the selected catalog product. Same reasoning\n            as HSN — edit at catalog level, not per-order. */}\n        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: selected ? \"var(--text)\" : \"var(--muted)\" }}>\n          {selected ? selected.pack_size : \"—\"}\n        </span>\n      </td>\n      <td style={tdCell}>\n        <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: selected ? \"var(--text)\" : \"var(--muted)\" }}>\n          {selected?.unit ?? \"—\"}\n        </span>\n      </td>\n      <td style={{ ...tdCell, textAlign: \"right\", color: \"var(--muted)\" }}>\n        {/* Received is \"—\" until the item exists in the order — there's\n            nothing to have received yet. The colSpan-3 merge that used\n            to span Pack/Unit/Received here was misleading because\n            Pack/Unit ARE known the moment a product is picked. */}\n        —\n      </td>\n      <td style={{ ...tdCell, textAlign: \"right\" }}>\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          className=\"primary-button\"\n          disabled={!selected || addMutation.isPending}\n          onClick={() => addMutation.mutate()}\n          style={{ padding: \"4px 10px\", fontSize: 12, display: \"inline-flex\", alignItems: \"center\", gap: 4 }}\n          title=\"Add this item to the order\"\n        >\n          {addMutation.isPending ? \"Adding…\" : (<><Plus size={14} /> Add</>)}\n        </button>\n      </td>\n    </tr>\n  );\n}"
}

> AGENT

Type-clean.

## What changed

**Catalog tab (`CatalogProductRow`)**
- The Edit button moved from a 12 px pencil icon to a labeled **"Edit"** ghost button (icon + text), much more discoverable. `InventorySection.tsx:2007-2014`.
- Clicking Edit now also auto-opens the expansion row (barcodes + vendor aliases), so the admin can change name/category/HSN/unit/pack **and** add/remove barcodes and aliases in one continuous flow — no more "edit → save → click chevron → edit barcode" dance. `InventorySection.tsx:1833-1834`.
- The Save and Cancel icons in edit mode are now full **"Save"** / **"Cancel"** buttons with their icons, primary and ghost styling respectively. While saving, the label shows "Saving…". `InventorySection.tsx:1972-1996`.

**Orders tab (`AddItemRow`)**
- New `ProductTypeahead` component replaces the long `<select>`. Type any part of the product name, HSN, or category — matches drop down ranked exact > prefix > substring > HSN/category. Arrow keys navigate, Enter selects, Escape closes. Shows pack/unit in the suggestion row so you confirm what you're picking. `InventorySection.tsx:1349-1494`.
- On selection, the row auto-fills HSN, Pack, and Unit from the catalog (display-only — editing those should happen at the catalog level, not per order). Qty defaults to 1; Rate stays blank for admin entry.
- The bare `<Plus />` button is now **"+ Add"** with the icon and text. While submitting, it shows "Adding…". `InventorySection.tsx:1593-1602`.
- About **the dash**: the previous code had `colSpan={3}>—` merging the Pack / Unit / Received columns into one em-dash. With the typeahead, Pack and Unit are known the moment a product is selected, so they now render real values in their own cells. The dash remains **only on the Received column**, which genuinely doesn't apply until the item exists in the order. `InventorySection.tsx:1571-1589`.

## To verify in the browser

- Catalog tab → row hover shows the "Edit" button clearly. Click it → row goes editable AND the barcode/alias expander opens beneath in the same gesture.
- Catalog row in edit mode → big green "Save" and gray "Cancel" buttons, both with icons. Save is disabled until you actually change something (existing behavior preserved).
- Orders tab → open any order → in the "Add item" row, type "tata" or an HSN code → dropdown appears with ranked matches showing pack/unit. Arrow keys + Enter work. Mouse selects too. After selecting, HSN/Pack/Unit show real values; Received still shows "—"; "+ Add" button activates.

Nothing committed or deployed — please open the dashboard and confirm before I move on.

> AGENT

The root cause: the order card wraps the items table in a `div` with `overflow: "hidden"`, which clips any absolutely-positioned dropdown rendered inside the table. The fix is to render the suggestion dropdown into a portal so it lives outside the clipping ancestor.

{

  "old_string": "function ProductTypeahead({\n  products,\n  selected,\n  onSelect,\n  onClear,\n  placeholder,\n}: {\n  products: CanonicalProduct[];\n  selected: CanonicalProduct | null;\n  onSelect: (p: CanonicalProduct) => void;\n  onClear: () => void;\n  placeholder?: string;\n}) {\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState(selected?.canonical_name ?? \"\");\n  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [activeIdx, setActiveIdx] = useState(0);\n  const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n\n  // Re-sync the visible text when the parent clears or swaps the\n  // selection externally (e.g. after a successful add).\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setQuery(selected?.canonical_name ?? \"\");\n  }, [selected]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    function onDocClick(e: MouseEvent) {\n      if (!wrapRef.current) return;\n      if (!wrapRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);\n    }\n    document.addEventListener(\"mousedown\", onDocClick);\n    return () => document.removeEventListener(\"mousedown\", onDocClick);\n  }, []);",
  "new_string": "function ProductTypeahead({\n  products,\n  selected,\n  onSelect,\n  onClear,\n  placeholder,\n}: {\n  products: CanonicalProduct[];\n  selected: CanonicalProduct | null;\n  onSelect: (p: CanonicalProduct) => void;\n  onClear: () => void;\n  placeholder?: string;\n}) {\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState(selected?.canonical_name ?? \"\");\n  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\n  const [activeIdx, setActiveIdx] = useState(0);\n  const wrapRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);\n  // Portal-anchored position of the dropdown. Recomputed every time\n  // `open` flips, on window scroll, and on resize so the panel stays\n  // glued to the input even if the page scrolls (the parent table is\n  // sometimes inside a scrollable container).\n  const [anchor, setAnchor] = useState<{ left: number; top: number; width: number } | null>(null);\n\n  // Re-sync the visible text when the parent clears or swaps the\n  // selection externally (e.g. after a successful add).\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setQuery(selected?.canonical_name ?? \"\");\n  }, [selected]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    // Close on outside click — but anchor handling must treat clicks\n    // INSIDE the portal'd dropdown as inside too, so we identify the\n    // panel via a marker attribute.\n    function onDocClick(e: MouseEvent) {\n      const target = e.target as Node | null;\n      if (!target) return;\n      if (wrapRef.current?.contains(target)) return;\n      const panel = (target as HTMLElement).closest?.(\"[data-product-typeahead-panel]\");\n      if (panel) return;\n      setOpen(false);\n    }\n    document.addEventListener(\"mousedown\", onDocClick);\n    return () => document.removeEventListener(\"mousedown\", onDocClick);\n  }, []);\n\n  // Track the input's position so the portal'd panel renders right\n  // under it. Updates on open + on scroll/resize while open.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!open) return;\n    function update() {\n      const r = inputRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();\n      if (!r) return;\n      setAnchor({ left: r.left, top: r.bottom + 2, width: r.width });\n    }\n    update();\n    window.addEventListener(\"scroll\", update, true);\n    window.addEventListener(\"resize\", update);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener(\"scroll\", update, true);\n      window.removeEventListener(\"resize\", update);\n    };\n  }, [open]);"
}

> AGENT

Type-clean. Recap of the fixes:

**1. Dropdown clipped by the order card / table — fixed via portal.**
The order card wraps its items table in `overflow: hidden` (`InventorySection.tsx:1167`), which clips anything absolutely-positioned inside the table. The dropdown now renders into `document.body` via `createPortal`, anchored to the input's `getBoundingClientRect`, updated on `scroll`/`resize`. It can extend beyond the card edge and is no longer constrained by the typeahead's narrow column. `z-index: 1000` keeps it above the rest of the dashboard. Outside-click detection now treats clicks inside the portal as "inside" via a `data-product-typeahead-panel` marker so clicking an option still selects it.

**2. AddItemRow content overflowing the card — fixed by widening the actions column.**
The previous version had `<td colSpan={3}>—</td>` covering Pack/Unit/Received with a single em-dash, so the actions column only needed to fit a small `+` glyph (50 px wide). With proper Pack/Unit cells and the new labeled "+ Add" button, that 50 px clipped the button. Bumped to 90 px in the `<colgroup>` — fine for `OrderItemRow`'s small trash icon too.

**3. Catalog cancel doesn't collapse the row — fixed.**
Added `cancelEdit()` that does both `setEditing(false)` and `setOpen(false)`. Wired into the Cancel button. Save keeps the expansion open (admin just saved successfully, leaving barcodes/aliases visible is the natural next step) — only Cancel collapses. If you want Save to collapse too, say the word.

To verify in the browser:
- Open an order → click "Add an item" row → type → dropdown should drop down freely, never clipped by the card.
- Scroll the page while the dropdown is open → it should stay glued to the input.
- Click outside → dropdown closes. Click an option → selection commits.
- In Catalog, click Edit → row expands. Click Cancel → row collapses fully. Click Edit again → re-expands.

Nothing committed.
